+import type { BarFrame } from "../../../types/frames";
+import type { AggressivePrint } from "../aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";
+import { firstPrintAtOrAfter, type IndicatorTicks, type TickCoverage } from "../../chart/indicatorTicks";
+import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../killzones/killzones";
+
+/**
+ * Size-Split Delta: the pure, incremental machinery
+ * (`pm/cycle2/brief-size-split-delta.md`, rules 1 to 13 and the round-3
+ * amendments).
+ *
+ * Every bar is a window `[ts_open, ts_open + interval_ms)` over the terminal's
+ * print store. A print at or above the bar's tier threshold is "large", the
+ * rest "small"; the signed sizes of each tier are the bar's two contributions,
+ * and the two lines are their cumulative sums since the anchor. The threshold
+ * is read once, at the bar's first dispatch, from a ring of the last 4,096
+ * sizes of committed, completely covered windows (nearest-rank p98, 200 sizes
+ * minimum), or is a fixed size.
+ *
+ * Commits are keyed to the bar close and tail-only. An explicit closed
+ * dispatch rescans the window from the store; a supersession (the next bar
+ * arrives first) commits the tail's last revision values without a rescan,
+ * because the runtime cannot republish the previous bar. After commit nothing
+ * about the bar changes until a rebuild replays everything from the store.
+ *
+ * Coverage from the store is the only gate on tick-derived values; a window
+ * that is not `"all"` has no value. The tape share (print volume over candle
+ * volume) must be known and inside `[minTapeShare, MAX_TAPE_SHARE]`, else the
+ * bar is ineligible; eligibility is frozen with the flow at commit.
+ */
+
+export const SIZE_RING_CAPACITY = 4_096;
+export const TIER_MIN_SIZES = 200;
+export const TIER_QUANTILE = 0.98;
+export const MAX_TAPE_SHARE = 1.25;
+export const RECORD_RING_CAPACITY = 10_080;
+export const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
+export const WEEK_MS = 7 * DAY_MS;
+export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_K = 20;
+export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_M = 2;
+export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_Q = 2;
+export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_MIN_TAPE_SHARE = 0.5;
+export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_TIER_SIZE = 0.5;
+
+export type TierMode = "auto" | "fixed";
+export type SizeSplitAnchor = "day" | "week" | "tape";
+
+export interface SizeSplitMathSettings {
+  tierMode: TierMode;
+  tierSize: number;
+  anchor: SizeSplitAnchor;
+  K: number;
+  m: number;
+  q: number;
+  minTapeShare: number;
+}
+
+/** `"noStore"` is a ctx without a print store (the REST transport, a replay with no extras). */
+export type SizeSplitCoverage = TickCoverage | "noStore";
+
+export type SizeSplitStatus = "noFeed" | "tapeStarting" | "thinned" | "learning" | "shareUnknown" | "shareMismatch" | "shareLow" | "gap" | "forming" | "ready";
+
+export type ConfirmationState = "confirms" | "lags" | "noExtreme";
+
+export type DivergenceDirection = "bearish" | "bullish";
+
+export interface SizeSplitCondition {
+  kind: "divergence" | "conflict";
+  direction: DivergenceDirection | null;
+  tsOpen: number;
+  /** `large_t - large_h` for a divergence; the two sums for a conflict. */
+  values: readonly number[];
+}
+
+/** The tail bar's record; `sizes` is the last revision's valid sizes (newest 4,096), dropped at commit. */
+export interface SizeSplitRecord {
+  tsOpen: number;
+  windowEnd: number;
+  close: number;
+  volume: number;
+  tierAtOpen: number | null;
+  coverage: SizeSplitCoverage;
+  /** Coverage `"none"` only because the live recording began inside this window: the next bar is covered (UX finding 2). */
+  tapeStarting: boolean;
+  largeBar: number | null;
+  smallBar: number | null;
+  tapeVolume: number;
+  printCount: number;
+  share: number | null;
+  eligible: boolean;
+  /** Coverage `"all"`, a threshold and an eligible share: the bar has a value. */
+  valid: boolean;
+  sizes: number[] | null;
+  committed: boolean;
+  /** Committed by an explicit close (final styling); false while forming or when superseded. */
+  confirmed: boolean;
+  /** Frozen at commit so a later dispatch of the same bar reads the same thing. */
+  reading: SizeSplitReading | null;
+}
+
+/** What the engine keeps per committed bar: one small struct, bounded to `RECORD_RING_CAPACITY`. */
+export interface CommittedBar {
+  tsOpen: number;
+  tierAtOpen: number | null;
+  largeBar: number | null;
+  smallBar: number | null;
+  share: number | null;
+  valid: boolean;
+  confirmed: boolean;
+}
+
+export interface SizeSplitReading {
+  tsOpen: number;
+  large: number | null;
+  small: number | null;
+  largeBar: number | null;
+  smallBar: number | null;
+  share: number | null;
+  tier: number | null;
+  sizesLearned: number;
+  status: SizeSplitStatus;
+  gapBars: number;
+  /** Committed by a close or a supersession; provisional styling ends here (UX finding 5). */
+  committed: boolean;
+  confirmed: boolean;
+  valid: boolean;
+  /** Valid committed bars in the marker ring (at most `K`). */
+  ringLength: number;
+  state: ConfirmationState | null;
+  divergence: DivergenceDirection | null;
+  depth: number | null;
+  /** The divergence condition at this bar, drawn or not. */
+  divergenceHeld: DivergenceDirection | null;
+  conflictHeld: boolean;
+  conflictOnset: boolean;
+  conflictSums: readonly [number, number] | null;
+  /** The newest divergence in this anchor window, this bar's own included; the def shows it only while its toggle is on. */
+  lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null;
+  /** The newest conflict onset in this anchor window, this bar's own included. */
+  lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null;
+}
+
+export interface MarkerRingEntry {
+  close: number;
+  largeBar: number;
+  smallBar: number;
+  large: number;
+}
+
+export interface WindowScan {
+  coverage: SizeSplitCoverage;
+  largeBar: number | null;
+  smallBar: number | null;
+  tapeVolume: number;
+  printCount: number;
+  sizes: number[];
+}
+
+function isFinitePositive(value: number): boolean {
+  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0;
+}
+
+/** The tape share of a bar, null when the candle volume is missing or not positive (fail closed). */
+export function tapeShareOf(tapeVolume: number, volume: number): number | null {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(volume) || volume <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(tapeVolume) || tapeVolume < 0) return null;
+  return tapeVolume / volume;
+}
+
+export function shareEligible(share: number | null, minTapeShare: number): boolean {
+  return share !== null && Number.isFinite(share) && share >= minTapeShare && share <= MAX_TAPE_SHARE;
+}
+
+/**
+ * The window `[fromTs, toTs)` scanned once from the store: valid prints only,
+ * `size >= tier` large with the boundary print included, buys positive. With
+ * no threshold nothing is classified, but the valid sizes are still collected
+ * so they can train the ring (amendment 3). The size list is bounded to the
+ * newest `SIZE_RING_CAPACITY` (amendment 1).
+ */
+export function scanWindow(ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined, fromTs: number, toTs: number, tier: number | null): WindowScan {
+  if (ticks === undefined) return { coverage: "noStore", largeBar: null, smallBar: null, tapeVolume: 0, printCount: 0, sizes: [] };
+  const coverage = ticks.coverageOf(fromTs, toTs);
+  const prints: readonly AggressivePrint[] = ticks.prints;
+  let largeBar = 0;
+  let smallBar = 0;
+  let tapeVolume = 0;
+  let printCount = 0;
+  const sizes: number[] = [];
+  for (let index = firstPrintAtOrAfter(prints, fromTs); index < prints.length; index += 1) {
+    const print = prints[index];
+    if (print.ts >= toTs) break;
+    if (!isFinitePositive(print.size) || !isFinitePositive(print.price)) continue;
+    printCount += 1;
+    tapeVolume += print.size;
+    sizes.push(print.size);
+    if (tier === null) continue;
+    const signed = print.side === "buy" ? print.size : -print.size;
+    if (print.size >= tier) largeBar += signed;
+    else smallBar += signed;
+  }
+  if (sizes.length > SIZE_RING_CAPACITY) sizes.splice(0, sizes.length - SIZE_RING_CAPACITY);
+  return { coverage, largeBar: tier === null ? null : largeBar, smallBar: tier === null ? null : smallBar, tapeVolume, printCount, sizes };
+}
+
+/**
+ * The size ring: the last `SIZE_RING_CAPACITY` sizes in arrival order, plus the
+ * same values kept sorted so the nearest-rank quantile is one index read. Both
+ * are typed arrays; an insert or an eviction moves at most 4,096 doubles with
+ * `copyWithin`, so a commit of a hundred prints costs well under a millisecond
+ * and nothing is ever re-sorted.
+ */
+export interface SizeRing {
+  readonly count: number;
+  push(sizes: readonly number[]): void;
+  /** Nearest-rank p98 of the held sizes, null under `TIER_MIN_SIZES`. */
+  threshold(): number | null;
+  /** The held sizes in arrival order (oldest first); for tests. */
+  values(): number[];
+}
+
+export function createSizeRing(capacity = SIZE_RING_CAPACITY, minSizes = TIER_MIN_SIZES, quantile = TIER_QUANTILE): SizeRing {
+  const arrival = new Float64Array(capacity);
+  const sorted = new Float64Array(capacity);
+  let head = 0;
+  let count = 0;
+
+  function lowerBound(value: number): number {
+    let low = 0;
+    let high = count;
+    while (low < high) {
+      const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
+      if (sorted[middle] < value) low = middle + 1;
+      else high = middle;
+    }
+    return low;
+  }
+
+  function removeSorted(value: number): void {
+    const index = lowerBound(value);
+    sorted.copyWithin(index, index + 1, count);
+    count -= 1;
+  }
+
+  function insertSorted(value: number): void {
+    const index = lowerBound(value);
+    sorted.copyWithin(index + 1, index, count);
+    sorted[index] = value;
+    count += 1;
+  }
+
+  return {
+    get count() {
+      return count;
+    },
+    push(sizes) {
+      for (const size of sizes) {
+        if (!isFinitePositive(size)) continue;
+        if (count === capacity) removeSorted(arrival[head]);
+        arrival[head] = size;
+        head = (head + 1) % capacity;
+        insertSorted(size);
+      }
+    },
+    threshold() {
+      if (count < minSizes) return null;
+      const index = Math.min(count - 1, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(quantile * count) - 1));
+      return sorted[index];
+    },
+    values() {
+      const out: number[] = [];
+      const start = count === capacity ? head : 0;
+      for (let offset = 0; offset < count; offset += 1) out.push(arrival[(start + offset) % capacity]);
+      return out;
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+/** The anchor window a bar belongs to: UTC-aligned day or week (epoch-aligned, as the session kernel), or null for `tape`. */
+export function anchorStartFor(tsOpen: number, anchor: SizeSplitAnchor): number | null {
+  if (anchor === "tape") return null;
+  const period = anchor === "day" ? DAY_MS : WEEK_MS;
+  if (!Number.isFinite(tsOpen)) return null;
+  return Math.floor(tsOpen / period) * period;
+}
+
+export interface MarkerEvaluation {
+  state: ConfirmationState | null;
+  /** The divergence condition at this bar. */
+  divergenceHeld: DivergenceDirection | null;
+  /** The drawn arrow: the condition's onset, held at this bar and not held in the same direction at the previous valid bar (PM amendment to rule 11). */
+  divergence: DivergenceDirection | null;
+  depth: number | null;
+  conflictHeld: boolean;
+  conflictOnset: boolean;
+  conflictSums: readonly [number, number] | null;
+}
+
+const NO_EVALUATION: MarkerEvaluation = Object.freeze({ state: null, divergenceHeld: null, divergence: null, depth: null, conflictHeld: false, conflictOnset: false, conflictSums: null });
+
+/**
+ * Rule 11 and amendment 2 for bar `t` against a full ring of `K` valid committed
+ * bars immediately before it: `h` the ring bar with the highest close (latest
+ * if tied), `l` the lowest; a strict new high confirms when `large_t >
+ * large_h`, lags otherwise, and draws the bearish arrow when `large_t <=
+ * large_h - m · mabsL`; the mirror for a strict new low. The conflict holds
+ * when the ring-plus-`t` sums of the two tiers oppose each other and each
+ * clears `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution. Both
+ * markers fire on the onset only: the condition holds at `t` and did not hold
+ * (in that direction) at the previous valid bar, so a trend where every bar is
+ * a new extreme with a lagging large tier marks once and again only after the
+ * condition has cleared (PM amendment to rule 11).
+ */
+export function evaluateMarkers(
+  ring: readonly MarkerRingEntry[],
+  bar: { close: number; largeBar: number; smallBar: number; large: number },
+  previous: { conflict: boolean; divergence: DivergenceDirection | null },
+  settings: Pick<SizeSplitMathSettings, "K" | "m" | "q">,
+): MarkerEvaluation {
+  const K = settings.K;
+  if (ring.length < K) return NO_EVALUATION;
+  let h = 0;
+  let l = 0;
+  let sumAbsLarge = 0;
+  let sumAbsSmall = 0;
+  let sumLarge = bar.largeBar;
+  let sumSmall = bar.smallBar;
+  for (let index = 0; index < ring.length; index += 1) {
+    const entry = ring[index];
+    if (entry.close >= ring[h].close) h = index;
+    if (entry.close <= ring[l].close) l = index;
+    sumAbsLarge += Math.abs(entry.largeBar);
+    sumAbsSmall += Math.abs(entry.smallBar);
+    sumLarge += entry.largeBar;
+    sumSmall += entry.smallBar;
+  }
+  const mabsL = sumAbsLarge / ring.length;
+  const mabsS = sumAbsSmall / ring.length;
+  let state: ConfirmationState = "noExtreme";
+  let divergenceHeld: DivergenceDirection | null = null;
+  let depth: number | null = null;
+  if (bar.close > ring[h].close) {
+    state = bar.large > ring[h].large ? "confirms" : "lags";
+    if (bar.large <= ring[h].large - settings.m * mabsL) {
+      divergenceHeld = "bearish";
+      depth = bar.large - ring[h].large;
+    }
+  } else if (bar.close < ring[l].close) {
+    state = bar.large < ring[l].large ? "confirms" : "lags";
+    if (bar.large >= ring[l].large + settings.m * mabsL) {
+      divergenceHeld = "bullish";
+      depth = bar.large - ring[l].large;
+    }
+  }
+  const divergence = divergenceHeld !== null && divergenceHeld !== previous.divergence ? divergenceHeld : null;
+  const strength = settings.q * Math.sqrt(K + 1);
+  const conflictHeld = Math.sign(sumLarge) * Math.sign(sumSmall) < 0 && Math.abs(sumLarge) >= strength * mabsL && Math.abs(sumSmall) >= strength * mabsS;
+  return { state, divergenceHeld, divergence, depth: divergence === null ? null : depth, conflictHeld, conflictOnset: conflictHeld && !previous.conflict, conflictSums: [sumLarge, sumSmall] };
+}
+
+/** Status precedence, first match wins (rule 12, amendment 5). */
+export function statusOf(record: Pick<SizeSplitRecord, "coverage" | "tapeStarting" | "tierAtOpen" | "share" | "eligible" | "committed">, gapBars: number, validSinceGap: number, K: number): SizeSplitStatus {
+  if (record.coverage === "none" && record.tapeStarting) return "tapeStarting";
+  if (record.coverage === "noStore" || record.coverage === "none") return "noFeed";
+  if (record.coverage === "thin") return "thinned";
+  if (record.tierAtOpen === null) return "learning";
+  if (record.share === null) return "shareUnknown";
+  if (record.share > MAX_TAPE_SHARE) return "shareMismatch";
+  if (!record.eligible) return "shareLow";
+  if (gapBars > 0 && validSinceGap < K) return "gap";
+  if (!record.committed) return "forming";
+  return "ready";
+}
+
+export interface SizeSplitEngine {
+  /** Feed one dispatch (forming revision, close, or the next bar) and read the current bar. */
+  onBar(bar: BarFrame, ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined): SizeSplitReading;
+  readonly tail: SizeSplitRecord | null;
+  readonly sizeRing: SizeRing;
+  readonly markerRing: readonly MarkerRingEntry[];
+  readonly base: { readonly large: number; readonly small: number };
+  readonly anchorStart: number | null;
+  /** Committed bars, oldest first, at most `RECORD_RING_CAPACITY`. */
+  readonly committed: readonly CommittedBar[];
+  readonly lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null;
+  readonly lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null;
+}
+
+export function createSizeSplitEngine(settings: SizeSplitMathSettings): SizeSplitEngine {
+  const sizeRing = createSizeRing();
+  const committed: CommittedBar[] = [];
+  const markerRing: MarkerRingEntry[] = [];
+  const base = { large: 0, small: 0 };
+  let tail: SizeSplitRecord | null = null;
+  let anchorStart: number | null = null;
+  let anchorSeen = false;
+  let previousConflict = false;
+  let previousDivergence: DivergenceDirection | null = null;
+  let lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
+  let lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
+  /** Whether a valid bar has committed in this anchor window (a gap needs a valid bar before it). */
+  let validEver = false;
+  /** Invalid committed bars in the most recent gap, and valid bars committed since it closed. */
+  let gapBars = 0;
+  let validSinceGap = 0;
+  let lastReading: SizeSplitReading | null = null;
+
+  function tierFor(): number | null {
+    if (settings.tierMode === "fixed") return isFinitePositive(settings.tierSize) ? settings.tierSize : null;
+    return sizeRing.threshold();
+  }
+
+  function divergenceOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
+    return evaluation.divergence === null ? null : { kind: "divergence", direction: evaluation.divergence, tsOpen, values: [evaluation.depth ?? 0] };
+  }
+
+  function conflictOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
+    return evaluation.conflictOnset && evaluation.conflictSums !== null ? { kind: "conflict", direction: null, tsOpen, values: evaluation.conflictSums } : null;
+  }
+
+  function readingOf(record: SizeSplitRecord): SizeSplitReading {
+    const large = record.valid ? base.large + (record.largeBar as number) : null;
+    const small = record.valid ? base.small + (record.smallBar as number) : null;
+    const evaluation = record.valid
+      ? evaluateMarkers(markerRing, { close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: large as number }, { conflict: previousConflict, divergence: previousDivergence }, settings)
+      : NO_EVALUATION;
+    const ownDivergence = record.valid ? divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
+    const ownConflict = record.valid ? conflictOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
+    return {
+      tsOpen: record.tsOpen,
+      large,
+      small,
+      largeBar: record.valid ? record.largeBar : null,
+      smallBar: record.valid ? record.smallBar : null,
+      share: record.share,
+      tier: record.tierAtOpen,
+      sizesLearned: sizeRing.count,
+      status: statusOf(record, gapBars, validSinceGap, settings.K),
+      gapBars,
+      committed: record.committed,
+      confirmed: record.confirmed,
+      valid: record.valid,
+      ringLength: markerRing.length,
+      state: evaluation.state,
+      divergence: evaluation.divergence,
+      divergenceHeld: evaluation.divergenceHeld,
+      depth: evaluation.depth,
+      conflictHeld: evaluation.conflictHeld,
+      conflictOnset: evaluation.conflictOnset,
+      conflictSums: evaluation.conflictSums,
+      lastDivergence: ownDivergence ?? lastDivergence,
+      lastConflict: ownConflict ?? lastConflict,
+    };
+  }
+
+  function fill(record: SizeSplitRecord, bar: BarFrame, ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined): void {
+    const scan = scanWindow(ticks, record.tsOpen, record.windowEnd, record.tierAtOpen);
+    record.close = bar.close;
+    record.volume = bar.volume;
+    record.coverage = scan.coverage;
+    record.tapeStarting = scan.coverage === "none" && ticks !== undefined && ticks.prints.length > 0 && ticks.liveSince !== null && ticks.liveSince < record.windowEnd;
+    record.largeBar = scan.largeBar;
+    record.smallBar = scan.smallBar;
+    record.tapeVolume = scan.tapeVolume;
+    record.printCount = scan.printCount;
+    record.sizes = scan.sizes;
+    record.share = scan.coverage === "all" ? tapeShareOf(scan.tapeVolume, bar.volume) : null;
+    record.eligible = shareEligible(record.share, settings.minTapeShare);
+    record.valid = scan.coverage === "all" && record.tierAtOpen !== null && scan.largeBar !== null && scan.smallBar !== null && record.eligible;
+  }
+
+  /** Steps 2 to 4 of rule 6: freeze flow and eligibility, train the ring, advance the cumulatives (or record the gap). */
+  function commit(record: SizeSplitRecord, confirmed: boolean): void {
+    record.confirmed = confirmed;
+    record.committed = true;
+    const reading = readingOf(record);
+    record.reading = reading;
+    if (record.coverage === "all" && record.sizes !== null) sizeRing.push(record.sizes);
+    if (record.valid) {
+      base.large += record.largeBar as number;
+      base.small += record.smallBar as number;
+      markerRing.push({ close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: base.large });
+      if (markerRing.length > settings.K) markerRing.splice(0, markerRing.length - settings.K);
+      previousConflict = reading.conflictHeld;
+      previousDivergence = reading.divergenceHeld;
+      const ownDivergence = divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
+      const ownConflict = conflictOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
+      if (ownDivergence !== null) lastDivergence = ownDivergence;
+      if (ownConflict !== null) lastConflict = ownConflict;
+      validEver = true;
+      validSinceGap += 1;
+    } else {
+      markerRing.length = 0;
+      previousConflict = false;
+      previousDivergence = null;
+      if (validEver) {
+        gapBars = validSinceGap > 0 ? 1 : gapBars + 1;
+        validSinceGap = 0;
+      }
+    }
+    record.sizes = null;
+    committed.push({ tsOpen: record.tsOpen, tierAtOpen: record.tierAtOpen, largeBar: record.largeBar, smallBar: record.smallBar, share: record.share, valid: record.valid, confirmed });
+    if (committed.length > RECORD_RING_CAPACITY) committed.splice(0, committed.length - RECORD_RING_CAPACITY);
+  }
+
+  /** Step 5 of rule 6: the new record, with the anchor reset applied first when the bar opens a new window (rule 8). */
+  function open(bar: BarFrame): SizeSplitRecord {
+    const start = anchorStartFor(bar.ts_open, settings.anchor);
+    if (!anchorSeen || start !== anchorStart) {
+      if (anchorSeen) {
+        base.large = 0;
+        base.small = 0;
+        markerRing.length = 0;
+        previousConflict = false;
+        previousDivergence = null;
+        lastDivergence = null;
+        lastConflict = null;
+        validEver = false;
+        gapBars = 0;
+        validSinceGap = 0;
+      }
+      anchorStart = start;
+      anchorSeen = true;
+    }
+    return {
+      tsOpen: bar.ts_open,
+      windowEnd: bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms,
+      close: bar.close,
+      volume: bar.volume,
+      tierAtOpen: tierFor(),
+      coverage: "noStore",
+      tapeStarting: false,
+      largeBar: null,
+      smallBar: null,
+      tapeVolume: 0,
+      printCount: 0,
+      share: null,
+      eligible: false,
+      valid: false,
+      sizes: null,
+      committed: false,
+      confirmed: false,
+      reading: null,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    onBar(bar, ticks) {
+      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open < tail.tsOpen) return lastReading ?? readingOf(tail);
+      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open > tail.tsOpen) {
+        if (!tail.committed) commit(tail, false);
+        tail = null;
+      }
+      if (tail === null) tail = open(bar);
+      if (tail.committed) {
+        lastReading = tail.reading ?? readingOf(tail);
+        return lastReading;
+      }
+      fill(tail, bar, ticks);
+      if (bar.closed) {
+        commit(tail, true);
+        lastReading = tail.reading as SizeSplitReading;
+        return lastReading;
+      }
+      lastReading = readingOf(tail);
+      return lastReading;
+    },
+    get tail() {
+      return tail;
+    },
+    sizeRing,
+    markerRing,
+    base,
+    get anchorStart() {
+      return anchorStart;
+    },
+    committed,
+    get lastDivergence() {
+      return lastDivergence;
+    },
+    get lastConflict() {
+      return lastConflict;
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+/* Formatting shared by the table and the doc examples (amendment 7). */
+
+const MINUS = "−";
+
+/** Three significant digits with `k`/`M` suffixes and an explicit sign; zero prints as `0`. */
+export function formatSigned(value: number): string {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "—";
+  if (value === 0) return "0";
+  const sign = value < 0 ? MINUS : "+";
+  return `${sign}${formatMagnitude(Math.abs(value))}`;
+}
+
+export function formatMagnitude(value: number): string {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return "—";
+  if (value >= 1e6) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e6)}M`;
+  if (value >= 1e3) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e3)}k`;
+  return trimDigits(value);
+}
+
+function trimDigits(value: number): string {
+  if (value === 0) return "0";
+  const text = value.toPrecision(3);
+  if (text.includes("e")) return Number(text).toString();
+  return text.includes(".") ? text.replace(/\.?0+$/, "") : text;
+}
+
+/** `HH:MM` in the instrument's clock: New York for dxFeed and Databento, UTC for Binance. */
+export function formatClock(tsOpen: number, provider: BarFrame["provider"]): string {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(tsOpen)) return "—";
+  const minuteOfDay = provider === "DXFEED" || provider === "DATABENTO" ? nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tsOpen) : Math.floor((tsOpen % DAY_MS) / 60_000);
+  const hours = Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60);
+  const minutes = minuteOfDay % 60;
+  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, "0")}`;
+}

+import type { BarFrame, TradeFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import type { MarkerCall } from "../../../chart/draw/markers";
+import type { IndicatorTicks } from "../../../chart/indicatorTicks";
+import type { RuntimeIndicatorDef } from "../../../chart/indicatorTypes";
+import { FIXTURE_T0, tapeOf, ticksOf, type SizeSplitFixture } from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { barDelta } from "../../barSides";
+import { evaluateMarkers, type MarkerRingEntry } from "../sizeSplitDeltaMath";
+
+/** The store a fixture's prints make, complete from the first bar (`liveSince = FIXTURE_T0`). */
+export function fixtureTicks(fixture: SizeSplitFixture, liveSince = FIXTURE_T0): IndicatorTicks {
+  return ticksOf(tapeOf(fixture.prints, liveSince));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Synthetic bars whose volume equals their prints' total, so the tape share
+ * reads 1 and the share gate passes: the protocol's `busyTapeFor` draws
+ * prints independently of the bar volume it was given.
+ */
+export function withTapeVolume(bars: readonly BarFrame[], printsByBar: ReadonlyMap<number, readonly TradeFrame[]>): BarFrame[] {
+  return bars.map((bar) => {
+    const prints = printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? [];
+    let volume = 0;
+    let buy = 0;
+    for (const print of prints) {
+      volume += print.size;
+      if (print.side === "buy") buy += print.size;
+    }
+    return { ...bar, volume, buy_volume: buy, sell_volume: volume - buy, trade_count: prints.length } as BarFrame;
+  });
+}
+
+/** Bar indices of the markers of one shape, in time order. */
+export function markerIndices(markers: readonly MarkerCall[], bars: readonly BarFrame[], shape: MarkerCall["shape"]): number[] {
+  return markers
+    .filter((call) => call.shape === shape)
+    .map((call) => bars.findIndex((bar) => bar.ts_open === call.time))
+    .sort((a, b) => a - b);
+}
+
+export interface CvdBaselineSettings {
+  K: number;
+  m: number;
+}
+
+interface CvdBaselineState {
+  ring: MarkerRingEntry[];
+  base: number;
+  tail: { tsOpen: number; committed: boolean; delta: number | null; close: number } | null;
+  value: number | null;
+  divergence: "bearish" | "bullish" | null;
+  /** The divergence condition at the last committed bar, for the onset rule. */
+  previousDivergence: "bearish" | "bullish" | null;
+  heldNow: "bearish" | "bullish" | null;
+}
+
+function commitCvdTail(state: CvdBaselineState, K: number): void {
+  const tail = state.tail;
+  if (tail === null || tail.committed) return;
+  tail.committed = true;
+  if (tail.delta === null) {
+    state.ring.length = 0;
+    state.previousDivergence = null;
+    return;
+  }
+  state.previousDivergence = state.heldNow;
+  state.base += tail.delta;
+  state.ring.push({ close: tail.close, largeBar: tail.delta, smallBar: 0, large: state.base });
+  if (state.ring.length > K) state.ring.splice(0, state.ring.length - K);
+}
+
+/**
+ * The "what candles alone show" baseline: the `orderFlow` cumulative delta from
+ * `barSides`, with the indicator's divergence rule applied to it (the whole
+ * candle delta plays the large tier, the small tier is zero). Tail-only
+ * commits at the close or on supersession, like the indicator. Test-only.
+ */
+export const cvdDivergenceBaseline: RuntimeIndicatorDef<CvdBaselineState, CvdBaselineSettings> = {
+  kind: "runtime",
+  id: "cvdDivergenceBaselineTest",
+  label: "CVD divergence baseline (test)",
+  category: "l1",
+  defaultSettings: { K: 20, m: 2 },
+  lines: [{ key: "cvd" }],
+  placement: { kind: "ownPane" },
+  createState: () => ({ ring: [], base: 0, tail: null, value: null, divergence: null, previousDivergence: null, heldNow: null }),
+  onBar: (ctx) => {
+    const { bar, state, settings } = ctx;
+    if (state.tail !== null && bar.ts_open < state.tail.tsOpen) return;
+    if (state.tail !== null && bar.ts_open > state.tail.tsOpen) {
+      commitCvdTail(state, settings.K);
+      state.tail = null;
+    }
+    if (state.tail === null) state.tail = { tsOpen: bar.ts_open, committed: false, delta: null, close: bar.close };
+    if (!state.tail.committed) {
+      state.tail.delta = barDelta(bar);
+      state.tail.close = bar.close;
+    }
+    const delta = state.tail.delta;
+    if (delta === null) {
+      state.value = null;
+      state.divergence = null;
+      state.heldNow = null;
+    } else {
+      const large = state.base + delta;
+      const evaluation = evaluateMarkers(state.ring, { close: bar.close, largeBar: delta, smallBar: 0, large }, { conflict: false, divergence: state.previousDivergence }, { K: settings.K, m: settings.m, q: 2 });
+      state.value = large;
+      state.divergence = evaluation.divergence;
+      state.heldNow = evaluation.divergenceHeld;
+      if (evaluation.divergence !== null) {
+        ctx.markers.plotshape({
+          shape: evaluation.divergence === "bearish" ? "arrowDown" : "arrowUp",
+          position: evaluation.divergence === "bearish" ? "aboveBar" : "belowBar",
+          color: "#787b86",
+          size: 1,
+        });
+      }
+    }
+    if (bar.closed && !state.tail.committed) commitCvdTail(state, settings.K);
+  },
+  readOutput: (state) => ({ cvd: state.value }),
+};

+    expect(sizeUnitOf({ provider: "DXFEED", symbol: "AAPL", assetClass: "EQUITY" })).toBe("shares");
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- table copy (brief Default view, rule 12, amendments 5 and 7, UX notes 1 to 3, 6, 8, 9)", () => {
+  it("row (a) is the confirmation state with compact values, falls back to a subject word when a row would exceed 30 characters, and names the warm-up of the ring", () => {
+    expect(stateText(reading({}), 20)).toBe(`L +12.4 · S ${MINUS}8.1 · confirms`);
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: "lags" }), 20)).toBe(`L +12.4 · S ${MINUS}8.1 · lags`);
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: "noExtreme" }), 20)).toBe(`L +12.4 · S ${MINUS}8.1 · no extreme`);
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: "noExtreme" }), 20).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS);
+    // Three-significant-digit values make the widest "confirms" row exactly 30 characters, so only "no extreme" can overflow;
+    // the "Large confirms" / "Large lags" fallbacks exist for the day a value grows past the formatter's six characters.
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: "noExtreme", large: 12_300, small: -45_600 }), 20)).toBe("No extreme");
+    const widest = stateText(reading({ state: "confirms", large: 1_230_000, small: -9_880_000 }), 20);
+    expect(widest).toBe(`L +1.23M · S ${MINUS}9.88M · confirms`);
+    expect(widest.length).toBe(SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS);
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: "lags", large: 1_230_000, small: -9_880_000 }), 20)).toBe(`L +1.23M · S ${MINUS}9.88M · lags`);
+    expect(stateText(reading({ state: null, ringLength: 7 }), 20)).toBe("Need 20 valid bars");
+    expect(stateText(reading({ valid: false, large: null, small: null }), 20)).toBe(DASH);
+    expect(stateText(null, 20)).toBe(DASH);
+  });
+
+  it("row (b) is the newest visible condition with its time and value, divergence winning on the same bar, a dash when none", () => {
+    const divergence = { kind: "divergence" as const, direction: "bearish" as const, tsOpen: FIXTURE_T0, values: [-8_120] };
+    const conflict = { kind: "conflict" as const, direction: null, tsOpen: FIXTURE_T0 + MINUTE_MS, values: [4.2, -3.1] };
+    expect(conditionText(null, "BINANCE")).toBe(DASH);
+    expect(conditionText(divergence, "BINANCE")).toBe(`Divergence ↓ 20:26 · ${MINUS}8.12k`);
+    expect(conditionText({ ...divergence, direction: "bullish", values: [6] }, "BINANCE")).toBe("Divergence ↑ 20:26 · +6");
+    expect(conditionText(conflict, "BINANCE")).toBe(`Conflict 20:27 · +4.2/${MINUS}3.1`);
+    // Three significant digits keep the longest conflict row at exactly 30 characters.
+    const widest = conditionText({ ...conflict, values: [1_230_000, -9_880_000] }, "BINANCE");
+    expect(widest).toBe(`Conflict 20:27 · +1.23M/${MINUS}9.88M`);
+    expect(widest.length).toBe(SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS);
+    const both = reading({ lastDivergence: divergence, lastConflict: conflict });
+    expect(visibleCondition(both, SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS)).toBe(divergence);
+    expect(visibleCondition(both, { showDivergence: true, showConflict: true })).toBe(conflict);
+    expect(visibleCondition(both, { showDivergence: false, showConflict: true })).toBe(conflict);
+    expect(visibleCondition(both, { showDivergence: false, showConflict: false })).toBeNull();
+    const sameBar = reading({ lastDivergence: divergence, lastConflict: { ...conflict, tsOpen: FIXTURE_T0 } });
+    expect(visibleCondition(sameBar, { showDivergence: true, showConflict: true })).toBe(divergence);
+  });
+
+  it("row (c) status strings follow the precedence and stay within 30 characters", () => {
+    const strings = [
+      statusText(reading({ status: "noFeed" }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "tapeStarting" }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "thinned" }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "learning", sizesLearned: 120 }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "shareUnknown", share: null }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "shareMismatch", share: 1.6 }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "shareLow", share: 0.38 }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "gap", gapBars: 3 }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "gap", gapBars: 1 }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ status: "forming", committed: false, confirmed: false }), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({}), "BTC", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ tier: 20, share: 0.999 }), "contracts", 0.5),
+      statusText(reading({ tier: 2_000 }), "shares", 0.5),
+    ];
+    expect(strings).toEqual([
+      "Needs live tape · no feed",
+      "Tape live · next bar",
+      "Needs live tape · thinned",
+      "Learning sizes · 120 of 200",
+      "Tape share unknown",
+      "Tape 160% · mismatch",
+      "Tape 38% · below 50%",
+      "Tape gap · 3 bars",
+      "Tape gap · 1 bar",
+      "Forming",
+      "≥ 0.106 BTC · tape 100%",
+      "≥ 20 contracts · tape 100%",
+      "≥ 2k shares · tape 100%",
+    ]);
+    for (const text of strings) expect(text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS);
+    const rows = sizeSplitTableRows(reading({ committed: false, confirmed: false, status: "forming" }), SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, BINANCE_STATE);
+    expect(rows.map((row) => row.text)).toEqual([`L +12.4 · S ${MINUS}8.1 · confirms`, DASH, "Forming"]);
+    // Row (a) is always the current reading, so it is never dimmed (UX finding 1).
+    expect(rows[0].color).toBe(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- markers, colours and outputs through a hand-built ctx", () => {
+  const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 1, anchor: "tape", K: 1, showBarLines: true };
+  const A = FIXTURE_T0;
+  const B = A + MINUTE_MS;
+  const prints = [
+    tradeFrame(A + 1_000, 100, 2, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "a0"),
+    tradeFrame(A + 2_000, 100, 0.5, "sell", BINANCE_PERP, "a1"),
+    tradeFrame(B + 1_000, 101, 5, "sell", BINANCE_PERP, "b0"),
+    tradeFrame(B + 2_000, 101, 0.4, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "b1"),
+  ];
+  const bar = (tsOpen: number, close: number, volume: number, closed: boolean): BarFrame =>
+    ({ type: "bar", ...BINANCE_PERP, interval_ms: MINUTE_MS, ts_open: tsOpen, open: close, high: close, low: close, close, volume, trade_count: null, closed }) as BarFrame;
+
+  it("draws the bearish arrow (size 2) above the bar in the large line's colour, provisional while forming and final once committed, and blanks hidden lines in readOutput", () => {
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, A));
+    const state = openState(settings);
+    const capture = captureMarkers(() => B);
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, bar(A, 100, 2.5, true), settings, capture.markers, ticks));
+    expect(capture.calls).toEqual([]);
+    expect(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.readOutput(state)).toEqual({ large: 2, small: -0.5, largeBar: 2, smallBar: -0.5 });
+    expect(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.lines[0].color!(state, settings)).toBe(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR);
+    capture.reset();
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, bar(B, 101, 5.4, false), settings, capture.markers, ticks));
+    expect(capture.calls).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(capture.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ shape: "arrowDown", position: "aboveBar", color: withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR, 0.5), size: 2, time: B });
+    expect(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.lines[1].color!(state, settings)).toBe(withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_SMALL_COLOR, 0.5));
+    expect(pendingTexts()).toEqual([`L ${MINUS}3 · S ${MINUS}0.1 · lags`, `Divergence ↓ 20:27 · ${MINUS}5`, "Forming"]);
+    expect(pendingGrid()["0,0"].textColor).toBe(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR);
+    capture.reset();
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(state, bar(B, 101, 5.4, true), settings, capture.markers, ticks));
+    expect(capture.calls[0]).toMatchObject({ shape: "arrowDown", color: SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR, size: 2 });
+    expect(pendingTexts()[2]).toBe("≥ 1 BTC · tape 100%");
+    // A bar committed by supersession is no longer provisional (UX finding 5).
+    const superseded = openState(settings);
+    const later = captureMarkers(() => B);
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(superseded, bar(A, 100, 2.5, false), settings, later.markers, ticks));
+    expect(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.lines[0].color!(superseded, settings)).toBe(withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR, 0.5));
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(superseded, bar(B, 101, 5.4, false), settings, later.markers, ticks));
+    expect(superseded.engine.committed[0].confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(superseded.engine.committed[0].valid).toBe(true);
+    const hidden = openState({ ...settings, showSmall: false, showBarLines: false });
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(hidden, bar(A, 100, 2.5, true), { ...settings, showSmall: false, showBarLines: false }, captureMarkers(() => A).markers, ticks));
+    expect(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.readOutput(hidden)).toEqual({ large: 2, small: null, largeBar: null, smallBar: null });
+    const noArrows = openState({ ...settings, showDivergence: false });
+    const quiet = captureMarkers(() => B);
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(noArrows, bar(A, 100, 2.5, true), { ...settings, showDivergence: false }, quiet.markers, ticks));
+    sizeSplitDeltaIndicator.onBar(ctxOf(noArrows, bar(B, 101, 5.4, true), { ...settings, showDivergence: false }, quiet.markers, ticks));
+    expect(quiet.calls).toEqual([]);
+    // With the arrows hidden, row (b) no longer names the divergence (UX finding 3).
+    expect(pendingTexts()[1]).toBe(DASH);
+  });
+});

+/**
+ * Protocol checks 5 and 7 and the brief's Acceptance, on the frozen fixtures:
+ * the indicator in `auto` mode against the same indicator in `fixed` mode (the
+ * one controlled comparison, identical detector) and against the `orderFlow`
+ * CVD from `barSides` with the same divergence rule ("what candles alone
+ * show"). Truth comes from the generators' hidden state; every arrow the
+ * detector draws is also checked against the independent reference
+ * implementation of the rule (amendment 8). Numbers are pinned here and
+ * quoted in the doc page.
+ */
+
+const AUTO: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, anchor: "tape", showConflict: true, showBarLines: true };
+const FIXED: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 0.5 };
+const RULE = { K: 20, m: 2, q: 2 };
+const MECHANISM = sizeSplitFixture();
+
+interface Run {
+  bearish: number[];
+  bullish: number[];
+  circles: number[];
+  mask: Set<number>;
+  outputs: Readonly<Record<string, readonly (number | null)[]>>;
+  reference: ReturnType<typeof referenceMarkers>;
+}
+
+function runIndicator(fixture: SizeSplitFixture, settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings): Run {
+  const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, fixture.bars, settings, { ticks: fixtureTicks(fixture) });
+  expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+  const reference = referenceMarkers(
+    fixture.bars.map((bar, index) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: capture.outputs.largeBar[index], smallBar: capture.outputs.smallBar[index] })),
+    RULE,
+  );
+  return {
+    bearish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowDown"),
+    bullish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowUp"),
+    circles: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "circle"),
+    mask: new Set(reference.evaluated),
+    outputs: capture.outputs,
+    reference,
+  };
+}
+
+function runCvd(fixture: SizeSplitFixture): { bearish: number[]; bullish: number[]; reference: ReturnType<typeof referenceMarkers> } {
+  const capture = replayCapture(cvdDivergenceBaseline, fixture.bars, { K: RULE.K, m: RULE.m });
+  const reference = referenceMarkers(fixture.bars.map((bar) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: barSides(bar).delta, smallBar: 0 })), RULE);
+  return { bearish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowDown"), bullish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowUp"), reference };
+}
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- mechanism fixture (frozen, seed 20261102)", () => {
+  const auto = runIndicator(MECHANISM, AUTO);
+  const fixed = runIndicator(MECHANISM, FIXED);
+  const cvd = runCvd(MECHANISM);
+
+  it("the fixture is the frozen one: 300 bars, 100 prints each, 8 planted divergences at 45 + 30 j, exact tape volume", () => {
+    expect(MECHANISM.bars).toHaveLength(300);
+    expect(MECHANISM.prints).toHaveLength(30_000);
+    expect(MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices).toEqual([45, 75, 105, 135, 165, 195, 225, 255]);
+    expect(MECHANISM.labels.direction).toEqual([-1, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1]);
+    for (let index = 0; index < MECHANISM.bars.length; index += 1) {
+      const prints = MECHANISM.printsByBar[index];
+      expect(prints).toHaveLength(100);
+      for (let k = 1; k < prints.length; k += 1) expect(prints[k].ts_exchange).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(prints[k - 1].ts_exchange);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].close).toBe(prints[prints.length - 1].price);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].open).toBe(prints[0].price);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].volume).toBe(prints.reduce((sum, print) => sum + print.size, 0));
+    }
+    for (const [position, p] of MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices.entries()) {
+      const d = MECHANISM.labels.direction[position];
+      const window = MECHANISM.bars.slice(p - 20, p).map((bar) => bar.close);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[p].close).toBe(d > 0 ? Math.max(...window) + 30 : Math.min(...window) - 30);
+      for (let back = 0; back <= 2; back += 1) for (const print of MECHANISM.printsByBar[p - back].filter((print) => print.size === 1)) expect(print.side).toBe(d > 0 ? "sell" : "buy");
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the auto tier equals the nearest-rank p98 of the ring the fixture actually produces on every bar after the warm-up, and the contributions equal the reference classification with the boundary print large (amendment 6)", () => {
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "auto", tierSize: 0.5, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+    const ticks = fixtureTicks(MECHANISM);
+    const ring: number[] = [];
+    let firstTier = -1;
+    MECHANISM.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar, ticks);
+      const expectedTier = ring.length < TIER_MIN_SIZES ? null : referenceNearestRank(ring.slice(-SIZE_RING_CAPACITY));
+      expect(reading.tier).toBe(expectedTier);
+      if (expectedTier !== null && firstTier < 0) firstTier = index;
+      const expected = referenceClassify(MECHANISM.printsByBar[index], bar.ts_open, bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms, expectedTier);
+      expect(reading.largeBar).toBe(expected.largeBar);
+      expect(reading.smallBar).toBe(expected.smallBar);
+      expect(auto.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBe(expected.largeBar);
+      for (const print of MECHANISM.printsByBar[index]) ring.push(print.size);
+    });
+    expect(firstTier).toBe(2);
+    // With fewer than 4,096 sizes the p98 lands on the largest small print, which then classifies large; with a full ring it is 1.0.
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[2]).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.committed[41].tierAtOpen).toBe(1);
+    expect(engine.committed[2].tierAtOpen).toBeLessThan(0.05);
+  });
+
+  it("pins the arrows: auto and fixed hit all 8 planted bars exactly with no off-label arrow; every arrow equals the reference rule's; the planted bars are the condition's onsets", () => {
+    expect(auto.reference.bearishHeld).toEqual(auto.reference.bearish);
+    expect(auto.reference.bullishHeld).toEqual(auto.reference.bullish);
+    expect(auto.bearish).toEqual([75, 135, 195, 255]);
+    expect(auto.bullish).toEqual([45, 105, 165, 225]);
+    expect(auto.reference.bearish).toEqual(auto.bearish);
+    expect(auto.reference.bullish).toEqual(auto.bullish);
+    expect(fixed.bearish).toEqual(auto.bearish);
+    expect(fixed.bullish).toEqual(auto.bullish);
+    expect(referenceScore(auto, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask)).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 0, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+    expect(referenceScore(fixed, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, fixed.mask)).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 0, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+  });
+
+  it("pins the conflict onsets to the count the reference implementation produces (17), on the same bars", () => {
+    expect(auto.reference.conflictOnsets).toEqual([22, 50, 54, 56, 59, 78, 109, 111, 114, 117, 139, 175, 200, 232, 236, 257, 285]);
+    expect(auto.circles).toEqual(auto.reference.conflictOnsets);
+    expect(auto.circles).toHaveLength(17);
+  });
+
+  it("measured on the CVD baseline (amendment 9): the candle delta also hits all 8 planted bars, with 5 off-label arrows the split does not draw; the CVD def equals the reference rule", () => {
+    expect(cvd.bearish).toEqual(cvd.reference.bearish);
+    expect(cvd.bullish).toEqual(cvd.reference.bullish);
+    expect(cvd.bearish).toEqual([75, 135, 190, 195, 255]);
+    expect(cvd.bullish).toEqual([42, 45, 56, 105, 165, 222, 225, 288]);
+    const score = referenceScore(cvd, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask);
+    expect(score).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 5, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+    // Acceptance: recall >= 7/8 on bar p exactly; false divergences <= 2 and <= the CVD baseline's.
+    expect(referenceScore(auto, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask).offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Math.min(2, score.offLabel));
+  });
+
+  it("marker budget at default settings (conflict circles off, day anchor) is within 40 per 1,000 on the mechanism fixture", () => {
+    const budget = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, MECHANISM.bars, SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(MECHANISM) } });
+    const verdict = judgeBudget(budget, 40, 2);
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta mechanism fixture, defaults", budget, verdict));
+    // Seven, not eight: the default day anchor resets at the UTC midnight between bars 213 and 214, and planted bar 225 has no full ring yet.
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(7);
+    expect(verdict.ok).toBe(true);
+    const withConflict = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, MECHANISM.bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showConflict: true }, { extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(MECHANISM) } });
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta mechanism fixture, conflict circles on", withConflict, judgeBudget(withConflict, 40, 2)));
+    expect(withConflict.total).toBe(budget.total + (withConflict.byShape.circle ?? 0));
+    expect(withConflict.byShape.circle ?? 0).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(withConflict.maxPerBar).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
+  });
+});
+
+interface RegimeTotals {
+  hits: number;
+  offLabel: number;
+  eligible: number;
+  planted: number;
+}
+
+function regimeTable(priceFollows: SizeSplitPriceFollows): { auto: RegimeTotals; fixed: RegimeTotals; cvd: RegimeTotals; arrowsEqualReference: boolean } {
+  const totals = { auto: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 }, fixed: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 }, cvd: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 } };
+  let arrowsEqualReference = true;
+  for (const seed of SIZE_SPLIT_REGIME_SEEDS) {
+    const fixture = sizeSplitRegimeBars({ seed, priceFollows });
+    const auto = runIndicator(fixture, AUTO);
+    const fixed = runIndicator(fixture, FIXED);
+    const cvd = runCvd(fixture);
+    // The common evaluation mask: bars where both configurations have values and a full ring (amendment 8).
+    const mask = new Set([...auto.mask].filter((index) => fixed.mask.has(index)));
+    for (const [name, run] of [["auto", auto], ["fixed", fixed], ["cvd", cvd]] as const) {
+      const score = referenceScore(run, fixture.labels.plantedIndices, fixture.labels.direction, mask);
+      totals[name].hits += score.hits;
+      totals[name].offLabel += score.offLabel;
+      totals[name].eligible += mask.size;
+      totals[name].planted += score.plantedEvaluable;
+      if (JSON.stringify(run.bearish) !== JSON.stringify(run.reference.bearish) || JSON.stringify(run.bullish) !== JSON.stringify(run.reference.bullish)) arrowsEqualReference = false;
+    }
+  }
+  return { ...totals, arrowsEqualReference };
+}
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- regime fixtures, 10 seeds each (Acceptance, round 3 rulings 7 and 8)", () => {
+  const large = regimeTable("large");
+  const small = regimeTable("small");
+  const none = regimeTable("none");
+
+  it("prints the acceptance table", () => {
+    const row = (regime: string, name: string, totals: RegimeTotals) => `| ${regime} | ${name} | ${totals.eligible} | ${totals.planted} | ${totals.hits} | ${totals.offLabel} |`;
+    console.log(
+      [
+        "| regime | configuration | eligible bars | planted | hits | off-label arrows |",
+        "|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
+        row("large", "auto", large.auto),
+        row("large", "fixed 0.5", large.fixed),
+        row("large", "CVD", large.cvd),
+        row("small", "auto", small.auto),
+        row("small", "fixed 0.5", small.fixed),
+        row("small", "CVD", small.cvd),
+        row("none", "auto", none.auto),
+        row("none", "fixed 0.5", none.fixed),
+        row("none", "CVD", none.cvd),
+      ].join("\n"),
+    );
+    expect(large.arrowsEqualReference && small.arrowsEqualReference && none.arrowsEqualReference).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("pins the published counts over 10 seeds", () => {
+    expect(large.auto).toEqual({ hits: 100, offLabel: 0, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    expect(large.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 100, offLabel: 0, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    expect(large.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 80, offLabel: 171, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    // Onset-only arrows (PM amendment to rule 11): the held condition would count 703/709/37 on small and 290/293/186 on none.
+    expect(small.auto).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 488, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(small.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 490, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(small.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 37, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.auto).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 256, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 256, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 180, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+  });
+
+  it("acceptance: auto beats or matches fixed (no more false markers on small and none at equal or better recall on large); the split beats the candle delta on its own regime", () => {
+    expect(large.auto.hits).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(large.fixed.hits);
+    expect(small.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(small.fixed.offLabel);
+    expect(none.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(none.fixed.offLabel);
+    expect(large.auto.hits).toBeGreaterThan(large.cvd.hits);
+    expect(large.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThan(large.cvd.offLabel);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- whale versus crowd (protocol check 7) in fixed mode, tier 1.0 (round 3 ruling 9)", () => {
+  it("responds differently to the two tapes that make the same candle while the candle delta cannot", () => {
+    const fixture = whaleVersusCrowd({ whaleSize: 50, crowdCount: 100 });
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...FIXED, tierSize: 1 };
+    const whale = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], settings, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.whaleTape) });
+    const crowd = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], settings, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.crowdTape) });
+    expect(whale.outputs.largeBar[0]).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(whale.outputs.smallBar[0]).toBe(0);
+    expect(crowd.outputs.largeBar[0]).toBe(0);
+    expect(crowd.outputs.smallBar[0]).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(fixture.candle.trade_count).toBeNull();
+    expect(barSides(fixture.whaleCandle).delta).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(barSides(fixture.crowdCandle).delta).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    // In auto mode an isolated tape cannot classify before 200 sizes: honest null, not a guess.
+    const auto = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], AUTO, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.whaleTape) });
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[0]).toBeNull();
+  });
+});

+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- 10,000 real BTC-USDT aggregate trades (recorded 2026-10-26)", () => {
+  const BINANCE_SPOT: FixtureIdentity = { provider: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT", symbol: "BTC-USDT" };
+
+  function loadAggregates(): TradeFrame[] {
+    const file = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../../../workers/tests/fixtures/binance-mirror/aggtrades-btcusdt-10k.json.gz");
+    const rows = JSON.parse(gunzipSync(readFileSync(file)).toString("utf8")) as [number, string, string, number, number][];
+    return rows.map(([id, price, quantity, time, maker]) => ({ type: "trade", ...BINANCE_SPOT, ts_exchange: time, price: Number(price), size: Number(quantity), side: maker ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${id}` }) as TradeFrame);
+  }
+
+  it("the auto tier after each commit equals the test's nearest-rank p98 of the same ring, the share is 1 within 1e-9 and the tier totals equal the signed sum", () => {
+    const prints = loadAggregates();
+    expect(prints).toHaveLength(10_000);
+    const firstMinute = Math.ceil(prints[0].ts_exchange / 60_000) * 60_000;
+    const lastMinute = Math.floor(prints[prints.length - 1].ts_exchange / 60_000) * 60_000;
+    const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
+    const byBar: TradeFrame[][] = [];
+    for (let tsOpen = firstMinute; tsOpen < lastMinute; tsOpen += 60_000) {
+      const inside = prints.filter((print) => print.ts_exchange >= tsOpen && print.ts_exchange < tsOpen + 60_000);
+      let volume = 0;
+      let buy = 0;
+      for (const print of inside) {
+        volume += print.size;
+        if (print.side === "buy") buy += print.size;
+      }
+      bars.push({ type: "bar", ...BINANCE_SPOT, interval_ms: 60_000, ts_open: tsOpen, open: inside[0].price, high: Math.max(...inside.map((print) => print.price)), low: Math.min(...inside.map((print) => print.price)), close: inside[inside.length - 1].price, volume, buy_volume: buy, sell_volume: volume - buy, trade_count: inside.length, closed: true } as BarFrame);
+      byBar.push(inside);
+    }
+    expect(bars).toHaveLength(5);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, firstMinute));
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "auto", tierSize: 0.5, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+    const ring: number[] = [];
+    let signed = 0;
+    let tiered = 0;
+    bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar, ticks);
+      const expectedTier = ring.length < TIER_MIN_SIZES ? null : referenceNearestRank(ring.slice(-SIZE_RING_CAPACITY));
+      expect(reading.tier).toBe(expectedTier);
+      expect(reading.share).toBeCloseTo(1, 9);
+      if (index > 0) {
+        expect(expectedTier).not.toBeNull();
+        expect(reading.valid).toBe(true);
+        tiered += (reading.largeBar as number) + (reading.smallBar as number);
+        for (const print of byBar[index]) signed += print.side === "buy" ? print.size : -print.size;
+      }
+      for (const print of byBar[index]) ring.push(print.size);
+    });
+    expect(tiered).toBeCloseTo(signed, 9);
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta aggregates: ${bars.length} full minutes, ${bars.map((bar) => bar.trade_count).join("/")} aggregates per minute, tiers ${engine.committed.map((bar) => (bar.tierAtOpen === null ? "null" : bar.tierAtOpen.toFixed(5))).join(", ")}`);
+  });
+});

+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- marker budget on the protocol's busy instrument (check 5)", () => {
+  it("reports the budget on trendBars with busyTapeFor at default settings with onset-only arrows: 59 per 1,000, still above the 40 signal ceiling, because on a monotone trend the ring high is the previous bar and the condition rarely persists (PM decision)", () => {
+    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: 1000 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 60 });
+    const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []), bars[0].ts_open));
+    const budget = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extras: { ticks } });
+    const verdict = judgeBudget(budget, 40, 2);
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta trendBars(1000) + busyTapeFor(60), defaults", budget, verdict));
+    expect(budget.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(59);
+    expect(budget.maxPerBar).toBe(1);
+    // How often the condition persists from one bar to the next on this fixture: the onset rule can only remove those bars.
+    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showBarLines: true }, { ticks });
+    const reference = referenceMarkers(bars.map((bar, index) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: capture.outputs.largeBar[index], smallBar: capture.outputs.smallBar[index] })), RULE);
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta trendBars: condition held on ${reference.bearishHeld.length + reference.bullishHeld.length} bars, onsets ${reference.bearish.length + reference.bullish.length}`);
+    expect(reference.bearish.length + reference.bullish.length).toBe(59);
+    const deeper = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, m: 3 }, { extras: { ticks } });
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta same instrument, m = 3", deeper, judgeBudget(deeper, 40, 2)));
+    const newHighs = bars.filter((bar, index) => index >= 20 && bar.close > Math.max(...bars.slice(index - 20, index).map((earlier) => earlier.close))).length;
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta trendBars: ${newHighs} of ${bars.length - 20} bars close above their previous 20 closes`);
+    expect(newHighs).toBeGreaterThan(500);
+  }, 60_000);
+});
