+import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame, Side } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { AggressiveTape } from "../../aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";
+import { indicatorTicksOf } from "../../../chart/indicatorTicks";
+import { BINANCE_PERP, FIXTURE_T0, MINUTE_MS, referenceNearestRank, tradeFrame } from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { nyLocalMinuteOfDay } from "../../killzones/killzones";
+import {
+  DAY_MS,
+  MAX_TAPE_SHARE,
+  SIZE_RING_CAPACITY,
+  TIER_MIN_SIZES,
+  anchorStartFor,
+  createSizeRing,
+  createSizeSplitEngine,
+  evaluateMarkers,
+  formatClock,
+  formatMagnitude,
+  formatSigned,
+  scanWindow,
+  shareEligible,
+  statusOf,
+  tapeShareOf,
+  type SizeSplitMathSettings,
+} from "../sizeSplitDeltaMath";
+
+const FIXED: SizeSplitMathSettings = { tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 1, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 };
+
+interface HandPrint {
+  offsetMs: number;
+  price: number;
+  size: number;
+  side: Side;
+}
+
+/** A store that records live from `liveSince`, holding the given prints. */
+function storeOf(prints: readonly ReturnType<typeof tradeFrame>[], liveSince: number): { tape: AggressiveTape; ticks: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof indicatorTicksOf>> } {
+  const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => liveSince);
+  tape.reset();
+  for (const print of prints) tape.add(print);
+  const ticks = indicatorTicksOf(tape);
+  if (ticks === null) throw new Error("a tape always yields ticks");
+  return { tape, ticks };
+}
+
+function barAt(tsOpen: number, close: number, volume: number, closed = true): BarFrame {
+  return { type: "bar", ...BINANCE_PERP, interval_ms: MINUTE_MS, ts_open: tsOpen, open: close, high: close, low: close, close, volume, trade_count: null, closed } as BarFrame;
+}
+
+function printsAt(tsOpen: number, hand: readonly HandPrint[], tag: string) {
+  return hand.map((print, index) => tradeFrame(tsOpen + print.offsetMs, print.price, print.size, print.side, BINANCE_PERP, `${tag}-${index}`));
+}
+
+/**
+ * The hand-computed three-bar fixture (success criterion 1), fixed tier 1.0:
+ *   A: buy 2.0, sell 0.5, buy 0.3   -> large +2.0, small -0.2, volume 2.8
+ *   B: sell 1.5, sell 0.4, buy 0.1  -> large -1.5, small -0.3, volume 2.0
+ *   C: buy 1.0 (boundary), buy 0.2  -> large +1.0, small +0.2, volume 1.2
+ * cumulative large 2.0, 0.5, 1.5; small -0.2, -0.5, -0.3; share 1 on every bar.
+ */
+const A = FIXTURE_T0;
+const B = A + MINUTE_MS;
+const C = B + MINUTE_MS;
+const HAND_A: HandPrint[] = [
+  { offsetMs: 1_000, price: 100, size: 2, side: "buy" },
+  { offsetMs: 2_000, price: 99.5, size: 0.5, side: "sell" },
+  { offsetMs: 3_000, price: 100.2, size: 0.3, side: "buy" },
+];
+const HAND_B: HandPrint[] = [
+  { offsetMs: 1_000, price: 99, size: 1.5, side: "sell" },
+  { offsetMs: 2_000, price: 98.8, size: 0.4, side: "sell" },
+  { offsetMs: 3_000, price: 99.1, size: 0.1, side: "buy" },
+];
+const HAND_C: HandPrint[] = [
+  { offsetMs: 1_000, price: 99.5, size: 1, side: "buy" },
+  { offsetMs: 2_000, price: 99.6, size: 0.2, side: "buy" },
+];
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- hand-computed three-bar fixture", () => {
+  it("classifies with the boundary print large, sums signed sizes per tier and cumulates since the anchor", () => {
+    const { ticks } = storeOf([...printsAt(A, HAND_A, "a"), ...printsAt(B, HAND_B, "b"), ...printsAt(C, HAND_C, "c")], A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    const a = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    expect(a.largeBar).toBeCloseTo(2, 12);
+    expect(a.smallBar).toBeCloseTo(-0.2, 12);
+    expect(a.large).toBeCloseTo(2, 12);
+    expect(a.small).toBeCloseTo(-0.2, 12);
+    expect(a.share).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
+    expect(a.valid).toBe(true);
+    expect(a.confirmed).toBe(true);
+    expect(a.status).toBe("ready");
+    expect(a.tier).toBe(1);
+    const b = engine.onBar(barAt(B, 99.1, 2), ticks);
+    expect(b.largeBar).toBeCloseTo(-1.5, 12);
+    expect(b.smallBar).toBeCloseTo(-0.3, 12);
+    expect(b.large).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 12);
+    expect(b.small).toBeCloseTo(-0.5, 12);
+    const c = engine.onBar(barAt(C, 99.6, 1.2), ticks);
+    expect(c.largeBar).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
+    expect(c.smallBar).toBeCloseTo(0.2, 12);
+    expect(c.large).toBeCloseTo(1.5, 12);
+    expect(c.small).toBeCloseTo(-0.3, 12);
+    // The ring a bar is judged against holds the valid committed bars before it: A and B.
+    expect(c.ringLength).toBe(2);
+    expect(c.state).toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.committed.map((bar) => bar.valid)).toEqual([true, true, true]);
+    expect(engine.base.large).toBeCloseTo(1.5, 12);
+  });
+
+  it("in auto mode nothing is classified until 200 sizes have trained the ring, while the sizes still train it (amendment 3)", () => {
+    const { ticks } = storeOf([...printsAt(A, HAND_A, "a"), ...printsAt(B, HAND_B, "b")], A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ ...FIXED, tierMode: "auto" });
+    const a = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    expect(a.tier).toBeNull();
+    expect(a.largeBar).toBeNull();
+    expect(a.large).toBeNull();
+    expect(a.valid).toBe(false);
+    expect(a.status).toBe("learning");
+    // Sizes train the ring at commit, so the bar's own reading still counts none.
+    expect(a.sizesLearned).toBe(0);
+    engine.onBar(barAt(B, 99.1, 2), ticks);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.count).toBe(6);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.values()).toEqual([2, 0.5, 0.3, 1.5, 0.4, 0.1]);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- size ring and threshold", () => {
+  it("nearest-rank p98 equals the reference on every push, evicting the oldest size first", () => {
+    const ring = createSizeRing(8, 3);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBeNull();
+    ring.push([5, 1, 3]);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(5);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(referenceNearestRank(ring.values()));
+    ring.push([2, 4, 6, 7, 8]);
+    expect(ring.count).toBe(8);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(8);
+    ring.push([0.5]);
+    expect(ring.values()).toEqual([1, 3, 2, 4, 6, 7, 8, 0.5]);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(referenceNearestRank(ring.values()));
+    ring.push([9]);
+    expect(ring.values()).toEqual([3, 2, 4, 6, 7, 8, 0.5, 9]);
+    expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(9);
+    ring.push([Number.NaN, -1, 0]);
+    expect(ring.count).toBe(8);
+  });
+
+  it("the production ring holds 4,096 sizes, needs 200 and matches the reference on a random stream", () => {
+    const ring = createSizeRing();
+    let state = 7;
+    const random = () => {
+      state = (Math.imul(state, 1664525) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
+      return state / 4294967296;
+    };
+    const all: number[] = [];
+    for (let batch = 0; batch < 60; batch += 1) {
+      const sizes = Array.from({ length: 100 }, () => (random() < 0.02 ? 1 : 0.01 + 0.039 * random()));
+      ring.push(sizes);
+      all.push(...sizes);
+      const expected = all.length < TIER_MIN_SIZES ? null : referenceNearestRank(all.slice(-SIZE_RING_CAPACITY));
+      expect(ring.threshold()).toBe(expected);
+    }
+    expect(ring.count).toBe(SIZE_RING_CAPACITY);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- window scan", () => {
+  it("takes prints in [from, to) only, skips invalid sizes and prices, and bounds the size list to 4,096", () => {
+    const prints = [
+      tradeFrame(A - 1, 100, 1, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "before"),
+      tradeFrame(A, 100, 2, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "at-open"),
+      tradeFrame(A + 30_000, 100, Number.NaN, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "nan-size"),
+      tradeFrame(A + 30_001, 0, 3, "sell", BINANCE_PERP, "zero-price"),
+      tradeFrame(A + 30_002, 100, 0, "sell", BINANCE_PERP, "zero-size"),
+      tradeFrame(A + 59_999, 100, 0.5, "sell", BINANCE_PERP, "last-inside"),
+      tradeFrame(A + 60_000, 100, 4, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "at-end"),
+    ];
+    const { ticks } = storeOf(prints, A - 1);
+    const scan = scanWindow(ticks, A, A + MINUTE_MS, 1);
+    expect(scan.coverage).toBe("all");
+    expect(scan.largeBar).toBe(2);
+    expect(scan.smallBar).toBe(-0.5);
+    expect(scan.tapeVolume).toBe(2.5);
+    expect(scan.printCount).toBe(2);
+    expect(scan.sizes).toEqual([2, 0.5]);
+    const untiered = scanWindow(ticks, A, A + MINUTE_MS, null);
+    expect(untiered.largeBar).toBeNull();
+    expect(untiered.sizes).toEqual([2, 0.5]);
+    expect(scanWindow(undefined, A, A + MINUTE_MS, 1).coverage).toBe("noStore");
+    const many = Array.from({ length: 5_000 }, (_, index) => tradeFrame(A + index, 100, 0.001 + index * 1e-7, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, `m${index}`));
+    const big = scanWindow(storeOf(many, A).ticks, A, A + MINUTE_MS, 1).sizes;
+    expect(big).toHaveLength(SIZE_RING_CAPACITY);
+    expect(big[0]).toBe(many[5_000 - SIZE_RING_CAPACITY].size);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- tape share gate, fail closed (rule 5, amendment 5)", () => {
+  it("pins the share cases", () => {
+    expect(tapeShareOf(2.8, 2.8)).toBeCloseTo(1, 12);
+    expect(tapeShareOf(2.8, 0)).toBeNull();
+    expect(tapeShareOf(2.8, Number.NaN)).toBeNull();
+    expect(tapeShareOf(0, 5)).toBe(0);
+    expect(tapeShareOf(0, 0)).toBeNull();
+    expect(shareEligible(1, 0.5)).toBe(true);
+    expect(shareEligible(0.5, 0.5)).toBe(true);
+    expect(shareEligible(0.49, 0.5)).toBe(false);
+    expect(shareEligible(MAX_TAPE_SHARE, 0.5)).toBe(true);
+    expect(shareEligible(1.26, 0.5)).toBe(false);
+    expect(shareEligible(null, 0.5)).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it("zero or missing candle volume with prints is ineligible; no prints with positive volume is share 0; no prints and zero volume is unknown; above 1.25 is a mismatch", () => {
+    const { ticks } = storeOf(printsAt(A, HAND_A, "a"), A);
+    const zeroVolume = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(A, 100, 0), ticks);
+    expect(zeroVolume.share).toBeNull();
+    expect(zeroVolume.valid).toBe(false);
+    expect(zeroVolume.large).toBeNull();
+    expect(zeroVolume.status).toBe("shareUnknown");
+    const noPrints = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(B, 100, 3), ticks);
+    expect(noPrints.share).toBe(0);
+    expect(noPrints.valid).toBe(false);
+    expect(noPrints.status).toBe("shareLow");
+    const nothing = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(B, 100, 0), ticks);
+    expect(nothing.share).toBeNull();
+    expect(nothing.status).toBe("shareUnknown");
+    const mismatch = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1.75), ticks);
+    expect(mismatch.share).toBeCloseTo(1.6, 12);
+    expect(mismatch.valid).toBe(false);
+    expect(mismatch.status).toBe("shareMismatch");
+    const low = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(A, 100, 7), ticks);
+    expect(low.share).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 12);
+    expect(low.status).toBe("shareLow");
+  });
+
+  it("status precedence, first match wins", () => {
+    const base = { coverage: "all" as const, tapeStarting: false, tierAtOpen: 1, share: 1, eligible: true, committed: true };
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, coverage: "noStore" }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("noFeed");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, coverage: "none" }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("noFeed");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, coverage: "none", tapeStarting: true }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("tapeStarting");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, coverage: "thin", tierAtOpen: null }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("thinned");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, tierAtOpen: null, share: null }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("learning");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, share: null, eligible: false }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("shareUnknown");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, share: 1.6, eligible: false }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("shareMismatch");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, share: 0.3, eligible: false }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("shareLow");
+    expect(statusOf(base, 3, 5, 20)).toBe("gap");
+    expect(statusOf(base, 3, 20, 20)).toBe("ready");
+    expect(statusOf({ ...base, committed: false }, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("forming");
+    expect(statusOf(base, 0, 0, 20)).toBe("ready");
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- commit paths (rule 6, amendment 1)", () => {
+  it("an explicit close rescans from the store: a print that landed after the last revision is in the committed flow and trains the next threshold", () => {
+    const { tape, ticks } = storeOf(printsAt(A, HAND_A.slice(0, 2), "a"), A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    const revision = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 2.5, false), ticks);
+    expect(revision.largeBar).toBe(2);
+    expect(revision.smallBar).toBe(-0.5);
+    expect(revision.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(A + 3_000, 100.2, 0.3, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "late"));
+    const closed = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    expect(closed.smallBar).toBeCloseTo(-0.2, 12);
+    expect(closed.confirmed).toBe(true);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.values()).toEqual([2, 0.5, 0.3]);
+    // A later dispatch of the same bar changes nothing, whatever the store or the candle now say.
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(A + 4_000, 100.2, 7, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "after-commit"));
+    const again = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 50), ticks);
+    expect(again).toEqual(closed);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.count).toBe(3);
+  });
+
+  it("a supersession commits the last revision values without a rescan: a print between the last revision and the next bar is excluded from the flow and from the next threshold until a rebuild", () => {
+    const { tape, ticks } = storeOf(printsAt(A, HAND_A.slice(0, 2), "a"), A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    const revision = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 2.5, false), ticks);
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(A + 3_000, 100.2, 0.7777, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "between"));
+    for (const print of printsAt(B, HAND_B, "b")) tape.add(print);
+    const next = engine.onBar(barAt(B, 99.1, 2, false), ticks);
+    const committed = engine.committed[0];
+    expect(committed.tsOpen).toBe(A);
+    expect(committed.largeBar).toBe(revision.largeBar);
+    expect(committed.smallBar).toBe(revision.smallBar);
+    expect(committed.confirmed).toBe(false);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.values()).toEqual([2, 0.5]);
+    expect(engine.sizeRing.values()).not.toContain(0.7777);
+    expect(next.large).toBeCloseTo(2 - 1.5, 12);
+    // A rebuild (a fresh engine over the same store) sees the print.
+    const rebuilt = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    rebuilt.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 2.5, false), ticks);
+    rebuilt.onBar(barAt(B, 99.1, 2, false), ticks);
+    expect(rebuilt.committed[0].smallBar).toBeCloseTo(-0.5 + 0.7777, 12);
+    expect(rebuilt.sizeRing.values()).toContain(0.7777);
+  });
+
+  it("ignores a dispatch older than the tail", () => {
+    const { ticks } = storeOf([...printsAt(A, HAND_A, "a"), ...printsAt(B, HAND_B, "b")], A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    const b = engine.onBar(barAt(B, 99.1, 2), ticks);
+    const stale = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    expect(stale).toEqual(b);
+    expect(engine.committed).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- anchor and gaps (rules 8 and 9, round 3 ruling 3)", () => {
+  const midnight = Math.ceil(FIXTURE_T0 / DAY_MS) * DAY_MS;
+  const lastOfDay = midnight - MINUTE_MS;
+
+  it("anchor windows are UTC-aligned days or weeks, or none for the tape", () => {
+    expect(anchorStartFor(lastOfDay, "day")).toBe(midnight - DAY_MS);
+    expect(anchorStartFor(midnight, "day")).toBe(midnight);
+    expect(anchorStartFor(midnight + 5 * MINUTE_MS, "week")).toBe(Math.floor(midnight / (7 * DAY_MS)) * 7 * DAY_MS);
+    expect(anchorStartFor(midnight, "tape")).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it("the first dispatch inside a new day resets the base, empties the marker ring and clears the condition, the forming bar showing only its own contribution", () => {
+    const { ticks } = storeOf([...printsAt(lastOfDay, HAND_A, "x"), ...printsAt(midnight, HAND_B, "y")], lastOfDay);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ ...FIXED, anchor: "day" });
+    const x = engine.onBar(barAt(lastOfDay, 100.2, 2.8), ticks);
+    expect(x.large).toBeCloseTo(2, 12);
+    expect(engine.markerRing).toHaveLength(1);
+    const y = engine.onBar(barAt(midnight, 99.1, 2, false), ticks);
+    expect(engine.anchorStart).toBe(midnight);
+    expect(y.large).toBeCloseTo(-1.5, 12);
+    expect(y.small).toBeCloseTo(-0.3, 12);
+    expect(y.ringLength).toBe(0);
+    expect(y.lastDivergence).toBeNull();
+    expect(y.lastConflict).toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.base).toEqual({ large: 0, small: 0 });
+    const yClosed = engine.onBar(barAt(midnight, 99.1, 2), ticks);
+    expect(yClosed.large).toBeCloseTo(-1.5, 12);
+    expect(engine.markerRing).toHaveLength(1);
+  });
+
+  it("a gap carries the cumulative, empties the ring and is named until K valid bars have followed", () => {
+    const settings = { ...FIXED, K: 2 };
+    const bars = [A, B, C, C + MINUTE_MS, C + 2 * MINUTE_MS];
+    const prints = bars.flatMap((tsOpen, index) => printsAt(tsOpen, HAND_C, `g${index}`));
+    const { ticks } = storeOf(prints, A);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(settings);
+    const r0 = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[0], 99.6, 1.2), ticks);
+    expect(r0.status).toBe("ready");
+    const r1 = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[1], 99.6, 1.2), ticks);
+    expect(r1.large).toBeCloseTo(2, 12);
+    const gap = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[2], 99.6, 0), ticks);
+    expect(gap.large).toBeNull();
+    expect(gap.status).toBe("shareUnknown");
+    const after = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[3], 99.6, 1.2), ticks);
+    expect(after.large).toBeCloseTo(3, 12);
+    expect(after.status).toBe("gap");
+    expect(after.gapBars).toBe(1);
+    expect(after.ringLength).toBe(0);
+    const later = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[4], 99.6, 1.2), ticks);
+    expect(later.status).toBe("gap");
+    expect(engine.markerRing).toHaveLength(2);
+    const ready = engine.onBar(barAt(bars[4] + MINUTE_MS, 99.6, 0), ticks);
+    expect(ready.status).toBe("shareUnknown");
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- marker rule (rule 11, amendment 2, PM onset amendment)", () => {
+  const settings = { K: 2, m: 2, q: 2 };
+  const NONE = { conflict: false, divergence: null };
+  const ring = [
+    { close: 100, largeBar: 2, smallBar: -1, large: 2 },
+    { close: 101, largeBar: 1, smallBar: -1, large: 3 },
+  ];
+
+  it("a strict new high confirms when large_t exceeds large_h, lags on equality, and arrows at depth m times the mean absolute contribution", () => {
+    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: 0.5, smallBar: 0, large: 3.5 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("confirms");
+    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: 0, smallBar: 0, large: 3 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("lags");
+    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 0, smallBar: 0, large: 3 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("noExtreme");
+    // mabsL = 1.5, depth needed 3: large_t <= 0
+    const shallow = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -2.5, smallBar: 0, large: 0.5 }, NONE, settings);
+    expect(shallow.state).toBe("lags");
+    expect(shallow.divergence).toBeNull();
+    const bearish = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, NONE, settings);
+    expect(bearish.divergence).toBe("bearish");
+    expect(bearish.depth).toBe(-3);
+    const bullish = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 99, largeBar: 3, smallBar: 0, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
+    // l is the bar with close 100 (large 2); 6 >= 2 + 3
+    expect(bullish.divergence).toBe("bullish");
+    expect(bullish.state).toBe("lags");
+    expect(bullish.depth).toBe(4);
+    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring.slice(0, 1), { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, NONE, settings).state).toBeNull();
+    // Onset only: the same condition held at the previous valid bar draws nothing but still reads as held.
+    const continuing = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, { conflict: false, divergence: "bearish" }, settings);
+    expect(continuing.divergenceHeld).toBe("bearish");
+    expect(continuing.divergence).toBeNull();
+    expect(continuing.depth).toBeNull();
+    const flipped = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, { conflict: false, divergence: "bullish" }, settings);
+    expect(flipped.divergence).toBe("bearish");
+  });
+
+  it("the conflict holds when the two tier sums oppose at strength q·sqrt(K+1) and fires only at its onset", () => {
+    // ring sums: large 3, small -2; mabsL 1.5, mabsS 1; strength 2*sqrt(3) = 3.464 -> need |sumL| >= 5.2, |sumS| >= 3.46
+    const held = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: -2, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
+    expect(held.conflictHeld).toBe(true);
+    expect(held.conflictOnset).toBe(true);
+    expect(held.conflictSums).toEqual([6, -4]);
+    const continuing = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: -2, large: 6 }, { conflict: true, divergence: null }, settings);
+    expect(continuing.conflictHeld).toBe(true);
+    expect(continuing.conflictOnset).toBe(false);
+    const sameSign = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: 5, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
+    expect(sameSign.conflictHeld).toBe(false);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- formatting (amendment 7)", () => {
+  it("three significant digits with k/M suffixes and an explicit sign", () => {
+    expect(formatSigned(12.4)).toBe("+12.4");
+    expect(formatSigned(-8.1)).toBe("−8.1");
+    expect(formatSigned(0)).toBe("0");
+    expect(formatSigned(1234)).toBe("+1.23k");
+    expect(formatSigned(-2_500_000)).toBe("−2.5M");
+    expect(formatSigned(0.00608)).toBe("+0.00608");
+    expect(formatSigned(100)).toBe("+100");
+    expect(formatMagnitude(0.106)).toBe("0.106");
+    expect(formatMagnitude(20)).toBe("20");
+    expect(formatMagnitude(2_000)).toBe("2k");
+    expect(formatMagnitude(Number.NaN)).toBe("—");
+  });
+
+  it("formats the clock in UTC for Binance and New York for dxFeed", () => {
+    expect(formatClock(FIXTURE_T0, "BINANCE")).toBe("20:26");
+    const ny = nyLocalMinuteOfDay(FIXTURE_T0);
+    expect(formatClock(FIXTURE_T0, "DXFEED")).toBe(`${String(Math.floor(ny / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(ny % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- the tape starting inside the window (UX finding 2)", () => {
+  it("a window that began before the live recording reads tapeStarting, not noFeed; a store without a live stretch reads noFeed", () => {
+    const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => A + 20_000);
+    tape.reset();
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(A + 20_000, 100, 1, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "first-live"));
+    const ticks = indicatorTicksOf(tape)!;
+    expect(ticks.liveSince).toBe(A + 20_000);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    const first = engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1, false), ticks);
+    expect(first.status).toBe("tapeStarting");
+    expect(first.large).toBeNull();
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(B + 1_000, 100, 1, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "next"));
+    const next = engine.onBar(barAt(B, 100, 1, false), ticks);
+    expect(next.status).toBe("forming");
+    expect(next.large).toBe(1);
+    const empty = new AggressiveTape(() => A);
+    empty.reset();
+    const silent = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1, false), indicatorTicksOf(empty)!);
+    expect(silent.status).toBe("noFeed");
+    expect(createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED).onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1, false), undefined).status).toBe("noFeed");
+  });
+
+  it("a REST-only snapshot (absorb of no trades) sets liveSince but has never held a print: noFeed until the first print arrives", () => {
+    const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => A + 20_000);
+    tape.reset();
+    tape.absorb([]);
+    const ticks = indicatorTicksOf(tape)!;
+    expect(ticks.liveSince).toBe(A + 20_000);
+    expect(ticks.prints.length).toBe(0);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine(FIXED);
+    expect(engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1, false), ticks).status).toBe("noFeed");
+    tape.add(tradeFrame(A + 30_000, 100, 1, "buy", BINANCE_PERP, "first-live"));
+    expect(engine.onBar(barAt(A, 100, 1, false), ticks).status).toBe("tapeStarting");
+  });
+});

+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS } from "../../../chart/candleLifecycle";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import {
+  busyTapeFor,
+  formatFormingBarProbe,
+  formatPooledReplayProbe,
+  formatReplayProbe,
+  formatRolloverProbe,
+  probeFormingBar,
+  probeReplay,
+  probeReplayPooled,
+  probeRollover,
+  tapeOf,
+  ticksOf,
+  trendBars,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, type SizeSplitDeltaSettings } from "../sizeSplitDelta";
+import { withTapeVolume } from "./helpers";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/**
+ * Protocol check 6 with the brief's frozen method: `probeReplayPooled` (three
+ * untimed warm-ups, five timed runs, `onBar` plus `readOutput`) on 10,080
+ * trend bars whose 20 prints each sit in one store of 201,600 prints (under
+ * the tape's cap, so every window is `"all"`); `probeReplay` for the real
+ * activation; `probeFormingBar` in fixed mode so the whole classification path
+ * runs on 1,000 prints revised 50 times a second; `probeRollover` for the
+ * bounded state. Set SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT=<file> to keep the JSON.
+ */
+
+const POOLED_P99_CEILING_MS = 0.5;
+const FORMING_P99_CEILING_MS = 0.1;
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- performance (protocol check 6, brief Performance)", () => {
+  it("pooled p99 under 0.5 ms, forming p99 under 0.1 ms, replay wall under 5 s, every output key 10,080 long after the rollover with flat heap growth", () => {
+    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, driftPerBar: 0.5, noise: 25 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 20 });
+    const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const prints = bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []);
+    expect(prints).toHaveLength(201_600);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, bars[0].ts_open));
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, anchor: "tape" };
+
+    const pooled = probeReplayPooled(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { warmupRuns: 3, timedRuns: 5, extras: { ticks } });
+    const replay = probeReplay(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { ticks });
+    const rollover = probeRollover(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { extraBars: 1_000, extras: { ticks } });
+    const fixed: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...settings, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 0.5 };
+    const forming = probeFormingBar(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars.slice(-300), fixed, { seconds: 60, revisionsPerSecond: 50, printsPerMinute: 1_000 });
+
+    const lines = [
+      formatPooledReplayProbe(pooled),
+      formatReplayProbe(replay),
+      formatFormingBarProbe(forming),
+      `sizeSplitDelta p99 side by side: replay (pooled) ${pooled.pooled.p99.toFixed(4)} ms, forming bar ${forming.onBar.p99.toFixed(4)} ms`,
+      formatRolloverProbe(rollover),
+    ];
+    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
+    if (process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT) {
+      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT), { recursive: true });
+      writeFileSync(process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT, `${JSON.stringify({ node: process.version, pooled, replay, forming, rollover }, null, 2)}\n`);
+    }
+
+    expect(pooled.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.count).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS * 5);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.p99).toBeLessThan(POOLED_P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(replay.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(replay.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
+    expect(replay.wallMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5_000);
+    expect(forming.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(forming.revisions).toBe(3_000);
+    expect(forming.prints).toBe(1_000);
+    expect(forming.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(FORMING_P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(rollover.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(rollover.retainedBars).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
+    expect(rollover.outputLengths).toEqual({ large: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, small: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, largeBar: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, smallBar: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS });
+    expect(rollover.markersBeforeRetention).toBe(0);
+    // The heap slope is meaningful only under NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc; without it the probe says so and the number is reported, not judged.
+    if (rollover.heap.gcExposed) expect(rollover.bytesPerExtraBar).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3 * 370);
+    else console.log("sizeSplitDelta rollover heap slope not judged: gc not exposed");
+  }, 600_000);
+});

+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame, TradeFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS } from "../../../chart/candleLifecycle";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import { AggressiveTape } from "../../aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";
+import { indicatorTicksOf } from "../../../chart/indicatorTicks";
+import {
+  DXFEED_EQUITY,
+  FIXTURE_T0,
+  busyTapeFor,
+  checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent,
+  checkLiveReplayEquivalence,
+  checkRebuiltEquivalence,
+  checkTruncationInvariance,
+  compressionBreakoutBars,
+  diffMarkers,
+  diffOutputs,
+  driveLive,
+  equityUnsidedBars,
+  historyTapeOf,
+  openLiveSession,
+  referenceClassify,
+  replayCapture,
+  sampledIndices,
+  sizeSplitFixture,
+  summarizeEquivalence,
+  summarizeTruncation,
+  tapeOf,
+  ticksOf,
+  timeGapBars,
+  trendBars,
+  type Arrival,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, type SizeSplitDeltaSettings } from "../sizeSplitDelta";
+import { SIZE_RING_CAPACITY, TIER_MIN_SIZES, anchorStartFor, createSizeSplitEngine } from "../sizeSplitDeltaMath";
+import { fixtureTicks, markerIndices, withTapeVolume } from "./helpers";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/** The per-bar seams are compared too, so they are switched on; the anchor is the tape so no reset hides a difference. */
+const AUTO: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, anchor: "tape", showBarLines: true, showConflict: true };
+const FIXED: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 0.5 };
+const FIXTURE = sizeSplitFixture();
+
+function noNaN(outputs: Readonly<Record<string, readonly (number | null)[]>>): void {
+  for (const values of Object.values(outputs)) for (const value of values) expect(value === null || Number.isFinite(value)).toBe(true);
+}
+
+/** A busy synthetic instrument whose candles carry their prints' volume, with every print in one store. */
+function busyInstrument(count: number, printsPerBar: number, seed = 5) {
+  const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count, driftPerBar: 0.5, noise: 25 });
+  const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar, seed });
+  const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+  const prints = bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []);
+  return { bars, prints, printsByBar, ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, bars[0].ts_open)) };
+}
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- no future data: truncation invariance (protocol check 1, rule 13)", () => {
+  it("passes at every cut of the mechanism fixture with the whole tape in the store: a committed bar ignores prints past its window end", () => {
+    const report = checkTruncationInvariance(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, { extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) } });
+    console.log(summarizeTruncation(report));
+    expect(report.checked).toBe(FIXTURE.bars.length);
+    expect(report.ok).toBe(true);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("passes on compressionBreakoutBars and a sampled trend sweep with a busy tape", () => {
+    const breakout = compressionBreakoutBars();
+    const breakoutPrints = busyTapeFor(breakout.bars, { printsPerBar: 40 });
+    const breakoutBars = withTapeVolume(breakout.bars, breakoutPrints);
+    const breakoutTicks = ticksOf(tapeOf(breakoutBars.flatMap((bar) => breakoutPrints.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []), breakoutBars[0].ts_open));
+    const first = checkTruncationInvariance(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, breakoutBars, AUTO, { extras: { ticks: breakoutTicks }, indices: sampledIndices(breakoutBars.length, 13) });
+    expect(first.ok).toBe(true);
+    const busy = busyInstrument(600, 60);
+    const second = checkTruncationInvariance(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, busy.bars, AUTO, { extras: { ticks: busy.ticks }, indices: sampledIndices(busy.bars.length, 17) });
+    expect(second.ok).toBe(true);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("a store loaded with the whole fixture up front equals one fed bar by bar", () => {
+    const upFront = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
+    const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => FIXTURE_T0);
+    tape.reset();
+    const ticks = indicatorTicksOf(tape)!;
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = FIXTURE.bars.map((bar, index) => ({
+      bar,
+      act: () => {
+        for (const print of FIXTURE.printsByBar[index]) tape.add(print);
+      },
+    }));
+    const fed = driveLive(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, arrivals, AUTO, { ticks });
+    expect(diffOutputs(upFront.outputs, fed.outputs)).toEqual([]);
+    const markers = diffMarkers(upFront.markers, fed.markers);
+    expect(markers.leftOnly).toEqual([]);
+    expect(markers.rightOnly).toEqual([]);
+  }, 60_000);
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- replay versus live equivalence (protocol check 2, round 3 ruling 4)", () => {
+  it("ends on the same committed values and markers whether fed live with forming revisions or replayed, both reading the same store", () => {
+    const report = checkLiveReplayEquivalence(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, { revisionsPerBar: 5, extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) } });
+    console.log(summarizeEquivalence("sizeSplitDelta mechanism fixture", report));
+    expect(report.mutations.filter((mutation) => mutation === "REPLACED_EDGE")).toHaveLength(FIXTURE.bars.length * 5);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.live.markers.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    noNaN(report.live.outputs);
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  it("exact eligibility after the rollover: per-bar seams, cumulatives and markers agree from the first retained bar whose anchor start and preceding 4,096 committed sizes both lie inside the retained series", () => {
+    const extra = 300;
+    const busy = busyInstrument(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS + extra, 20, 11);
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, anchor: "day" };
+    const live = driveLive(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, busy.bars.map((bar) => ({ bar })), settings, { ticks: busy.ticks });
+    expect(live.bars).toHaveLength(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
+    const retained = live.bars;
+    const replay = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, retained, settings, { ticks: busy.ticks });
+    let sizesBefore = 0;
+    let eligibleFrom = -1;
+    for (let index = 0; index < retained.length; index += 1) {
+      const anchorInside = (anchorStartFor(retained[index].ts_open, "day") as number) >= retained[0].ts_open;
+      if (anchorInside && sizesBefore >= SIZE_RING_CAPACITY) {
+        eligibleFrom = index;
+        break;
+      }
+      sizesBefore += (busy.printsByBar.get(retained[index].ts_open) ?? []).length;
+    }
+    expect(eligibleFrom).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    let compared = 0;
+    for (const key of Object.keys(replay.outputs)) {
+      for (let index = eligibleFrom; index < retained.length; index += 1) {
+        expect(replay.outputs[key][index]).toBe(live.outputs[key][index]);
+        if (key === "large" && live.outputs[key][index] !== null) compared += 1;
+      }
+    }
+    expect(compared).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    const cutoff = retained[eligibleFrom].ts_open;
+    const liveMarkers = live.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= cutoff);
+    const replayMarkers = replay.markers.filter((call) => call.time >= cutoff);
+    const diff = diffMarkers(liveMarkers, replayMarkers);
+    expect(diff.leftOnly).toEqual([]);
+    expect(diff.rightOnly).toEqual([]);
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta retention equivalence: eligible from retained bar ${eligibleFrom}, ${compared} large values compared equal, ${liveMarkers.length} markers compared`);
+  }, 300_000);
+
+  it("forming prefix (amendment 4): at every revision the forming output equals the frozen cumulative plus the contribution of the prints received so far", () => {
+    const index = 60;
+    const bars = FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, index + 1);
+    const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => FIXTURE_T0);
+    tape.reset();
+    for (let bar = 0; bar < index; bar += 1) for (const print of FIXTURE.printsByBar[bar]) tape.add(print);
+    const ticks = indicatorTicksOf(tape)!;
+    const session = openLiveSession(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, AUTO, { ticks }, []);
+    try {
+      for (let bar = 0; bar < index; bar += 1) session.dispatch({ bar: bars[bar] });
+      const before = session.capture();
+      const base = before.outputs.large[index - 1] as number;
+      const baseSmall = before.outputs.small[index - 1] as number;
+      const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "auto", tierSize: 0.5, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+      for (let bar = 0; bar < index; bar += 1) engine.onBar(bars[bar], ticks);
+      const forming = bars[index];
+      const prints = FIXTURE.printsByBar[index];
+      const revisions = 6;
+      let received: TradeFrame[] = [];
+      for (let revision = 1; revision <= revisions; revision += 1) {
+        const cutoff = forming.ts_open + Math.floor((revision / revisions) * forming.interval_ms);
+        const incoming = prints.filter((print) => print.ts_exchange < cutoff && !received.includes(print));
+        for (const print of incoming) tape.add(print);
+        received = [...received, ...incoming];
+        const volume = received.reduce((sum, print) => sum + print.size, 0);
+        const closed = revision === revisions;
+        session.dispatch({ bar: { ...forming, volume, close: received[received.length - 1]?.price ?? forming.open, closed } as BarFrame });
+        const capture = session.capture();
+        const tier = engine.tail?.tierAtOpen ?? engine.sizeRing.threshold();
+        const expected = referenceClassify(received, forming.ts_open, forming.ts_open + forming.interval_ms, tier);
+        expect(capture.outputs.large[index]).toBeCloseTo(base + (expected.largeBar as number), 9);
+        expect(capture.outputs.small[index]).toBeCloseTo(baseSmall + (expected.smallBar as number), 9);
+        expect(capture.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBeCloseTo(expected.largeBar as number, 9);
+      }
+      expect(received).toHaveLength(prints.length);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  }, 60_000);
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- gap, duplicate, late and reconnect behaviour (protocol check 3, rule 6, amendment 1)", () => {
+  const ticks = fixtureTicks(FIXTURE);
+
+  it("duplicate closed bar is idempotent and adds no marker", () => {
+    const report = checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, 75, { extras: { ticks } });
+    expect(report.duplicateMutation).toBe("REPLACED_EDGE");
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("late bar rebuilds to the replay", () => {
+    const report = checkRebuiltEquivalence(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, 100, { extras: { ticks } });
+    expect(report.sawRebuilt).toBe(true);
+    expect(report.equal).toBe(true);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("a print arriving after the tail's last revision and before supersession leaves the committed outputs equal to the displayed history and to a replay built from the same displayed decisions; a REBUILT then includes it", () => {
+    const index = 80;
+    const tape = new AggressiveTape(() => FIXTURE_T0);
+    tape.reset();
+    for (let bar = 0; bar <= index; bar += 1) for (const print of FIXTURE.printsByBar[bar]) if (bar < index || print.ts_exchange < FIXTURE.bars[index].ts_open + 50_000) tape.add(print);
+    const live = indicatorTicksOf(tape)!;
+    const session = openLiveSession(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, AUTO, { ticks: live }, []);
+    try {
+      for (let bar = 0; bar < index; bar += 1) session.dispatch({ bar: FIXTURE.bars[bar] });
+      const partial = FIXTURE.printsByBar[index].filter((print) => print.ts_exchange < FIXTURE.bars[index].ts_open + 50_000);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...FIXTURE.bars[index], closed: false, volume: partial.reduce((sum, print) => sum + print.size, 0) } as BarFrame });
+      const displayed = session.capture();
+      const shownLarge = displayed.outputs.largeBar[index];
+      // The late print: one of the fixture's own large prints of this bar, landing after the last revision.
+      const late = FIXTURE.printsByBar[index].find((print) => print.ts_exchange >= FIXTURE.bars[index].ts_open + 50_000 && print.size === 1)!;
+      tape.add(late);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: FIXTURE.bars[index + 1] });
+      const after = session.capture();
+      expect(after.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBe(shownLarge);
+      expect(after.outputs.large[index]).toBe(displayed.outputs.large[index]);
+      // The replay built from the same displayed decisions (a store without the late print) equals the live history.
+      const tapeWithout = new AggressiveTape(() => FIXTURE_T0);
+      tapeWithout.reset();
+      for (let bar = 0; bar <= index; bar += 1) for (const print of FIXTURE.printsByBar[bar]) if (bar < index || partial.includes(print)) tapeWithout.add(print);
+      const ticksWithout = indicatorTicksOf(tapeWithout)!;
+      const replayDisplayed = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, index + 2).map((bar, i) => (i === index + 1 ? { ...bar, closed: false } : bar)) as BarFrame[], AUTO, { ticks: ticksWithout });
+      expect(replayDisplayed.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBe(shownLarge);
+      // A REBUILT over the store that holds the late print includes it, once the candle's volume carries the print too
+      // (a revised candle for an older bar is itself a REBUILT); with the stale partial volume the bar would fail the share gate.
+      const partialVolume = partial.reduce((sum, print) => sum + print.size, 0);
+      session.dispatch({ bar: { ...FIXTURE.bars[index], closed: false, volume: partialVolume + late.size } as BarFrame });
+      expect(session.mutations.at(-1)).toBe("REBUILT");
+      const rebuilt = session.capture();
+      expect(rebuilt.outputs.largeBar[index]).not.toBe(shownLarge);
+      expect(rebuilt.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBeCloseTo((shownLarge as number) + (late.side === "buy" ? 1 : -1), 9);
+    } finally {
+      session.close();
+    }
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("reconnect clears the store honestly: bars the new store does not cover read null, the rest recover after the warm-up", () => {
+    const at = 150;
+    const arrivals: Arrival[] = FIXTURE.bars.map((bar, index) => ({
+      bar,
+      act:
+        index === at
+          ? (session) => {
+              session.setTicks(ticksOf(tapeOf(FIXTURE.prints.filter((print) => print.ts_exchange >= bar.ts_open), bar.ts_open)));
+            }
+          : undefined,
+    }));
+    const result = driveLive(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, arrivals, AUTO, { ticks });
+    for (let index = 0; index < at; index += 1) expect(result.outputs.large[index]).toBeNull();
+    expect(result.outputs.large[at]).toBeNull();
+    expect(result.outputs.large[at + 1]).toBeNull();
+    expect(result.outputs.large[at + 2]).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(result.markers.every((call) => call.time >= FIXTURE.bars[at].ts_open)).toBe(true);
+    noNaN(result.outputs);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("out-of-order prints: a shuffled store gives the same outputs as an in-order one", () => {
+    let state = 99;
+    const random = () => {
+      state = (Math.imul(state, 1664525) + 1013904223) >>> 0;
+      return state / 4294967296;
+    };
+    const shuffled = [...FIXTURE.prints];
+    for (let index = shuffled.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
+      const swap = Math.floor(random() * (index + 1));
+      [shuffled[index], shuffled[swap]] = [shuffled[swap], shuffled[index]];
+    }
+    const ordered = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, { ticks });
+    const unordered = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, AUTO, { ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf(shuffled, FIXTURE_T0)) });
+    expect(diffOutputs(ordered.outputs, unordered.outputs)).toEqual([]);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("time gap: the cumulative carries across missing minutes and nothing before the gap changes", () => {
+    const { bars: raw, labels } = timeGapBars({ count: 400 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 40 });
+    const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const gapTicks = ticksOf(tapeOf(bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []), bars[0].ts_open));
+    const gapped = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, FIXED, { ticks: gapTicks });
+    const prefix = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars.slice(0, labels.gapAfterIndex + 1), FIXED, { ticks: gapTicks });
+    expect(diffOutputs(gapped.outputs, prefix.outputs, 0, labels.gapAfterIndex)).toEqual([]);
+    const after = labels.firstBarAfterGapIndex;
+    expect(gapped.outputs.large[after]).toBeCloseTo((gapped.outputs.large[after - 1] as number) + (gapped.outputs.largeBar[after] as number), 9);
+    noNaN(gapped.outputs);
+  }, 60_000);
+
+  it("thin history is null: a store that only holds the server's floor-filtered prints covers no window", () => {
+    const thin = historyTapeOf(FIXTURE.prints.filter((print) => print.size >= 1), 1, FIXTURE_T0, FIXTURE_T0 + FIXTURE.bars.length * 60_000);
+    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, FIXED, { ticks: ticksOf(thin) });
+    expect(capture.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+    expect(capture.markers).toEqual([]);
+    const full = historyTapeOf(FIXTURE.prints, 0, FIXTURE_T0, FIXTURE_T0 + FIXTURE.bars.length * 60_000);
+    const served = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, FIXED, { ticks: ticksOf(full) });
+    expect(served.outputs.large.filter((value) => value !== null).length).toBe(FIXTURE.bars.length);
+  }, 60_000);
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- warm-up, empty input and NaN (protocol check 4)", () => {
+  it("auto mode is null until 200 trade frames from complete windows have been committed, then values appear at the next bar; fixed mode values from the first bar; never NaN or a fabricated zero", () => {
+    const auto = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 10), AUTO, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[0]).toBeNull();
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[1]).toBeNull();
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[2]).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(FIXTURE.printsByBar[0].length + FIXTURE.printsByBar[1].length).toBe(TIER_MIN_SIZES);
+    const fixed = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 10), FIXED, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
+    expect(fixed.outputs.large[0]).not.toBeNull();
+    noNaN(auto.outputs);
+    noNaN(fixed.outputs);
+    const shorter = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 2), AUTO, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
+    expect(shorter.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("handles an empty bar array and a store without prints", () => {
+    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [], AUTO, { ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf([], FIXTURE_T0)) });
+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(capture.outputs.large).toEqual([]);
+    const empty = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 5), FIXED, { ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf([], FIXTURE_T0)) });
+    expect(empty.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(empty.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+  });
+});

+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- equity tape share (protocol check 9, rule 5)", () => {
+  it("draws when the sided tape covers 60 percent of the candle volume and is null with the share row at 40 percent; candle sides are never read", () => {
+    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: 120, identity: DXFEED_EQUITY, startPrice: 190, driftPerBar: 0.02, noise: 0.3 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 50, identity: DXFEED_EQUITY, smallSize: 10 });
+    const taped = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const prints = taped.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []);
+    const equityTicks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, taped[0].ts_open));
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...FIXED, tierSize: 50 };
+    const sixty = taped.map((bar) => ({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume / 0.6, buy_volume: undefined, sell_volume: undefined }) as BarFrame);
+    const forty = taped.map((bar) => ({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume / 0.4, buy_volume: undefined, sell_volume: undefined }) as BarFrame);
+    const ok = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, sixty, settings, { ticks: equityTicks });
+    expect(ok.outputs.large.every((value) => value !== null)).toBe(true);
+    const low = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, forty, settings, { ticks: equityTicks });
+    expect(low.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+    expect(low.markers).toEqual([]);
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 50, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+    const readingLow = engine.onBar(forty[0], equityTicks);
+    expect(readingLow.status).toBe("shareLow");
+    expect(readingLow.share).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 9);
+  });
+
+  it("equityUnsidedBars without a tape is null throughout, whatever the candle sides say", () => {
+    const { bars } = equityUnsidedBars();

+    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+    for (const values of Object.values(capture.outputs)) expect(values.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
+    expect(capture.markers).toEqual([]);
+  });
+});

+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- anchor rollover on the fixture (rule 8, round 3 ruling 3)", () => {
+  it("the first bar of the new UTC day shows only its own contribution, the ring is empty and no marker crosses the reset", () => {
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, anchor: "day" };
+    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, settings, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });

+    expect(capture.outputs.large[first]).toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.largeBar[first] as number, 9);
+    expect(capture.outputs.small[first]).toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.smallBar[first] as number, 9);
+    expect(capture.outputs.large[first - 1]).not.toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.largeBar[first - 1] as number, 3);
+    const arrows = [...markerIndices(capture.markers, FIXTURE.bars, "arrowDown"), ...markerIndices(capture.markers, FIXTURE.bars, "arrowUp")];
+    expect(arrows.filter((index) => index >= first && index < first + 20)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(arrows.filter((index) => index >= first + 20).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  });

+# Size-Split Delta (large vs small)
+
+[Handbook](../../README.md) · [Indicator index](README.md) ·
+[Data for indicator authors](data-for-indicator-authors.md) ·
+[Setting presets](setting-presets.md)
+
+Code: `apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/` (`sizeSplitDelta.ts` is
+the registry definition, `sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts` the pure machinery, `tests/`
+the evidence). Registry id `sizeSplitDelta`, label "Size-Split Delta (large vs
+small)", category L1, own pane, `ticksWanted`. The code wins over this page.
+Brief: `pm/cycle2/brief-size-split-delta.md`.
+
+## The question it answers
+
+"Does large-print flow confirm this price extreme?" Small-print flow is shown
+as context, not as a second question.
+
+Aggressive flow is split by **trade frame size as delivered**: a print at or
+above the bar's tier threshold is "large", the rest "small". The purple line is
+the cumulative signed size of the large tier since the anchor, the blue line
+the small tier's. When price makes a new extreme and the large line makes it
+too, the large prints confirm; when it does not, they lag, and when they lag by
+enough an arrow marks the bar. Print size is not participant size (a sliced
+iceberg prints small), and the tool never infers a side it was not given.
+Joining or fading remain the trader's decisions. Descriptive only.
+
+## Calculation
+
+One bar at a time, incrementally; `onBar` never rebuilds from an array.
+
+1. **Window.** `[ts_open, ts_open + interval_ms)` over the terminal's print
+   store (`ctx.ticks`), located with `firstPrintAtOrAfter`; a print with a
+   non-finite or non-positive size or price is skipped.
+2. **Tier threshold, read before write.** On a bar's first dispatch, after the
+   previous bar has committed, the bar stores `tierAtOpen`: in `auto` mode the
+   nearest-rank 98th percentile (index `ceil(0.98 n) - 1` of the ascending
+   sort) of the size ring, `null` while the ring holds fewer than 200 sizes;
+   in `fixed` mode the `tierSize` setting. Every revision and the commit of the
+   bar use that value. "Top 2%" is therefore an estimate from preceding trade
+   frames, ties included: a bar can classify more than 2 percent of its prints
+   large, and with fewer than 4,096 sizes the quantile can land on a small
+   print, which then classifies large.
+3. **Size ring.** The last 4,096 sizes of committed windows with coverage
+   `"all"`, both tiers, kept in arrival order and in sorted order in two typed
+   arrays (64 KB); an insert or eviction moves at most 4,096 doubles and nothing
+   is ever re-sorted. With no threshold nothing is classified, but valid sizes
+   still train the ring.
+4. **Contribution.** Per print, `size >= tierAtOpen` is large (the boundary
+   print included); buys positive, sells negative; `largeBar` and `smallBar`
+   are the bar's two sums, `tapeVolume` the sum of all valid sizes.
+5. **Tape share, fail closed.** `share = tapeVolume / bar.volume` when the
+   candle volume is finite and positive, else unknown. The bar is eligible when
+   the share is known and inside `[minTapeShare, 1.25]`. Zero or missing candle
+   volume with prints, no prints with positive volume (share 0), no prints and
+   zero volume (unknown) and a share above 1.25 (a mismatch between tape and
+   candle) are all ineligible; an ineligible bar's flow is `null`, never zero.
+6. **Commit, keyed to the bar close, tail-only.** A bar commits at the first of
+   (a) its first dispatch with `closed: true`, (b) the first dispatch of a
+   later bar while it is uncommitted. On (a) the window is rescanned once from
+   the store, flow and eligibility are frozen, the sizes enter the ring (if the
+   window was `"all"`), and the cumulatives advance if the bar is valid. On (b)
+   the runtime cannot republish the previous bar, so the commit uses the last
+   revision's values with no rescan: the committed history equals what was
+   displayed, and **a print that arrived after the last revision and before
+   the next bar is excluded** from that bar's flow and from the next threshold
+   until a rebuild replays everything from the store. After commit nothing
+   about the bar changes on a later dispatch of the same bar.
+7. **Forming bar.** Every revision rescans the forming window from scratch and
+   overwrites the tail; the forming output is the frozen cumulative under the
+   current anchor plus the forming contribution, drawn provisional (50 percent
+   alpha, status `Forming`) until the bar commits, by its close or by
+   supersession (UX pass, finding 5).
+8. **Anchor and reset.** `day` (default) and `week` are UTC-aligned windows
+   (`floor(ts / period) · period`, the session kernel's convention: the week
+   window starts on the epoch's weekday, a Thursday); `tape` cumulates from the
+   first covered bar. On the first dispatch inside a new window, forming
+   included, the cumulative base is zero, the marker ring is empty and the
+   table's state and condition rows are cleared.
+9. **Gaps.** A bar without a value (coverage not `"all"`, no threshold,
+   ineligible share) adds nothing; both lines are `null` on it; the cumulative
+   resumes unchanged at the next valid bar, so a sum after a gap omits the gap's
+   flow. The marker ring empties and the status row names the gap until `K`
+   valid bars have followed.
+10. **Markers**, a function of committed state, provisional on the forming bar
+    and final at the close. The marker ring holds exactly `K` (default 20)
+    consecutive valid committed bars immediately before the bar; a gap or an
+    anchor reset empties it and nothing fires until it is full again. `mabsL`
+    and `mabsS` are the means of `|largeBar|` and `|smallBar|` over the ring
+    (the bar itself excluded). With `h` the ring bar with the highest close
+    (latest if tied) and `l` the lowest: a strict new high (`close > close_h`)
+    **confirms** when `large > large_h` and **lags** otherwise; the bearish
+    divergence condition holds when `large <= large_h - m · mabsL` (`m`
+    default 2); the mirror for a strict new low. The **conflict** holds when
+    the ring-plus-bar sums of the two tiers have opposite signs and each clears
+    `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution (`q` default
+    2). **Both markers fire on the onset only** (PM amendment to the brief's
+    rule 11): the arrow or circle is drawn when the condition holds at the bar
+    and did not hold, in that direction, at the previous valid bar, the
+    condition being re-evaluated every bar as written. A trend where every bar
+    is a new extreme with a persistently lagging large tier marks once and
+    again only after the condition has cleared. At most two markers on a bar.
+11. **No future data.** The window end is the cutoff, so a committed bar
+    ignores prints at or after it even when the store already holds them;
+    thresholds come from committed bars only. Truncating the series changes no
+    earlier output or committed marker (tested at every cut of the fixture).
+12. **Fail closed.** Any non-finite intermediate yields `null`; nothing ever
+    emits `NaN`.
+
+## Data and where the tool works
+
+Only the print store is read for flow: `ctx.ticks` (`prints`, `liveSince`,
+`coverageOf`). From the bar: `ts_open`, `interval_ms`, `closed`, `close`,
+`volume`. Never `barSides`, `buy_volume`, `ctx.trades` or
+`ctx.tradeAvailability`: tick-store coverage is the only gate on tick-derived
+values. Sizes are provider units (coins on Binance, contracts on a future,
+shares on an equity) and are never added across providers.
+
+| Path | Tape | Indicator |
+|---|---|---|
+| Runtime WebSocket, Binance or dxFeed | live prints complete from connect; the pre-connect pool and `aggressive_history` are floor-filtered (`"thin"`) | lines begin at the first bar whose whole window is `"all"`; earlier bars `null`, status `Needs live tape · thinned` |
+| Browser-direct | live `trade` stream complete from connect; the `aggTrades` bootstrap is `"thin"` | as above; the bootstrap never enters a value |
+| REST candle transport | no trade frames, coverage `"none"`, no store | every output `null`, no markers, status `Needs live tape · no feed` |
+| Historical Mode | no store | unavailable: "Needs the live tape: the archive keeps only the largest prints of each minute." |
+| Equities (dxFeed) | only prints with an aggressor side reach the browser | the tape share gate: on AAPL/TSLA the sided share is often under 50 percent and the bar reads `null` with `Tape 38% · below 50%` |
+
+The store drops live prints past 250,000 (`liveDroppedThrough`): at about
+1,600 trade frames a minute on BTC-USDT the complete stretch is roughly 2.6
+hours, and windows that reached the drop read `"thin"`. The cumulative starts
+at the later of the anchor and the covered start; the line's own start shows
+it. The first forming bar after connect (or after a reconnect) is not covered
+either, because its window began before the live recording: that is its own
+state, `tapeStarting`, shown as `Tape live · next bar` (coverage `"none"`
+while the store exists, has held at least one print and `liveSince` falls
+inside the window); `Needs live tape · no feed` is kept for no store at all or
+a store that has never held a print (UX pass, finding 2). The "never held a
+print" clause is the build's addition: the REST-only path absorbs an empty
+trade list on every snapshot, which sets `liveSince` without any feed, and
+without the clause a REST-only chart would read `Tape live · next bar` for
+ever.
+
+Aggregates: the 10,000-row BTC-USDT fixture holds Binance aggregate trades
+(one taker order at one price), so a tier estimated on it is higher than one
+estimated on individual prints (p98 0.106 BTC, p99 0.198, max 2.0; the top 2
+percent carry 52.5 percent of its volume). The tier is "per trade frame as
+delivered"; fixture numbers do not transfer between transports.
+
+## Default view
+
+Own pane with a dotted zero line: `large` purple `#8672b3` width 2, `small`
+blue `#2563eb` width 1, no fill; both at 50 percent alpha until the bar
+commits. Divergence arrows (`arrowDown` above the bar for a bearish
+divergence, `arrowUp` below for a bullish one, size 2) in the large line's
+colour; conflict circles (`inBar`, size 1, same colour) are off by default.
+Table, top right, three rows of at most 30 characters, values with three
+significant digits and `k`/`M` suffixes; row (a) always in the solid large
+colour, since it describes the current reading:
+
+| Row | Content |
+|---|---|
+| (a) state | `L +12.4 · S −8.1 · confirms`, `… · lags` or `… · no extreme`; `Need 20 valid bars` until the ring is full; `—` while the bar has no value. If a row would exceed 30 characters the values are dropped: `Large confirms`, `Large lags`, `No extreme`. |
+| (b) condition | the newest condition in this anchor window whose Visibility toggle is on, with its time (UTC on Binance, New York on dxFeed) and value: `Divergence ↓ 12:34 · −8.12k` (depth `large − large_h`) or `Conflict 12:34 · +4.21k/−3.12k` (the two sums; over 30 characters, `Conflict 12:34`); divergence wins when both fire on one bar; `—` when none, or when the toggle of the only condition is off; cleared on an anchor reset. |
+| (c) status | first match wins: `Needs live tape · no feed`, `Tape live · next bar`, `Needs live tape · thinned`, `Learning sizes · 120 of 200`, `Tape share unknown`, `Tape 160% · mismatch`, `Tape 38% · below 50%`, `Tape gap · 3 bars`, `Forming`, ready `≥ 0.106 BTC · tape 100%`. |
+
+## Controls
+
+Inputs (primary), each with a one-line hint: `tierMode` "Large tier"
+(Largest 2% of prints / Fixed size), `tierSize` "Large from (BTC)" (shown for
+a fixed tier; the label names the instrument's unit, the hint suggests 0.5
+BTC, 20 contracts or 2,000 shares; the stored default is 0.5), `anchor`
+"Anchor" (Day (UTC) / Week (UTC) / Tape start), and a closing paragraph:
+"Needs the live tape; lines start one bar after connect. Arrow: price made a
+new high or low that the large prints did not. Circle: large and small prints
+pulling opposite ways." Advanced: `K` "Extreme window (bars)" 10..60 step 5,
+`m` "Divergence depth (×)" 1..4 step 0.5, `q` "Conflict strength (×)" 1..4
+step 0.5, `minTapeShare` "Tape share floor (0–1)" 0.2..1 step 0.05.
+Visibility: `showSmall` "Show small tier (blue)" (on), `showDivergence` (on),
+`showConflict` (off), `showBarLines` "Show per-bar lines" (off; the per-bar
+contributions as two grey lines, the harness's seams). Every setting replays,
+the Visibility toggles included (a hidden line reads `null`, which `recolor`
+cannot do), so a toggle costs a replay of the retained bars, well under a
+second; there are no colour pickers.
+
+## Alert conditions
+
+| Name | Rule | Fires on | On the mechanism fixture |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| Large-tier divergence (arrow) | strict new `K`-window close extreme with `large` on the wrong side of `large_h` (or `large_l`) by at least `m · mabs`; onset only | the first bar the condition holds after it did not, provisional while forming, final at its close | 7 of 300 bars at the default day anchor (8 planted; one falls inside the 20 bars after the UTC-midnight reset), 8 with the tape anchor; every planted bar is an onset |
+| Tier conflict (circle, off by default) | the ring-plus-bar sums of the two tiers oppose each other, each at `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution; onset only | the first bar the condition holds after it did not | 17 of 300 bars with the tape anchor |
+
+## Warm-up
+
+Auto mode: 200 trade frames from complete windows, then the next bar open; on
+the fixture (100 prints a bar) the first value is bar 2 (`Learning sizes · 0
+of 200`, then `100 of 200`). Fixed mode values from the first covered bar.
+Markers and row (a) need `K` valid committed bars before the bar (`Need 20
+valid bars`). The first minute after connect reads `Tape live · next bar`.
+
+## Replay and Historical Mode
+
+Live and replay read the same store, so a replay reproduces the live values
+for every bar whose window the store covers; the forming bar is provisional
+and excluded. The documented differences are a reconnect (a new store covers
+fewer windows, which read `null`) and a rebuild after a late print (a print
+that arrived after a bar's last revision and before its supersession enters
+only when a `REBUILT` or `setTicks` replay rescans the bar from the store,
+and only if the candle's volume carries it too, else the share gate refuses
+the bar). The runtime trims its outputs and markers on the rollover but does
+not notify indicator state, so the indicator bounds its own: two running
+totals, the 4,096-size ring, the `K`-ring and a ring of 10,080 committed bars
+(`maxReplayBars`); the size ring may hold sizes from bars the chart has
+discarded, so a replay over the retained bars can give the earliest retained
+bars a different threshold. Equivalence is tested from the first retained bar
+whose anchor start and whose preceding 4,096 committed sizes both lie inside
+the retained series (8,726 bars compared equal on the 10,380-bar rollover
+fixture). Historical Mode is unavailable with the reason above; a replay with
+no store is `null` everywhere.
+
+## Baseline comparison
+
+Two comparisons, both from `tests/sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts`, truth
+from the generators' hidden state, every arrow also checked against the
+independent reference implementation of the rule
+(`validation/sizeSplitReference.ts`).
+
+Mechanism fixture `sizeSplitFixture()` (300 bars, seed 20261102, 2 large
+prints of 1.0 BTC and 98 small in [0.01, 0.049) a bar, 8 planted divergences at
+bars 45 + 30 j where the large prints oppose a forced strict new extreme), tape
+anchor:
+
+| Configuration | planted | hits on the planted bar | off-label arrows | conflict onsets |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| auto (top 2%) | 8 | 8 | 0 | 17 (equal to the reference) |
+| fixed 0.5 BTC | 8 | 8 | 0 | |
+| CVD from `barSides`, same rule | 8 | 8 | 5 | |
+
+Regime fixtures `sizeSplitRegimeBars({ seed, priceFollows })`, 600 bars, seeds
+1..10, the common evaluation mask being the bars where both configurations have
+a value and a full ring (5,780 bars per regime over the ten seeds); `"large"`
+plants 10 events per seed, `"small"` and `"none"` plant nothing so every arrow
+there is off-label:
+
+| regime | configuration | eligible | planted | hits | off-label arrows |
+|---|---|---|---|---|---|
+| large | auto | 5,780 | 100 | 100 | 0 |
+| large | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 100 | 100 | 0 |
+| large | CVD | 5,780 | 100 | 80 | 171 |
+| small | auto | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 488 |
+| small | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 490 |
+| small | CVD | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 37 |
+| none | auto | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 256 |
+| none | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 256 |
+| none | CVD | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 180 |
+
+(Arrows are onsets; the held condition would count 703/709/37 on `"small"`
+and 290/293/186 on `"none"`.) Acceptance holds: auto matches fixed on
+`"large"` (100 of 100) with no more off-label arrows on `"small"` (488 against
+490) and `"none"` (256 against 256), and beats the candle delta on its own
+regime (100 hits against 80, 0 off-label against 171). What the split shows that candles hide: on the
+`"large"` regime the candle delta misses one planted event in five and draws
+171 arrows the rule does not support, because the small prints' opposite flow
+is folded into one number. The off-label counts on `"small"` are the rule
+reading correctly a regime built so that price follows the small tier: the
+large tier lags most extremes there by construction.
+
+`whaleVersusCrowd()` in fixed mode with `tierSize` 1.0: the single-large-print
+tape reads `largeBar +50, smallBar 0`, the hundred-print tape `0, +50`, the
+candle is identical and the candle delta reads +50 on both. The 10,000
+aggregate trades: five full minutes of 1,199 to 2,345 aggregates each, the auto
+tier after each commit equal to the test's nearest-rank p98 of the same ring
+(0.0963 to 0.106 BTC), tape share 1 within 1e-9, tier totals equal to the signed
+sum.
+
+## Marker budget
+
+Mechanism fixture at default settings: 7 markers over 300 bars (23 per 1,000),
+at most one on a bar; with conflict circles on, 24 over 300. The protocol's
+busy instrument (`trendBars(1000)` with `busyTapeFor` at 60 prints a bar)
+measures 59 onset arrows per 1,000 bars at defaults (66 before the onset
+rule), above the 40 per 1,000 ceiling for a signal tool. That fixture closes
+above its previous 20 closes on 980 of 980 bars, so the ring high is almost
+always the previous bar and the divergence condition reduces to
+`largeBar <= -m · mabsL` for the bar itself: a random large tier meets it on
+about one bar in seventeen and rarely on two bars running, so the onset rule
+removes few of them. The number is reported, not hidden; the PM decides.
+
+## Performance and bounded state
+
+Frozen method (`probeReplayPooled`, three warm-ups, five timed runs, `onBar`
+plus `readOutput`) on 10,080 trend bars with 20 prints each in one store of
+201,600 prints: pooled p99 0.038 ms (p50 0.011); real activation p99 0.035 ms,
+wall 150 ms; forming bar in fixed mode, 3,000 revisions over 1,000 prints, p99
+0.047 ms; rollover of 1,000 extra bars: every output key 10,080 long, no stale
+marker, 412 bytes per extra bar with gc exposed (the harness floor is about
+370). Measured on the final build with the machine otherwise idle; the test
+asserts pooled p99 under 0.5 ms and forming p99 at or under 0.1 ms. State: a ring of 10,080 committed bars of seven fields (under 1 MB), two
+32 KB typed arrays, the `K`-ring, two totals, and on the forming bar only its
+last revision's sizes (at most 4,096).
+
+## Limitations
+
+Print size is not participant size; the tier follows the delivered trade
+frame. Lines start at the tape start (about 2.6 hours back on BTC-USDT at
+most) and never in Historical Mode or on the REST transport. A bar superseded
+without a close keeps its last displayed values until a rebuild. Equities show
+sided flow only and are `null` under the share gate on most bars.
+Cumulative-since-anchor is a presentation choice with no claimed benefit over
+a per-bar view; a per-bar histogram display mode is v2. The `week` anchor is
+epoch-aligned (Thursday 00:00 UTC). No hover tooltip. Descriptive; no forward
+claim.
+
+## Tests
+
+`tests/sizeSplitDeltaMath.test.ts` (the hand-computed three-bar fixture, the
+size ring against the reference quantile, the window scan, the share cases,
+both commit paths, the anchor reset, gaps, the marker rule, formatting),
+`tests/sizeSplitDelta.test.ts` (identity, policy, schema, table copy, markers
+through a hand-built ctx), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaValidation.test.ts` (protocol
+checks 1 to 4 and 9, the forming prefix, the late-print supersession, the
+reconnect, the retention equivalence), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts`
+(checks 5 and 7, the acceptance table, whale versus crowd, the aggregate
+fixture), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaPerf.test.ts` (check 6). Run with
+`NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc npx vitest run src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta`.
+

+both with Fixed size selected, `cream-dialog-visibility`). Each shot comes in a
+`-zoomed-out` and a `-zoomed-in` pair taken from the same reset view.

+    const priceFix = prefix.length > 0 ? fixture.bars[0].open - prefix[prefix.length - 1].close : 0;
+    for (const bar of prefix) { bar.open += priceFix; bar.high += priceFix; bar.low += priceFix; bar.close += priceFix; }
+    const history = fixture.bars.slice(0, -1).map((bar) => shiftBar(bar, tsShift, 0));
+    closedBars = [...prefix, ...history];
+    const pooledBars = poolBars === null ? POOL_PRINTS / fixture.printsByBar[0].length : poolBars;
+    const firstPoolBar = Math.max(0, fixtureBars - 1 - pooledBars);
+    const poolPrints = [];
+    for (let i = firstPoolBar; i < fixtureBars - 1; i += 1) for (const print of fixture.printsByBar[i]) poolPrints.push(shiftPrint(print, tsShift, 0));
+    if (pooledBars > 0) pool = { aggressive_prints: poolPrints.slice(-POOL_PRINTS), aggressive_prints_floor: 0, aggressive_prints_from: history[firstPoolBar].ts_open };
+    const last = fixture.bars[fixtureBars - 1];
+    forming = { type: "bar", ...identity, interval_ms: INTERVAL, ts_open: openTime, open: last.open, high: last.open, low: last.open, close: last.open, volume: 0, buy_volume: 0, sell_volume: 0, trade_count: 0, closed: false };
+    pending = fixture.printsByBar[fixtureBars - 1].map((print) => ({ offset: print.ts_exchange - last.ts_open, print: shiftPrint(print, tsShift, 0) }));
+  } else {
+    const bars = syntheticBars(barCount, openTime, 65_000);
+    // Every snapshot bar must be closed (the parser refuses a forming bar there), so the
+    // forming bar is a separate live `bar` frame for the current minute, revised every second.
+    closedBars = bars.slice(0, -1);
+    forming = { ...bars[bars.length - 1], ts_open: openTime, closed: false };
+  }
+
+  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
+    res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, fixture: true, tape: withTape, symbol: identity.symbol, bars: closedBars.length + 1 }));
+  });
+  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: "/ws" });
+  const send = (socket, frame) => { if (socket.readyState === 1) socket.send(JSON.stringify(frame)); };
+  const broadcast = (frame) => { for (const socket of wss.clients) send(socket, frame); };
+  const initialize = (socket) => {
+    send(socket, { type: "selection", ...identity });
+    send(socket, { type: "snapshot", ...identity, bars: closedBars, trades: sent, markers: [], ...(pool ?? {}) });
+    send(socket, { type: "health", ...identity, recoveryState: "READY", stale: false });
+    send(socket, { type: "book", ...identity, status: "READY", bids: [{ price: forming.close - 0.5, size: 3.2 }], asks: [{ price: forming.close + 0.5, size: 2.1 }], lastUpdateId: 1, stale: false });
+    send(socket, { ...forming });
+  };
+  wss.on("connection", (socket) => {
+    socket.on("message", (wire) => {
+      try { if (JSON.parse(wire.toString()).type === "select") initialize(socket); } catch { /* not JSON: ignore */ }
+    });
+    initialize(socket);
+  });
+
+  /** The minute whose candle volume is inflated so the share gate refuses it (1 = the first live minute). */
+  let minuteIndex = 1;
+  /** Fold one print into the forming bar's candle. */
+  const fold = (print) => {
+    if (forming.volume === 0) { forming.open = print.price; forming.high = print.price; forming.low = print.price; }
+    forming.close = print.price;
+    forming.high = Math.max(forming.high, print.price);
+    forming.low = Math.min(forming.low, print.price);
+    forming.volume += lowShareMinute === minuteIndex ? print.size * 2.6 : print.size;
+    if (print.side === "buy") forming.buy_volume += print.size; else forming.sell_volume += print.size;
+    forming.trade_count += 1;
+  };
+
+  /** After the fixture's last minute: the next minute replays fixture bars 200..299 with prices continuing from the last close. */
+  const nextMinute = () => {
+    const sourceIndex = fixture.bars.length - 100 + (cycle % 100);
+    cycle += 1;
+    const source = fixture.bars[sourceIndex];
+    const priceShift = Math.round((forming.close - source.open) * 100) / 100;
+    const tsShift = forming.ts_open + INTERVAL - source.ts_open;
+    forming = { type: "bar", ...identity, interval_ms: INTERVAL, ts_open: forming.ts_open + INTERVAL, open: forming.close, high: forming.close, low: forming.close, close: forming.close, volume: 0, buy_volume: 0, sell_volume: 0, trade_count: 0, closed: false };
+    pending = fixture.printsByBar[sourceIndex].map((print) => ({ offset: print.ts_exchange - source.ts_open, print: shiftPrint(print, tsShift, priceShift, `-c${cycle}`) }));
+    sent = [];
+    minuteIndex += 1;
+  };
+
+  setInterval(() => {
+    if (!withTape) {
+      forming.close = Math.round((forming.close + (random() - 0.5) * 12) * 10) / 10;
+      forming.high = Math.max(forming.high, forming.close);
+      forming.low = Math.min(forming.low, forming.close);
+      broadcast({ ...forming });
+      return;
+    }
+    const elapsed = Date.now() - forming.ts_open;
+    while (pending.length > 0 && pending[0].offset <= Math.min(elapsed, INTERVAL - 1)) {
+      const { print } = pending.shift();
+      fold(print);
+      sent.push(print);
+      broadcast(print);
+    }
+    if (elapsed >= INTERVAL) {
+      const closed = { ...forming, closed: true };
+      broadcast(closed);
+      closedBars.push(closed);
+      // A browser connecting later gets this minute's prints with the pool, as the runtime's pool would hold them.
+      if (pool !== null) pool.aggressive_prints = [...pool.aggressive_prints, ...sent].slice(-POOL_PRINTS);
+      else if (sent.length > 0) pool = { aggressive_prints: [...sent], aggressive_prints_floor: 0, aggressive_prints_from: forming.ts_open };
+      nextMinute();
+    }
+    broadcast({ ...forming });
+  }, 1000);
+
+  server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1", () => {
+    console.log(`fixture runtime on http://127.0.0.1:${port} (ws at /ws), ${closedBars.length + 1} synthetic 1m bars for ${identity.symbol}${withTape ? `, tape: ${pool ? `${pool.aggressive_prints.length} pooled prints from ${new Date(pool.aggressive_prints_from).toISOString()}` : "no pooled history"} plus live trade frames${lowShareMinute === null ? "" : `, low share on live minute ${lowShareMinute}`}` : ""}`);
+  });
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exit(1); });
