  /** Committed bars, oldest first, at most `RECORD_RING_CAPACITY`. */
  readonly committed: readonly CommittedBar[];
  readonly lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null;
  readonly lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null;
}

export function createSizeSplitEngine(settings: SizeSplitMathSettings): SizeSplitEngine {
  const sizeRing = createSizeRing();
  const committed: CommittedBar[] = [];
  const markerRing: MarkerRingEntry[] = [];
  const base = { large: 0, small: 0 };
  let tail: SizeSplitRecord | null = null;
  let anchorStart: number | null = null;
  let anchorSeen = false;
  let previousConflict = false;
  let previousDivergence: DivergenceDirection | null = null;
  let lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
  let lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
  /** Whether a valid bar has committed in this anchor window (a gap needs a valid bar before it). */
  let validEver = false;
  /** Invalid committed bars in the most recent gap, and valid bars committed since it closed. */
  let gapBars = 0;
  let validSinceGap = 0;
  let lastReading: SizeSplitReading | null = null;

  function tierFor(): number | null {
    if (settings.tierMode === "fixed") return isFinitePositive(settings.tierSize) ? settings.tierSize : null;
    return sizeRing.threshold();
  }

  function divergenceOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
    return evaluation.divergence === null ? null : { kind: "divergence", direction: evaluation.divergence, tsOpen, values: [evaluation.depth ?? 0] };
  }

  function conflictOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
    return evaluation.conflictOnset && evaluation.conflictSums !== null ? { kind: "conflict", direction: null, tsOpen, values: evaluation.conflictSums } : null;
  }

  function readingOf(record: SizeSplitRecord): SizeSplitReading {
    const large = record.valid ? base.large + (record.largeBar as number) : null;
    const small = record.valid ? base.small + (record.smallBar as number) : null;
    const evaluation = record.valid
      ? evaluateMarkers(markerRing, { close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: large as number }, { conflict: previousConflict, divergence: previousDivergence }, settings)
      : NO_EVALUATION;
    const ownDivergence = record.valid ? divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
    const ownConflict = record.valid ? conflictOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
    return {
      tsOpen: record.tsOpen,
      large,
      small,
      largeBar: record.valid ? record.largeBar : null,
      smallBar: record.valid ? record.smallBar : null,
      share: record.share,
      tier: record.tierAtOpen,
      sizesLearned: sizeRing.count,
      status: statusOf(record, gapBars, validSinceGap, settings.K),
      gapBars,
      committed: record.committed,
      confirmed: record.confirmed,
      valid: record.valid,
      ringLength: markerRing.length,
      state: evaluation.state,
      divergence: evaluation.divergence,
      divergenceHeld: evaluation.divergenceHeld,
      depth: evaluation.depth,
      conflictHeld: evaluation.conflictHeld,
      conflictOnset: evaluation.conflictOnset,
      conflictSums: evaluation.conflictSums,
      lastDivergence: ownDivergence ?? lastDivergence,
      lastConflict: ownConflict ?? lastConflict,
    };
  }

  function fill(record: SizeSplitRecord, bar: BarFrame, ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined): void {
    const scan = scanWindow(ticks, record.tsOpen, record.windowEnd, record.tierAtOpen);
    record.close = bar.close;
    record.volume = bar.volume;
    record.coverage = scan.coverage;
    record.tapeStarting = scan.coverage === "none" && ticks !== undefined && ticks.prints.length > 0 && ticks.liveSince !== null && ticks.liveSince < record.windowEnd;
    record.largeBar = scan.largeBar;
    record.smallBar = scan.smallBar;
    record.tapeVolume = scan.tapeVolume;
    record.printCount = scan.printCount;
    record.sizes = scan.sizes;
    record.share = scan.coverage === "all" ? tapeShareOf(scan.tapeVolume, bar.volume) : null;
    record.eligible = shareEligible(record.share, settings.minTapeShare);
    record.valid = scan.coverage === "all" && record.tierAtOpen !== null && scan.largeBar !== null && scan.smallBar !== null && record.eligible;
  }

  /** Steps 2 to 4 of rule 6: freeze flow and eligibility, train the ring, advance the cumulatives (or record the gap). */
  function commit(record: SizeSplitRecord, confirmed: boolean): void {
    record.confirmed = confirmed;
    record.committed = true;
    const reading = readingOf(record);
    record.reading = reading;
    if (record.coverage === "all" && record.sizes !== null) sizeRing.push(record.sizes);
    if (record.valid) {
      base.large += record.largeBar as number;
      base.small += record.smallBar as number;
      markerRing.push({ close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: base.large });
      if (markerRing.length > settings.K) markerRing.splice(0, markerRing.length - settings.K);
      previousConflict = reading.conflictHeld;
      previousDivergence = reading.divergenceHeld;
      const ownDivergence = divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
      const ownConflict = conflictOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
      if (ownDivergence !== null) lastDivergence = ownDivergence;
      if (ownConflict !== null) lastConflict = ownConflict;
      validEver = true;
      validSinceGap += 1;
    } else {
      markerRing.length = 0;
      previousConflict = false;
      previousDivergence = null;
      if (validEver) {
        gapBars = validSinceGap > 0 ? 1 : gapBars + 1;
        validSinceGap = 0;
      }
    }
    record.sizes = null;
    committed.push({ tsOpen: record.tsOpen, tierAtOpen: record.tierAtOpen, largeBar: record.largeBar, smallBar: record.smallBar, share: record.share, valid: record.valid, confirmed });
    if (committed.length > RECORD_RING_CAPACITY) committed.splice(0, committed.length - RECORD_RING_CAPACITY);
  }

  /** Step 5 of rule 6: the new record, with the anchor reset applied first when the bar opens a new window (rule 8). */
  function open(bar: BarFrame): SizeSplitRecord {
    const start = anchorStartFor(bar.ts_open, settings.anchor);
    if (!anchorSeen || start !== anchorStart) {
      if (anchorSeen) {
        base.large = 0;
        base.small = 0;
        markerRing.length = 0;
        previousConflict = false;
        previousDivergence = null;
        lastDivergence = null;
        lastConflict = null;
        validEver = false;
        gapBars = 0;
        validSinceGap = 0;
      }
      anchorStart = start;
      anchorSeen = true;
    }
    return {
      tsOpen: bar.ts_open,
      windowEnd: bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms,
      close: bar.close,
      volume: bar.volume,
      tierAtOpen: tierFor(),
      coverage: "noStore",
      tapeStarting: false,
      largeBar: null,
      smallBar: null,
      tapeVolume: 0,
      printCount: 0,
      share: null,
      eligible: false,
      valid: false,
      sizes: null,
      committed: false,
      confirmed: false,
      reading: null,
    };
  }

  return {
    onBar(bar, ticks) {
      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open < tail.tsOpen) return lastReading ?? readingOf(tail);
      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open > tail.tsOpen) {
        if (!tail.committed) commit(tail, false);
        tail = null;
      }
      if (tail === null) tail = open(bar);
      if (tail.committed) {
        lastReading = tail.reading ?? readingOf(tail);
        return lastReading;
      }
      fill(tail, bar, ticks);
      if (bar.closed) {
        commit(tail, true);
        lastReading = tail.reading as SizeSplitReading;
        return lastReading;
      }
      lastReading = readingOf(tail);
      return lastReading;
    },
    get tail() {
      return tail;
    },
    sizeRing,
    markerRing,
    base,
    get anchorStart() {
      return anchorStart;
    },
    committed,
    get lastDivergence() {
      return lastDivergence;
    },
    get lastConflict() {
      return lastConflict;
    },
  };
}

/* Formatting shared by the table and the doc examples (amendment 7). */

const MINUS = "−";

/** Three significant digits with `k`/`M` suffixes and an explicit sign; zero prints as `0`. */
export function formatSigned(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "—";
  if (value === 0) return "0";
  const sign = value < 0 ? MINUS : "+";
  return `${sign}${formatMagnitude(Math.abs(value))}`;
}

export function formatMagnitude(value: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return "—";
  if (value >= 1e6) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e6)}M`;
  if (value >= 1e3) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e3)}k`;
  return trimDigits(value);
}

function trimDigits(value: number): string {
  if (value === 0) return "0";
  const text = value.toPrecision(3);
  if (text.includes("e")) return Number(text).toString();
  return text.includes(".") ? text.replace(/\.?0+$/, "") : text;
}

/** `HH:MM` in the instrument's clock: New York for dxFeed and Databento, UTC for Binance. */
export function formatClock(tsOpen: number, provider: BarFrame["provider"]): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(tsOpen)) return "—";
  const minuteOfDay = provider === "DXFEED" || provider === "DATABENTO" ? nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tsOpen) : Math.floor((tsOpen % DAY_MS) / 60_000);
  const hours = Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60);
  const minutes = minuteOfDay % 60;
  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/**
 * Size-Split Delta (large vs small): the cumulative aggressive delta of the
 * prints at or above a size tier and of the rest, since the anchor, so a
 * trader sees whether the large prints made the new price extreme with price
 * or lagged it. Own pane, two lines, a three-row table, a divergence arrow
 * (primary) and an optional conflict circle. Tape only: reads `ctx.ticks`
 * and never the candle sides; honest nulls wherever the store does not cover the bar.
 */

export const SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR = "#8672b3";
export const SIZE_SPLIT_SMALL_COLOR = "#2563eb";
export const SIZE_SPLIT_BAR_LINE_COLOR = "#787b86";
export const SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR = "#d1d4dc";
export const SIZE_SPLIT_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA = 0.5;
export const SIZE_SPLIT_UNAVAILABLE = "—";
export const SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS = 30;

export interface SizeSplitDeltaSettings {
  tierMode: TierMode;
  tierSize: number;
  anchor: SizeSplitAnchor;
  K: number;
  m: number;
  q: number;
  minTapeShare: number;
  showSmall: boolean;
  showDivergence: boolean;
  showConflict: boolean;
  showBarLines: boolean;
}

export interface SizeSplitDeltaState {
  engine: SizeSplitEngine;
  table: TableHandle;
  /** The settings the state was created with; `readOutput` reads the Visibility toggles from here. */
  settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings;
  reading: SizeSplitReading | null;
  provider: BarFrame["provider"] | null;
  symbol: string | null;
  assetClass: FrameIdentity["assetClass"] | null;
}

export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = Object.freeze({
  tierMode: "auto",
  tierSize: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_TIER_SIZE,
  anchor: "day",
  K: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_K,
  m: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_M,
  q: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_Q,
  minTapeShare: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_MIN_TAPE_SHARE,
  showSmall: true,
  showDivergence: true,
  showConflict: false,
  showBarLines: false,
});

/** `#rgb` or `#rrggbb` with an alpha; any other colour string is returned unchanged. */
export function withAlpha(color: string, alpha: number): string {
  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(color.trim());
  if (hex === null) return color;
  let digits = hex[1];
  if (digits.length === 3) digits = digits.split("").map((d) => d + d).join("");
  const r = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(0, 2), 16);
  const g = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(2, 4), 16);
  const b = Number.parseInt(digits.slice(4, 6), 16);
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${alpha})`;
}

const LARGE_PROVISIONAL = withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR, SIZE_SPLIT_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA);
const SMALL_PROVISIONAL = withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_SMALL_COLOR, SIZE_SPLIT_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA);
const BAR_LINE_PROVISIONAL = withAlpha(SIZE_SPLIT_BAR_LINE_COLOR, SIZE_SPLIT_PROVISIONAL_ALPHA);

/** Provisional until the bar commits (by a close or a supersession), not until it is confirmed (UX finding 5). */
function provisional(reading: SizeSplitReading | null): boolean {
  return reading !== null && !reading.committed;
}

export function largeLineColor(reading: SizeSplitReading | null): string {
  return provisional(reading) ? LARGE_PROVISIONAL : SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR;
}

export function smallLineColor(reading: SizeSplitReading | null): string {
  return provisional(reading) ? SMALL_PROVISIONAL : SIZE_SPLIT_SMALL_COLOR;
}

function barLineColor(reading: SizeSplitReading | null): string {
  return provisional(reading) ? BAR_LINE_PROVISIONAL : SIZE_SPLIT_BAR_LINE_COLOR;
}

/** The size unit named in the ready status: the coin on Binance, contracts on a future, shares on an equity. */
export function sizeUnitOf(state: Pick<SizeSplitDeltaState, "provider" | "symbol" | "assetClass">): string {
  if (state.provider === "BINANCE") {
    const symbol = state.symbol ?? "";
    const dash = symbol.indexOf("-");
    return dash > 0 ? symbol.slice(0, dash) : symbol || "units";
  }
  if (state.assetClass === "FUTURE") return "contracts";
  if (state.assetClass === "EQUITY") return "shares";
  return "units";
}

function percent(share: number): string {
  return `${Math.round(share * 100)}%`;
}

/** Rule 12 and amendment 5: the status row, first match wins; each string stays within 30 characters. */
export function statusText(reading: SizeSplitReading | null, unit: string, minTapeShare: number): string {
  if (reading === null) return "Needs live tape · no feed";
  switch (reading.status) {
    case "noFeed":
      return "Needs live tape · no feed";
    case "tapeStarting":
      return "Tape live · next bar";
    case "thinned":
      return "Needs live tape · thinned";
    case "learning":
      return `Learning sizes · ${reading.sizesLearned} of ${TIER_MIN_SIZES}`;
    case "shareUnknown":
      return "Tape share unknown";
    case "shareMismatch":
      return `Tape ${percent(reading.share ?? MAX_TAPE_SHARE)} · mismatch`;
    case "shareLow":
      return `Tape ${percent(reading.share ?? 0)} · below ${percent(minTapeShare)}`;
    case "gap":
      return `Tape gap · ${reading.gapBars} bar${reading.gapBars === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
    case "forming":
      return "Forming";
    default:
      return `≥ ${formatMagnitude(reading.tier ?? 0)} ${unit} · tape ${percent(reading.share ?? 0)}`;
  }
}

/** Row (a): the confirmation state at the current bar with compact values (amendment 7). */
export function stateText(reading: SizeSplitReading | null, K: number): string {
  if (reading === null || !reading.valid) return SIZE_SPLIT_UNAVAILABLE;
  if (reading.state === null) return `Need ${K} valid bars`;
  const word = reading.state === "noExtreme" ? "no extreme" : reading.state;
  const withValues = `L ${formatSigned(reading.large ?? 0)} · S ${formatSigned(reading.small ?? 0)} · ${word}`;
  if (withValues.length <= SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS) return withValues;
  return reading.state === "noExtreme" ? "No extreme" : `Large ${word}`;
}

/** The newest condition whose Visibility toggle is on; divergence wins on the same bar (UX finding 3). */
export function visibleCondition(reading: SizeSplitReading | null, settings: Pick<SizeSplitDeltaSettings, "showDivergence" | "showConflict">): SizeSplitCondition | null {
  if (reading === null) return null;
  const divergence = settings.showDivergence ? reading.lastDivergence : null;
  const conflict = settings.showConflict ? reading.lastConflict : null;
  if (divergence === null) return conflict;
  if (conflict === null) return divergence;
  return conflict.tsOpen > divergence.tsOpen ? conflict : divergence;
}

/** Row (b): the last visible condition with its time and value, `—` when none in this anchor window. */
export function conditionText(condition: SizeSplitCondition | null, provider: BarFrame["provider"] | null): string {
  if (condition === null) return SIZE_SPLIT_UNAVAILABLE;
  const time = formatClock(condition.tsOpen, provider ?? "BINANCE");
  if (condition.kind === "divergence") {
    const arrow = condition.direction === "bearish" ? "↓" : "↑";
    return `Divergence ${arrow} ${time} · ${formatSigned(condition.values[0] ?? 0)}`;
  }
  const text = `Conflict ${time} · ${formatSigned(condition.values[0] ?? 0)}/${formatSigned(condition.values[1] ?? 0)}`;
  return text.length <= SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_MAX_CHARS ? text : `Conflict ${time}`;
}

export interface SizeSplitTableRow {
  text: string;
  color: string;
}

export function sizeSplitTableRows(
  reading: SizeSplitReading | null,
  settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings,
  state: Pick<SizeSplitDeltaState, "provider" | "symbol" | "assetClass">,
): readonly SizeSplitTableRow[] {
  return [
    // Row (a) is always the current reading, so it takes the solid large colour, never the provisional one (UX finding 1).
    { text: stateText(reading, settings.K), color: SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR },
    { text: conditionText(visibleCondition(reading, settings), state.provider), color: SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR },
    { text: statusText(reading, sizeUnitOf(state), settings.minTapeShare), color: SIZE_SPLIT_TABLE_TEXT_COLOR },
  ];
}

/** The four output keys, with the Visibility toggles applied (a hidden line reads null). */
export function visibleOutputs(reading: SizeSplitReading | null, settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings): Record<string, number | null> {
  return {
    large: reading?.large ?? null,
    small: settings.showSmall ? (reading?.small ?? null) : null,
    largeBar: settings.showBarLines ? (reading?.largeBar ?? null) : null,
    smallBar: settings.showBarLines ? (reading?.smallBar ?? null) : null,
  };
}

const SIZE_SPLIT_LINES: readonly LineOutputSpec<SizeSplitDeltaState, SizeSplitDeltaSettings>[] = [
  // The colours are constants, so `recolor` keeps every bar's colour: a settings
  // update that changes no recompute key never replays.
  { key: "large", lineWidth: 2, color: (state) => largeLineColor(state.reading), recolor: (current) => current ?? SIZE_SPLIT_LARGE_COLOR },
  { key: "small", lineWidth: 1, color: (state) => smallLineColor(state.reading), recolor: (current) => current ?? SIZE_SPLIT_SMALL_COLOR },
  { key: "largeBar", lineWidth: 1, color: (state) => barLineColor(state.reading), recolor: (current) => current ?? SIZE_SPLIT_BAR_LINE_COLOR },
  { key: "smallBar", lineWidth: 1, color: (state) => barLineColor(state.reading), recolor: (current) => current ?? SIZE_SPLIT_BAR_LINE_COLOR },
];

const SIZE_SPLIT_PLACEMENT: PlacementSpec = { kind: "ownPane", priceLines: [0] };

  { kind: "boolean", key: "showDivergence", label: "Show divergence arrows", affects: "recompute", tab: "visibility" },

/** The fixed-size field names the instrument's unit; the suggested sizes live in its hint and the stored default stays 0.5. */
export function sizeSplitSettingsFieldsFor(identity: { symbol: string } & FrameIdentity): Partial<Record<keyof SizeSplitDeltaSettings, SettingsFieldSchema<SizeSplitDeltaSettings>>> {
  const base = SIZE_SPLIT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA.find((field) => field.kind === "numeric" && field.key === "tierSize");
  if (base === undefined || base.kind !== "numeric") return {};
  if (identity.provider === "BINANCE") {
    const coin = identity.symbol.includes("-") ? identity.symbol.slice(0, identity.symbol.indexOf("-")) : identity.symbol;
    return { tierSize: { ...base, label: `Large from (${coin})` } };
  }
  if (identity.assetClass === "FUTURE") return { tierSize: { ...base, label: "Large from (contracts)", min: 1, step: 1 } };
  if (identity.assetClass === "EQUITY") return { tierSize: { ...base, label: "Large from (shares)", min: 1, step: 100 } };
  return {};
}

export const sizeSplitDeltaIndicator: RuntimeIndicatorDef<SizeSplitDeltaState, SizeSplitDeltaSettings> = {
  kind: "runtime",
  id: "sizeSplitDelta",
  label: "Size-Split Delta (large vs small)",
  category: "l1",
  ticksWanted: true,
  maxReplayBars: 10_080,
  defaultSettings: SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  settingsSchema: SIZE_SPLIT_SETTINGS_SCHEMA,
  settingsFieldsFor: sizeSplitSettingsFieldsFor,
  lines: SIZE_SPLIT_LINES,
  placement: SIZE_SPLIT_PLACEMENT,
  createState: (settings) => ({
    engine: createSizeSplitEngine({
      tierMode: settings.tierMode,
      tierSize: settings.tierSize,
      anchor: settings.anchor,
      K: settings.K,
      m: settings.m,
      q: settings.q,
      minTapeShare: settings.minTapeShare,
    }),
    table: draw.table(),
    settings,
    reading: null,
    provider: null,
    symbol: null,
    assetClass: null,
  }),
  onBar: (ctx) => {
    const { bar, state, settings } = ctx;
    state.provider = bar.provider;
    state.symbol = bar.symbol;
    state.assetClass = bar.assetClass ?? null;
    const reading = state.engine.onBar(bar, ctx.ticks);
    state.reading = reading;

    if (reading.valid) {
      const color = largeLineColor(reading);
      if (settings.showDivergence && reading.divergence !== null) {
        ctx.markers.plotshape({
          shape: reading.divergence === "bearish" ? "arrowDown" : "arrowUp",
          position: reading.divergence === "bearish" ? "aboveBar" : "belowBar",
          color,
          size: 2,
        });
      }
      if (settings.showConflict && reading.conflictOnset) {
        ctx.markers.plotshape({ shape: "circle", position: "inBar", color, size: 1 });
      }
    }

    if (ctx.drawNow !== false) {
      const rows = sizeSplitTableRows(reading, settings, state);
      for (let row = 0; row < rows.length; row += 1) state.table.cell(row, 0, { text: rows[row].text, textColor: rows[row].color });
    }
  },
  readOutput: (state) => visibleOutputs(state.reading, state.settings),
};

     1	import type { BarFrame } from "../../../types/frames";

     6	/**
     7	 * Size-Split Delta: the pure, incremental machinery
     8	 * (`pm/cycle2/brief-size-split-delta.md`, rules 1 to 13 and the round-3
     9	 * amendments).
    10	 *
    11	 * Every bar is a window `[ts_open, ts_open + interval_ms)` over the terminal's
    12	 * print store. A print at or above the bar's tier threshold is "large", the
    13	 * rest "small"; the signed sizes of each tier are the bar's two contributions,
    14	 * and the two lines are their cumulative sums since the anchor. The threshold
    15	 * is read once, at the bar's first dispatch, from a ring of the last 4,096
    16	 * sizes of committed, completely covered windows (nearest-rank p98, 200 sizes
    17	 * minimum), or is a fixed size.
    18	 *
    19	 * Commits are keyed to the bar close and tail-only. An explicit closed
    20	 * dispatch rescans the window from the store; a supersession (the next bar
    21	 * arrives first) commits the tail's last revision values without a rescan,
    22	 * because the runtime cannot republish the previous bar. After commit nothing
    23	 * about the bar changes until a rebuild replays everything from the store.
    24	 *
    25	 * Coverage from the store is the only gate on tick-derived values; a window
    26	 * that is not `"all"` has no value. The tape share (print volume over candle
    27	 * volume) must be known and inside `[minTapeShare, MAX_TAPE_SHARE]`, else the
    28	 * bar is ineligible; eligibility is frozen with the flow at commit.
    29	 */
    30	
    31	export const SIZE_RING_CAPACITY = 4_096;
    32	export const TIER_MIN_SIZES = 200;
    33	export const TIER_QUANTILE = 0.98;
    34	export const MAX_TAPE_SHARE = 1.25;
    35	export const RECORD_RING_CAPACITY = 10_080;
    36	export const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
    37	export const WEEK_MS = 7 * DAY_MS;
    38	export const SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_K = 20;

   149	export interface WindowScan {

   158	function isFinitePositive(value: number): boolean {
   159	  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0;

   162	/** The tape share of a bar, null when the candle volume is missing or not positive (fail closed). */
   163	export function tapeShareOf(tapeVolume: number, volume: number): number | null {
   164	  if (!Number.isFinite(volume) || volume <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(tapeVolume) || tapeVolume < 0) return null;
   165	  return tapeVolume / volume;
   166	}

   168	export function shareEligible(share: number | null, minTapeShare: number): boolean {
   169	  return share !== null && Number.isFinite(share) && share >= minTapeShare && share <= MAX_TAPE_SHARE;
   170	}
   171	
   172	/**
   173	 * The window `[fromTs, toTs)` scanned once from the store: valid prints only,
   174	 * `size >= tier` large with the boundary print included, buys positive. With
   175	 * no threshold nothing is classified, but the valid sizes are still collected
   176	 * so they can train the ring (amendment 3). The size list is bounded to the
   177	 * newest `SIZE_RING_CAPACITY` (amendment 1).
   178	 */
   179	export function scanWindow(ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined, fromTs: number, toTs: number, tier: number | null): WindowScan {
   180	  if (ticks === undefined) return { coverage: "noStore", largeBar: null, smallBar: null, tapeVolume: 0, printCount: 0, sizes: [] };
   181	  const coverage = ticks.coverageOf(fromTs, toTs);
   182	  const prints: readonly AggressivePrint[] = ticks.prints;
   183	  let largeBar = 0;
   184	  let smallBar = 0;
   185	  let tapeVolume = 0;
   186	  let printCount = 0;
   187	  const sizes: number[] = [];
   188	  for (let index = firstPrintAtOrAfter(prints, fromTs); index < prints.length; index += 1) {
   189	    const print = prints[index];
   190	    if (print.ts >= toTs) break;
   191	    if (!isFinitePositive(print.size) || !isFinitePositive(print.price)) continue;
   192	    printCount += 1;
   193	    tapeVolume += print.size;
   194	    sizes.push(print.size);
   195	    if (tier === null) continue;
   196	    const signed = print.side === "buy" ? print.size : -print.size;
   197	    if (print.size >= tier) largeBar += signed;
   198	    else smallBar += signed;
   199	  }
   200	  if (sizes.length > SIZE_RING_CAPACITY) sizes.splice(0, sizes.length - SIZE_RING_CAPACITY);
   201	  return { coverage, largeBar: tier === null ? null : largeBar, smallBar: tier === null ? null : smallBar, tapeVolume, printCount, sizes };
   202	}
   203	
   204	/**
   205	 * The size ring: the last `SIZE_RING_CAPACITY` sizes in arrival order, plus the
   206	 * same values kept sorted so the nearest-rank quantile is one index read. Both
   207	 * are typed arrays; an insert or an eviction moves at most 4,096 doubles with
   208	 * `copyWithin`, so a commit of a hundred prints costs well under a millisecond
   209	 * and nothing is ever re-sorted.
   210	 */
   211	export interface SizeRing {
   212	  readonly count: number;
   213	  push(sizes: readonly number[]): void;
   214	  /** Nearest-rank p98 of the held sizes, null under `TIER_MIN_SIZES`. */
   215	  threshold(): number | null;
   216	  /** The held sizes in arrival order (oldest first); for tests. */
   217	  values(): number[];
   218	}
   219	
   220	export function createSizeRing(capacity = SIZE_RING_CAPACITY, minSizes = TIER_MIN_SIZES, quantile = TIER_QUANTILE): SizeRing {
   221	  const arrival = new Float64Array(capacity);
   222	  const sorted = new Float64Array(capacity);
   223	  let head = 0;
   224	  let count = 0;
   225	
   226	  function lowerBound(value: number): number {
   227	    let low = 0;
   228	    let high = count;
   229	    while (low < high) {
   230	      const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
   231	      if (sorted[middle] < value) low = middle + 1;
   232	      else high = middle;
   233	    }
   234	    return low;
   235	  }
   236	
   237	  function removeSorted(value: number): void {
   238	    const index = lowerBound(value);
   239	    sorted.copyWithin(index, index + 1, count);
   240	    count -= 1;
   241	  }
   242	
   243	  function insertSorted(value: number): void {
   244	    const index = lowerBound(value);
   245	    sorted.copyWithin(index + 1, index, count);
   246	    sorted[index] = value;
   247	    count += 1;
   248	  }
   249	
   250	  return {
   251	    get count() {
   252	      return count;
   253	    },
   254	    push(sizes) {
   255	      for (const size of sizes) {
   256	        if (!isFinitePositive(size)) continue;
   257	        if (count === capacity) removeSorted(arrival[head]);
   258	        arrival[head] = size;
   259	        head = (head + 1) % capacity;
   260	        insertSorted(size);
   261	      }
   262	    },
   263	    threshold() {
   264	      if (count < minSizes) return null;
   265	      const index = Math.min(count - 1, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(quantile * count) - 1));
   266	      return sorted[index];
   267	    },
   268	    values() {
   269	      const out: number[] = [];
   270	      const start = count === capacity ? head : 0;

   277	/** The anchor window a bar belongs to: UTC-aligned day or week (epoch-aligned, as the session kernel), or null for `tape`. */
   278	export function anchorStartFor(tsOpen: number, anchor: SizeSplitAnchor): number | null {
   279	  if (anchor === "tape") return null;
   280	  const period = anchor === "day" ? DAY_MS : WEEK_MS;
   281	  if (!Number.isFinite(tsOpen)) return null;
   282	  return Math.floor(tsOpen / period) * period;
   283	}
   284	
   285	export interface MarkerEvaluation {
   286	  state: ConfirmationState | null;
   287	  /** The divergence condition at this bar. */
   288	  divergenceHeld: DivergenceDirection | null;
   289	  /** The drawn arrow: the condition's onset, held at this bar and not held in the same direction at the previous valid bar (PM amendment to rule 11). */
   290	  divergence: DivergenceDirection | null;
   291	  depth: number | null;
   292	  conflictHeld: boolean;
   293	  conflictOnset: boolean;
   294	  conflictSums: readonly [number, number] | null;
   295	}
   296	
   297	const NO_EVALUATION: MarkerEvaluation = Object.freeze({ state: null, divergenceHeld: null, divergence: null, depth: null, conflictHeld: false, conflictOnset: false, conflictSums: null });
   298	
   299	/**
   300	 * Rule 11 and amendment 2 for bar `t` against a full ring of `K` valid committed
   301	 * bars immediately before it: `h` the ring bar with the highest close (latest
   302	 * if tied), `l` the lowest; a strict new high confirms when `large_t >
   303	 * large_h`, lags otherwise, and draws the bearish arrow when `large_t <=
   304	 * large_h - m · mabsL`; the mirror for a strict new low. The conflict holds
   305	 * when the ring-plus-`t` sums of the two tiers oppose each other and each
   306	 * clears `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution. Both
   307	 * markers fire on the onset only: the condition holds at `t` and did not hold
   308	 * (in that direction) at the previous valid bar, so a trend where every bar is
   309	 * a new extreme with a lagging large tier marks once and again only after the
   310	 * condition has cleared (PM amendment to rule 11).
   311	 */
   312	export function evaluateMarkers(
   313	  ring: readonly MarkerRingEntry[],
   314	  bar: { close: number; largeBar: number; smallBar: number; large: number },
   315	  previous: { conflict: boolean; divergence: DivergenceDirection | null },
   316	  settings: Pick<SizeSplitMathSettings, "K" | "m" | "q">,
   317	): MarkerEvaluation {
   318	  const K = settings.K;
   319	  if (ring.length < K) return NO_EVALUATION;
   320	  let h = 0;
   321	  let l = 0;
   322	  let sumAbsLarge = 0;
   323	  let sumAbsSmall = 0;
   324	  let sumLarge = bar.largeBar;
   325	  let sumSmall = bar.smallBar;
   326	  for (let index = 0; index < ring.length; index += 1) {
   327	    const entry = ring[index];
   328	    if (entry.close >= ring[h].close) h = index;
   329	    if (entry.close <= ring[l].close) l = index;
   330	    sumAbsLarge += Math.abs(entry.largeBar);
   331	    sumAbsSmall += Math.abs(entry.smallBar);
   332	    sumLarge += entry.largeBar;
   333	    sumSmall += entry.smallBar;
   334	  }
   335	  const mabsL = sumAbsLarge / ring.length;
   336	  const mabsS = sumAbsSmall / ring.length;
   337	  let state: ConfirmationState = "noExtreme";
   338	  let divergenceHeld: DivergenceDirection | null = null;
   339	  let depth: number | null = null;
   340	  if (bar.close > ring[h].close) {
   341	    state = bar.large > ring[h].large ? "confirms" : "lags";
   342	    if (bar.large <= ring[h].large - settings.m * mabsL) {
   343	      divergenceHeld = "bearish";
   344	      depth = bar.large - ring[h].large;
   345	    }
   346	  } else if (bar.close < ring[l].close) {
   347	    state = bar.large < ring[l].large ? "confirms" : "lags";
   348	    if (bar.large >= ring[l].large + settings.m * mabsL) {
   349	      divergenceHeld = "bullish";
   350	      depth = bar.large - ring[l].large;
   351	    }
   352	  }
   353	  const divergence = divergenceHeld !== null && divergenceHeld !== previous.divergence ? divergenceHeld : null;
   354	  const strength = settings.q * Math.sqrt(K + 1);
   355	  const conflictHeld = Math.sign(sumLarge) * Math.sign(sumSmall) < 0 && Math.abs(sumLarge) >= strength * mabsL && Math.abs(sumSmall) >= strength * mabsS;
   356	  return { state, divergenceHeld, divergence, depth: divergence === null ? null : depth, conflictHeld, conflictOnset: conflictHeld && !previous.conflict, conflictSums: [sumLarge, sumSmall] };
   357	}
   358	
   359	/** Status precedence, first match wins (rule 12, amendment 5). */
   360	export function statusOf(record: Pick<SizeSplitRecord, "coverage" | "tapeStarting" | "tierAtOpen" | "share" | "eligible" | "committed">, gapBars: number, validSinceGap: number, K: number): SizeSplitStatus {
   361	  if (record.coverage === "none" && record.tapeStarting) return "tapeStarting";
   362	  if (record.coverage === "noStore" || record.coverage === "none") return "noFeed";
   363	  if (record.coverage === "thin") return "thinned";
   364	  if (record.tierAtOpen === null) return "learning";
   365	  if (record.share === null) return "shareUnknown";
   366	  if (record.share > MAX_TAPE_SHARE) return "shareMismatch";
   367	  if (!record.eligible) return "shareLow";
   368	  if (gapBars > 0 && validSinceGap < K) return "gap";
   369	  if (!record.committed) return "forming";
   370	  return "ready";
   371	}
   372	
   373	export interface SizeSplitEngine {
   374	  /** Feed one dispatch (forming revision, close, or the next bar) and read the current bar. */
   375	  onBar(bar: BarFrame, ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined): SizeSplitReading;
   376	  readonly tail: SizeSplitRecord | null;
   377	  readonly sizeRing: SizeRing;
   378	  readonly markerRing: readonly MarkerRingEntry[];
   379	  readonly base: { readonly large: number; readonly small: number };
   380	  readonly anchorStart: number | null;
   381	  /** Committed bars, oldest first, at most `RECORD_RING_CAPACITY`. */
   382	  readonly committed: readonly CommittedBar[];
   383	  readonly lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null;
   384	  readonly lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null;
   385	}
   386	
   387	export function createSizeSplitEngine(settings: SizeSplitMathSettings): SizeSplitEngine {
   388	  const sizeRing = createSizeRing();
   389	  const committed: CommittedBar[] = [];
   390	  const markerRing: MarkerRingEntry[] = [];
   391	  const base = { large: 0, small: 0 };
   392	  let tail: SizeSplitRecord | null = null;
   393	  let anchorStart: number | null = null;
   394	  let anchorSeen = false;
   395	  let previousConflict = false;
   396	  let previousDivergence: DivergenceDirection | null = null;
   397	  let lastDivergence: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
   398	  let lastConflict: SizeSplitCondition | null = null;
   399	  /** Whether a valid bar has committed in this anchor window (a gap needs a valid bar before it). */
   400	  let validEver = false;
   401	  /** Invalid committed bars in the most recent gap, and valid bars committed since it closed. */
   402	  let gapBars = 0;
   403	  let validSinceGap = 0;
   404	  let lastReading: SizeSplitReading | null = null;
   405	
   406	  function tierFor(): number | null {
   407	    if (settings.tierMode === "fixed") return isFinitePositive(settings.tierSize) ? settings.tierSize : null;
   408	    return sizeRing.threshold();
   409	  }
   410	
   411	  function divergenceOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
   412	    return evaluation.divergence === null ? null : { kind: "divergence", direction: evaluation.divergence, tsOpen, values: [evaluation.depth ?? 0] };
   413	  }
   414	
   415	  function conflictOf(tsOpen: number, evaluation: MarkerEvaluation): SizeSplitCondition | null {
   416	    return evaluation.conflictOnset && evaluation.conflictSums !== null ? { kind: "conflict", direction: null, tsOpen, values: evaluation.conflictSums } : null;
   417	  }
   418	
   419	  function readingOf(record: SizeSplitRecord): SizeSplitReading {
   420	    const large = record.valid ? base.large + (record.largeBar as number) : null;
   421	    const small = record.valid ? base.small + (record.smallBar as number) : null;
   422	    const evaluation = record.valid
   423	      ? evaluateMarkers(markerRing, { close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: large as number }, { conflict: previousConflict, divergence: previousDivergence }, settings)
   424	      : NO_EVALUATION;
   425	    const ownDivergence = record.valid ? divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
   426	    const ownConflict = record.valid ? conflictOf(record.tsOpen, evaluation) : null;
   427	    return {
   428	      tsOpen: record.tsOpen,
   429	      large,
   430	      small,
   431	      largeBar: record.valid ? record.largeBar : null,
   432	      smallBar: record.valid ? record.smallBar : null,
   433	      share: record.share,
   434	      tier: record.tierAtOpen,
   435	      sizesLearned: sizeRing.count,
   436	      status: statusOf(record, gapBars, validSinceGap, settings.K),
   437	      gapBars,
   438	      committed: record.committed,
   439	      confirmed: record.confirmed,
   440	      valid: record.valid,
   441	      ringLength: markerRing.length,
   442	      state: evaluation.state,
   443	      divergence: evaluation.divergence,
   444	      divergenceHeld: evaluation.divergenceHeld,
   445	      depth: evaluation.depth,
   446	      conflictHeld: evaluation.conflictHeld,
   447	      conflictOnset: evaluation.conflictOnset,
   448	      conflictSums: evaluation.conflictSums,
   449	      lastDivergence: ownDivergence ?? lastDivergence,
   450	      lastConflict: ownConflict ?? lastConflict,
   451	    };
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function fill(record: SizeSplitRecord, bar: BarFrame, ticks: IndicatorTicks | undefined): void {
   455	    const scan = scanWindow(ticks, record.tsOpen, record.windowEnd, record.tierAtOpen);
   456	    record.close = bar.close;
   457	    record.volume = bar.volume;
   458	    record.coverage = scan.coverage;
   459	    record.tapeStarting = scan.coverage === "none" && ticks !== undefined && ticks.prints.length > 0 && ticks.liveSince !== null && ticks.liveSince < record.windowEnd;
   460	    record.largeBar = scan.largeBar;
   461	    record.smallBar = scan.smallBar;
   462	    record.tapeVolume = scan.tapeVolume;
   463	    record.printCount = scan.printCount;
   464	    record.sizes = scan.sizes;
   465	    record.share = scan.coverage === "all" ? tapeShareOf(scan.tapeVolume, bar.volume) : null;
   466	    record.eligible = shareEligible(record.share, settings.minTapeShare);
   467	    record.valid = scan.coverage === "all" && record.tierAtOpen !== null && scan.largeBar !== null && scan.smallBar !== null && record.eligible;
   468	  }
   469	
   470	  /** Steps 2 to 4 of rule 6: freeze flow and eligibility, train the ring, advance the cumulatives (or record the gap). */
   471	  function commit(record: SizeSplitRecord, confirmed: boolean): void {
   472	    record.confirmed = confirmed;
   473	    record.committed = true;
   474	    const reading = readingOf(record);
   475	    record.reading = reading;
   476	    if (record.coverage === "all" && record.sizes !== null) sizeRing.push(record.sizes);
   477	    if (record.valid) {
   478	      base.large += record.largeBar as number;
   479	      base.small += record.smallBar as number;
   480	      markerRing.push({ close: record.close, largeBar: record.largeBar as number, smallBar: record.smallBar as number, large: base.large });
   481	      if (markerRing.length > settings.K) markerRing.splice(0, markerRing.length - settings.K);
   482	      previousConflict = reading.conflictHeld;
   483	      previousDivergence = reading.divergenceHeld;
   484	      const ownDivergence = divergenceOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
   485	      const ownConflict = conflictOf(record.tsOpen, reading);
   486	      if (ownDivergence !== null) lastDivergence = ownDivergence;
   487	      if (ownConflict !== null) lastConflict = ownConflict;
   488	      validEver = true;
   489	      validSinceGap += 1;
   490	    } else {
   491	      markerRing.length = 0;
   492	      previousConflict = false;
   493	      previousDivergence = null;
   494	      if (validEver) {
   495	        gapBars = validSinceGap > 0 ? 1 : gapBars + 1;
   496	        validSinceGap = 0;
   497	      }
   498	    }
   499	    record.sizes = null;
   500	    committed.push({ tsOpen: record.tsOpen, tierAtOpen: record.tierAtOpen, largeBar: record.largeBar, smallBar: record.smallBar, share: record.share, valid: record.valid, confirmed });
   501	    if (committed.length > RECORD_RING_CAPACITY) committed.splice(0, committed.length - RECORD_RING_CAPACITY);
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  /** Step 5 of rule 6: the new record, with the anchor reset applied first when the bar opens a new window (rule 8). */
   505	  function open(bar: BarFrame): SizeSplitRecord {
   506	    const start = anchorStartFor(bar.ts_open, settings.anchor);
   507	    if (!anchorSeen || start !== anchorStart) {
   508	      if (anchorSeen) {
   509	        base.large = 0;
   510	        base.small = 0;
   511	        markerRing.length = 0;
   512	        previousConflict = false;
   513	        previousDivergence = null;
   514	        lastDivergence = null;
   515	        lastConflict = null;
   516	        validEver = false;
   517	        gapBars = 0;
   518	        validSinceGap = 0;
   519	      }
   520	      anchorStart = start;
   521	      anchorSeen = true;
   522	    }
   523	    return {
   524	      tsOpen: bar.ts_open,
   525	      windowEnd: bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms,
   526	      close: bar.close,
   527	      volume: bar.volume,
   528	      tierAtOpen: tierFor(),
   529	      coverage: "noStore",
   530	      tapeStarting: false,
   531	      largeBar: null,
   532	      smallBar: null,
   533	      tapeVolume: 0,
   534	      printCount: 0,
   535	      share: null,
   536	      eligible: false,
   537	      valid: false,
   538	      sizes: null,
   539	      committed: false,
   540	      confirmed: false,
   541	      reading: null,
   542	    };
   543	  }
   544	
   545	  return {
   546	    onBar(bar, ticks) {
   547	      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open < tail.tsOpen) return lastReading ?? readingOf(tail);
   548	      if (tail !== null && bar.ts_open > tail.tsOpen) {
   549	        if (!tail.committed) commit(tail, false);
   550	        tail = null;
   551	      }
   552	      if (tail === null) tail = open(bar);
   553	      if (tail.committed) {
   554	        lastReading = tail.reading ?? readingOf(tail);
   555	        return lastReading;
   556	      }
   557	      fill(tail, bar, ticks);
   558	      if (bar.closed) {
   559	        commit(tail, true);
   560	        lastReading = tail.reading as SizeSplitReading;
   561	        return lastReading;
   562	      }
   563	      lastReading = readingOf(tail);
   564	      return lastReading;
   565	    },
   566	    get tail() {
   567	      return tail;
   568	    },
   569	    sizeRing,
   570	    markerRing,
   571	    base,
   572	    get anchorStart() {
   573	      return anchorStart;
   574	    },
   575	    committed,
   576	    get lastDivergence() {
   577	      return lastDivergence;
   578	    },
   579	    get lastConflict() {
   580	      return lastConflict;
   581	    },
   582	  };
   583	}
   584	
   585	/* Formatting shared by the table and the doc examples (amendment 7). */
   586	
   587	const MINUS = "−";
   588	
   589	/** Three significant digits with `k`/`M` suffixes and an explicit sign; zero prints as `0`. */
   590	export function formatSigned(value: number): string {
   591	  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "—";
   592	  if (value === 0) return "0";
   593	  const sign = value < 0 ? MINUS : "+";
   594	  return `${sign}${formatMagnitude(Math.abs(value))}`;
   595	}
   596	
   597	export function formatMagnitude(value: number): string {
   598	  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return "—";
   599	  if (value >= 1e6) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e6)}M`;
   600	  if (value >= 1e3) return `${trimDigits(value / 1e3)}k`;
   601	  return trimDigits(value);
   602	}
   603	
   604	function trimDigits(value: number): string {
   605	  if (value === 0) return "0";
   606	  const text = value.toPrecision(3);
   607	  if (text.includes("e")) return Number(text).toString();
   608	  return text.includes(".") ? text.replace(/\.?0+$/, "") : text;
   609	}
   610	
   611	/** `HH:MM` in the instrument's clock: New York for dxFeed and Databento, UTC for Binance. */
   612	export function formatClock(tsOpen: number, provider: BarFrame["provider"]): string {
   613	  if (!Number.isFinite(tsOpen)) return "—";
   614	  const minuteOfDay = provider === "DXFEED" || provider === "DATABENTO" ? nyLocalMinuteOfDay(tsOpen) : Math.floor((tsOpen % DAY_MS) / 60_000);
   615	  const hours = Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60);
   616	  const minutes = minuteOfDay % 60;
   617	  return `${String(hours).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(minutes).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   618	}

# Size-Split Delta (large vs small)

[Handbook](../../README.md) · [Indicator index](README.md) ·
[Data for indicator authors](data-for-indicator-authors.md) ·
[Setting presets](setting-presets.md)

Code: `apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/` (`sizeSplitDelta.ts` is
the registry definition, `sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts` the pure machinery, `tests/`
the evidence). Registry id `sizeSplitDelta`, label "Size-Split Delta (large vs
small)", category L1, own pane, `ticksWanted`. The code wins over this page.
Brief: `pm/cycle2/brief-size-split-delta.md`.

## The question it answers

"Does large-print flow confirm this price extreme?" Small-print flow is shown
as context, not as a second question.

Aggressive flow is split by **trade frame size as delivered**: a print at or
above the bar's tier threshold is "large", the rest "small". The purple line is
the cumulative signed size of the large tier since the anchor, the blue line
the small tier's. When price makes a new extreme and the large line makes it
too, the large prints confirm; when it does not, they lag, and when they lag by
enough an arrow marks the bar. Print size is not participant size (a sliced
iceberg prints small), and the tool never infers a side it was not given.
Joining or fading remain the trader's decisions. Descriptive only.

## Calculation

One bar at a time, incrementally; `onBar` never rebuilds from an array.

1. **Window.** `[ts_open, ts_open + interval_ms)` over the terminal's print
   store (`ctx.ticks`), located with `firstPrintAtOrAfter`; a print with a
   non-finite or non-positive size or price is skipped.
2. **Tier threshold, read before write.** On a bar's first dispatch, after the
   previous bar has committed, the bar stores `tierAtOpen`: in `auto` mode the
   nearest-rank 98th percentile (index `ceil(0.98 n) - 1` of the ascending
   sort) of the size ring, `null` while the ring holds fewer than 200 sizes;
   in `fixed` mode the `tierSize` setting. Every revision and the commit of the
   bar use that value. "Top 2%" is therefore an estimate from preceding trade
   frames, ties included: a bar can classify more than 2 percent of its prints
   large, and with fewer than 4,096 sizes the quantile can land on a small
   print, which then classifies large.
3. **Size ring.** The last 4,096 sizes of committed windows with coverage
   `"all"`, both tiers, kept in arrival order and in sorted order in two typed
   arrays (64 KB); an insert or eviction moves at most 4,096 doubles and nothing
   is ever re-sorted. With no threshold nothing is classified, but valid sizes
   still train the ring.
4. **Contribution.** Per print, `size >= tierAtOpen` is large (the boundary
   print included); buys positive, sells negative; `largeBar` and `smallBar`
   are the bar's two sums, `tapeVolume` the sum of all valid sizes.
5. **Tape share, fail closed.** `share = tapeVolume / bar.volume` when the
   candle volume is finite and positive, else unknown. The bar is eligible when
   the share is known and inside `[minTapeShare, 1.25]`. Zero or missing candle
   volume with prints, no prints with positive volume (share 0), no prints and
   zero volume (unknown) and a share above 1.25 (a mismatch between tape and
   candle) are all ineligible; an ineligible bar's flow is `null`, never zero.
6. **Commit, keyed to the bar close, tail-only.** A bar commits at the first of
   (a) its first dispatch with `closed: true`, (b) the first dispatch of a
   later bar while it is uncommitted. On (a) the window is rescanned once from
   the store, flow and eligibility are frozen, the sizes enter the ring (if the
   window was `"all"`), and the cumulatives advance if the bar is valid. On (b)
   the runtime cannot republish the previous bar, so the commit uses the last
   revision's values with no rescan: the committed history equals what was
   displayed, and **a print that arrived after the last revision and before
   the next bar is excluded** from that bar's flow and from the next threshold
   until a rebuild replays everything from the store. After commit nothing
   about the bar changes on a later dispatch of the same bar.
7. **Forming bar.** Every revision rescans the forming window from scratch and
   overwrites the tail; the forming output is the frozen cumulative under the
   current anchor plus the forming contribution, drawn provisional (50 percent
   alpha, status `Forming`) until the bar commits, by its close or by
   supersession (UX pass, finding 5).
8. **Anchor and reset.** `day` (default) and `week` are UTC-aligned windows
   (`floor(ts / period) · period`, the session kernel's convention: the week
   window starts on the epoch's weekday, a Thursday); `tape` cumulates from the
   first covered bar. On the first dispatch inside a new window, forming
   included, the cumulative base is zero, the marker ring is empty and the
   table's state and condition rows are cleared.
9. **Gaps.** A bar without a value (coverage not `"all"`, no threshold,
   ineligible share) adds nothing; both lines are `null` on it; the cumulative
   resumes unchanged at the next valid bar, so a sum after a gap omits the gap's
   flow. The marker ring empties and the status row names the gap until `K`
   valid bars have followed.
10. **Markers**, a function of committed state, provisional on the forming bar
    and final at the close. The marker ring holds exactly `K` (default 20)
    consecutive valid committed bars immediately before the bar; a gap or an
    anchor reset empties it and nothing fires until it is full again. `mabsL`
    and `mabsS` are the means of `|largeBar|` and `|smallBar|` over the ring
    (the bar itself excluded). With `h` the ring bar with the highest close
    (latest if tied) and `l` the lowest: a strict new high (`close > close_h`)
    **confirms** when `large > large_h` and **lags** otherwise; the bearish
    divergence condition holds when `large <= large_h - m · mabsL` (`m`
    default 2); the mirror for a strict new low. The **conflict** holds when
    the ring-plus-bar sums of the two tiers have opposite signs and each clears
    `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution (`q` default
    2). **Both markers fire on the onset only** (PM amendment to the brief's
    rule 11): the arrow or circle is drawn when the condition holds at the bar
    and did not hold, in that direction, at the previous valid bar, the
    condition being re-evaluated every bar as written. A trend where every bar
    is a new extreme with a persistently lagging large tier marks once and
    again only after the condition has cleared. At most two markers on a bar.
11. **No future data.** The window end is the cutoff, so a committed bar
    ignores prints at or after it even when the store already holds them;
    thresholds come from committed bars only. Truncating the series changes no
    earlier output or committed marker (tested at every cut of the fixture).
12. **Fail closed.** Any non-finite intermediate yields `null`; nothing ever
    emits `NaN`.

## Data and where the tool works

Only the print store is read for flow: `ctx.ticks` (`prints`, `liveSince`,
`coverageOf`). From the bar: `ts_open`, `interval_ms`, `closed`, `close`,
`volume`. Never `barSides`, `buy_volume`, `ctx.trades` or
`ctx.tradeAvailability`: tick-store coverage is the only gate on tick-derived
values. Sizes are provider units (coins on Binance, contracts on a future,
shares on an equity) and are never added across providers.

| Path | Tape | Indicator |
|---|---|---|
| Runtime WebSocket, Binance or dxFeed | live prints complete from connect; the pre-connect pool and `aggressive_history` are floor-filtered (`"thin"`) | lines begin at the first bar whose whole window is `"all"`; earlier bars `null`, status `Needs live tape · thinned` |
| Browser-direct | live `trade` stream complete from connect; the `aggTrades` bootstrap is `"thin"` | as above; the bootstrap never enters a value |
| REST candle transport | no trade frames, coverage `"none"`, no store | every output `null`, no markers, status `Needs live tape · no feed` |
| Historical Mode | no store | unavailable: "Needs the live tape: the archive keeps only the largest prints of each minute." |
| Equities (dxFeed) | only prints with an aggressor side reach the browser | the tape share gate: on AAPL/TSLA the sided share is often under 50 percent and the bar reads `null` with `Tape 38% · below 50%` |

The store drops live prints past 250,000 (`liveDroppedThrough`): at about
1,600 trade frames a minute on BTC-USDT the complete stretch is roughly 2.6
hours, and windows that reached the drop read `"thin"`. The cumulative starts
at the later of the anchor and the covered start; the line's own start shows
it. The first forming bar after connect (or after a reconnect) is not covered
either, because its window began before the live recording: that is its own
state, `tapeStarting`, shown as `Tape live · next bar` (coverage `"none"`
while the store exists, has held at least one print and `liveSince` falls
inside the window); `Needs live tape · no feed` is kept for no store at all or
a store that has never held a print (UX pass, finding 2). The "never held a
print" clause is the build's addition: the REST-only path absorbs an empty
trade list on every snapshot, which sets `liveSince` without any feed, and
without the clause a REST-only chart would read `Tape live · next bar` for
ever.

Aggregates: the 10,000-row BTC-USDT fixture holds Binance aggregate trades
(one taker order at one price), so a tier estimated on it is higher than one
estimated on individual prints (p98 0.106 BTC, p99 0.198, max 2.0; the top 2
percent carry 52.5 percent of its volume). The tier is "per trade frame as
delivered"; fixture numbers do not transfer between transports.

## Default view

Own pane with a dotted zero line: `large` purple `#8672b3` width 2, `small`
blue `#2563eb` width 1, no fill; both at 50 percent alpha until the bar
commits. Divergence arrows (`arrowDown` above the bar for a bearish
divergence, `arrowUp` below for a bullish one, size 2) in the large line's
colour; conflict circles (`inBar`, size 1, same colour) are off by default.
Table, top right, three rows of at most 30 characters, values with three
significant digits and `k`/`M` suffixes; row (a) always in the solid large
colour, since it describes the current reading:

| Row | Content |
|---|---|
| (a) state | `L +12.4 · S −8.1 · confirms`, `… · lags` or `… · no extreme`; `Need 20 valid bars` until the ring is full; `—` while the bar has no value. If a row would exceed 30 characters the values are dropped: `Large confirms`, `Large lags`, `No extreme`. |
| (b) condition | the newest condition in this anchor window whose Visibility toggle is on, with its time (UTC on Binance, New York on dxFeed) and value: `Divergence ↓ 12:34 · −8.12k` (depth `large − large_h`) or `Conflict 12:34 · +4.21k/−3.12k` (the two sums; over 30 characters, `Conflict 12:34`); divergence wins when both fire on one bar; `—` when none, or when the toggle of the only condition is off; cleared on an anchor reset. |
| (c) status | first match wins: `Needs live tape · no feed`, `Tape live · next bar`, `Needs live tape · thinned`, `Learning sizes · 120 of 200`, `Tape share unknown`, `Tape 160% · mismatch`, `Tape 38% · below 50%`, `Tape gap · 3 bars`, `Forming`, ready `≥ 0.106 BTC · tape 100%`. |

## Controls

Inputs (primary), each with a one-line hint: `tierMode` "Large tier"
(Largest 2% of prints / Fixed size), `tierSize` "Large from (BTC)" (shown for
a fixed tier; the label names the instrument's unit, the hint suggests 0.5
BTC, 20 contracts or 2,000 shares; the stored default is 0.5), `anchor`
"Anchor" (Day (UTC) / Week (UTC) / Tape start), and a closing paragraph:
"Needs the live tape; lines start one bar after connect. Arrow: price made a
new high or low that the large prints did not. Circle: large and small prints
pulling opposite ways." Advanced: `K` "Extreme window (bars)" 10..60 step 5,
`m` "Divergence depth (×)" 1..4 step 0.5, `q` "Conflict strength (×)" 1..4
step 0.5, `minTapeShare` "Tape share floor (0–1)" 0.2..1 step 0.05.
Visibility: `showSmall` "Show small tier (blue)" (on), `showDivergence` (on),
`showConflict` (off), `showBarLines` "Show per-bar lines" (off; the per-bar
contributions as two grey lines, the harness's seams). Every setting replays,
the Visibility toggles included (a hidden line reads `null`, which `recolor`
cannot do), so a toggle costs a replay of the retained bars, well under a
second; there are no colour pickers.

## Alert conditions

| Name | Rule | Fires on | On the mechanism fixture |
|---|---|---|---|
| Large-tier divergence (arrow) | strict new `K`-window close extreme with `large` on the wrong side of `large_h` (or `large_l`) by at least `m · mabs`; onset only | the first bar the condition holds after it did not, provisional while forming, final at its close | 7 of 300 bars at the default day anchor (8 planted; one falls inside the 20 bars after the UTC-midnight reset), 8 with the tape anchor; every planted bar is an onset |
| Tier conflict (circle, off by default) | the ring-plus-bar sums of the two tiers oppose each other, each at `q · sqrt(K + 1)` times its tier's mean absolute contribution; onset only | the first bar the condition holds after it did not | 17 of 300 bars with the tape anchor |

## Warm-up

Auto mode: 200 trade frames from complete windows, then the next bar open; on
the fixture (100 prints a bar) the first value is bar 2 (`Learning sizes · 0
of 200`, then `100 of 200`). Fixed mode values from the first covered bar.
Markers and row (a) need `K` valid committed bars before the bar (`Need 20
valid bars`). The first minute after connect reads `Tape live · next bar`.

## Replay and Historical Mode

Live and replay read the same store, so a replay reproduces the live values
for every bar whose window the store covers; the forming bar is provisional
and excluded. The documented differences are a reconnect (a new store covers
fewer windows, which read `null`) and a rebuild after a late print (a print
that arrived after a bar's last revision and before its supersession enters
only when a `REBUILT` or `setTicks` replay rescans the bar from the store,
and only if the candle's volume carries it too, else the share gate refuses
the bar). The runtime trims its outputs and markers on the rollover but does
not notify indicator state, so the indicator bounds its own: two running
totals, the 4,096-size ring, the `K`-ring and a ring of 10,080 committed bars
(`maxReplayBars`); the size ring may hold sizes from bars the chart has
discarded, so a replay over the retained bars can give the earliest retained
bars a different threshold. Equivalence is tested from the first retained bar
whose anchor start and whose preceding 4,096 committed sizes both lie inside
the retained series (8,726 bars compared equal on the 10,380-bar rollover
fixture). Historical Mode is unavailable with the reason above; a replay with
no store is `null` everywhere.

## Baseline comparison

Two comparisons, both from `tests/sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts`, truth
from the generators' hidden state, every arrow also checked against the
independent reference implementation of the rule
(`validation/sizeSplitReference.ts`).

Mechanism fixture `sizeSplitFixture()` (300 bars, seed 20261102, 2 large
prints of 1.0 BTC and 98 small in [0.01, 0.049) a bar, 8 planted divergences at
bars 45 + 30 j where the large prints oppose a forced strict new extreme), tape
anchor:

| Configuration | planted | hits on the planted bar | off-label arrows | conflict onsets |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| auto (top 2%) | 8 | 8 | 0 | 17 (equal to the reference) |
| fixed 0.5 BTC | 8 | 8 | 0 | |
| CVD from `barSides`, same rule | 8 | 8 | 5 | |

Regime fixtures `sizeSplitRegimeBars({ seed, priceFollows })`, 600 bars, seeds
1..10, the common evaluation mask being the bars where both configurations have
a value and a full ring (5,780 bars per regime over the ten seeds); `"large"`
plants 10 events per seed, `"small"` and `"none"` plant nothing so every arrow
there is off-label:

| regime | configuration | eligible | planted | hits | off-label arrows |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| large | auto | 5,780 | 100 | 100 | 0 |
| large | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 100 | 100 | 0 |
| large | CVD | 5,780 | 100 | 80 | 171 |
| small | auto | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 488 |
| small | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 490 |
| small | CVD | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 37 |
| none | auto | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 256 |
| none | fixed 0.5 | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 256 |
| none | CVD | 5,780 | 0 | 0 | 180 |

(Arrows are onsets; the held condition would count 703/709/37 on `"small"`
and 290/293/186 on `"none"`.) Acceptance holds: auto matches fixed on
`"large"` (100 of 100) with no more off-label arrows on `"small"` (488 against
490) and `"none"` (256 against 256), and beats the candle delta on its own
regime (100 hits against 80, 0 off-label against 171). What the split shows that candles hide: on the
`"large"` regime the candle delta misses one planted event in five and draws
171 arrows the rule does not support, because the small prints' opposite flow
is folded into one number. The off-label counts on `"small"` are the rule
reading correctly a regime built so that price follows the small tier: the
large tier lags most extremes there by construction.

`whaleVersusCrowd()` in fixed mode with `tierSize` 1.0: the single-large-print
tape reads `largeBar +50, smallBar 0`, the hundred-print tape `0, +50`, the
candle is identical and the candle delta reads +50 on both. The 10,000
aggregate trades: five full minutes of 1,199 to 2,345 aggregates each, the auto
tier after each commit equal to the test's nearest-rank p98 of the same ring
(0.0963 to 0.106 BTC), tape share 1 within 1e-9, tier totals equal to the signed
sum.

## Marker budget

Mechanism fixture at default settings: 7 markers over 300 bars (23 per 1,000),
at most one on a bar; with conflict circles on, 24 over 300. The protocol's
busy instrument (`trendBars(1000)` with `busyTapeFor` at 60 prints a bar)
measures 59 onset arrows per 1,000 bars at defaults (66 before the onset
rule), above the 40 per 1,000 ceiling for a signal tool. That fixture closes
above its previous 20 closes on 980 of 980 bars, so the ring high is almost
always the previous bar and the divergence condition reduces to
`largeBar <= -m · mabsL` for the bar itself: a random large tier meets it on
about one bar in seventeen and rarely on two bars running, so the onset rule
removes few of them. The number is reported, not hidden; the PM decides.

## Performance and bounded state

Frozen method (`probeReplayPooled`, three warm-ups, five timed runs, `onBar`
plus `readOutput`) on 10,080 trend bars with 20 prints each in one store of
201,600 prints: pooled p99 0.038 ms (p50 0.011); real activation p99 0.035 ms,
wall 150 ms; forming bar in fixed mode, 3,000 revisions over 1,000 prints, p99
0.047 ms; rollover of 1,000 extra bars: every output key 10,080 long, no stale
marker, 412 bytes per extra bar with gc exposed (the harness floor is about
370). Measured on the final build with the machine otherwise idle; the test
asserts pooled p99 under 0.5 ms and forming p99 at or under 0.1 ms. State: a ring of 10,080 committed bars of seven fields (under 1 MB), two
32 KB typed arrays, the `K`-ring, two totals, and on the forming bar only its
last revision's sizes (at most 4,096).

## Limitations

Print size is not participant size; the tier follows the delivered trade
frame. Lines start at the tape start (about 2.6 hours back on BTC-USDT at
most) and never in Historical Mode or on the REST transport. A bar superseded
without a close keeps its last displayed values until a rebuild. Equities show
sided flow only and are `null` under the share gate on most bars.
Cumulative-since-anchor is a presentation choice with no claimed benefit over
a per-bar view; a per-bar histogram display mode is v2. The `week` anchor is
epoch-aligned (Thursday 00:00 UTC). No hover tooltip. Descriptive; no forward
claim.

## Tests

`tests/sizeSplitDeltaMath.test.ts` (the hand-computed three-bar fixture, the
size ring against the reference quantile, the window scan, the share cases,
both commit paths, the anchor reset, gaps, the marker rule, formatting),
`tests/sizeSplitDelta.test.ts` (identity, policy, schema, table copy, markers
through a hand-built ctx), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaValidation.test.ts` (protocol
checks 1 to 4 and 9, the forming prefix, the late-print supersession, the
reconnect, the retention equivalence), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts`
(checks 5 and 7, the acceptance table, whale versus crowd, the aggregate
fixture), `tests/sizeSplitDeltaPerf.test.ts` (check 6). Run with
`NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc npx vitest run src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta`.

`-zoomed-out` and a `-zoomed-in` pair taken from the same reset view.

codex
The close and supersession paths appear to preserve the stored threshold and last displayed flow as specified. I found two points to check further: arrows fire only on divergence onset, although the supplied brief limits only conflict markers that way; and the docs report marker counts above the acceptance budget.

+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import { gunzipSync } from "node:zlib";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import type { BarFrame, TradeFrame } from "../../../../types/frames";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import {
+  SIZE_SPLIT_REGIME_SEEDS,
+  busyTapeFor,
+  countMarkers,
+  judgeBudget,
+  referenceClassify,
+  referenceMarkers,
+  referenceNearestRank,
+  referenceScore,
+  replayCapture,

+  summarizeBudget,

+  type SizeSplitPriceFollows,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { barSides } from "../../barSides";
+import { SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, type SizeSplitDeltaSettings } from "../sizeSplitDelta";
+import { SIZE_RING_CAPACITY, TIER_MIN_SIZES, createSizeSplitEngine } from "../sizeSplitDeltaMath";
+import { cvdDivergenceBaseline, fixtureTicks, markerIndices, withTapeVolume } from "./helpers";
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
