+/**
+ * Protocol checks 5 and 7 and the brief's Acceptance, on the frozen fixtures:
+ * the indicator in `auto` mode against the same indicator in `fixed` mode (the
+ * one controlled comparison, identical detector) and against the `orderFlow`
+ * CVD from `barSides` with the same divergence rule ("what candles alone
+ * show"). Truth comes from the generators' hidden state; every arrow the
+ * detector draws is also checked against the independent reference
+ * implementation of the rule (amendment 8). Numbers are pinned here and
+ * quoted in the doc page.
+ */
+
+const AUTO: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, anchor: "tape", showConflict: true, showBarLines: true };
+const FIXED: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 0.5 };
+const RULE = { K: 20, m: 2, q: 2 };
+const MECHANISM = sizeSplitFixture();
+
+interface Run {
+  bearish: number[];
+  bullish: number[];
+  circles: number[];
+  mask: Set<number>;
+  outputs: Readonly<Record<string, readonly (number | null)[]>>;
+  reference: ReturnType<typeof referenceMarkers>;
+}
+
+function runIndicator(fixture: SizeSplitFixture, settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings): Run {
+  const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, fixture.bars, settings, { ticks: fixtureTicks(fixture) });
+  expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
+  const reference = referenceMarkers(
+    fixture.bars.map((bar, index) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: capture.outputs.largeBar[index], smallBar: capture.outputs.smallBar[index] })),
+    RULE,
+  );
+  return {
+    bearish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowDown"),
+    bullish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowUp"),
+    circles: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "circle"),
+    mask: new Set(reference.evaluated),
+    outputs: capture.outputs,
+    reference,
+  };
+}
+
+function runCvd(fixture: SizeSplitFixture): { bearish: number[]; bullish: number[]; reference: ReturnType<typeof referenceMarkers> } {
+  const capture = replayCapture(cvdDivergenceBaseline, fixture.bars, { K: RULE.K, m: RULE.m });
+  const reference = referenceMarkers(fixture.bars.map((bar) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: barSides(bar).delta, smallBar: 0 })), RULE);
+  return { bearish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowDown"), bullish: markerIndices(capture.markers, fixture.bars, "arrowUp"), reference };
+}
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- mechanism fixture (frozen, seed 20261102)", () => {
+  const auto = runIndicator(MECHANISM, AUTO);
+  const fixed = runIndicator(MECHANISM, FIXED);
+  const cvd = runCvd(MECHANISM);
+
+  it("the fixture is the frozen one: 300 bars, 100 prints each, 8 planted divergences at 45 + 30 j, exact tape volume", () => {
+    expect(MECHANISM.bars).toHaveLength(300);
+    expect(MECHANISM.prints).toHaveLength(30_000);
+    expect(MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices).toEqual([45, 75, 105, 135, 165, 195, 225, 255]);
+    expect(MECHANISM.labels.direction).toEqual([-1, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1]);
+    for (let index = 0; index < MECHANISM.bars.length; index += 1) {
+      const prints = MECHANISM.printsByBar[index];
+      expect(prints).toHaveLength(100);
+      for (let k = 1; k < prints.length; k += 1) expect(prints[k].ts_exchange).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(prints[k - 1].ts_exchange);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].close).toBe(prints[prints.length - 1].price);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].open).toBe(prints[0].price);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[index].volume).toBe(prints.reduce((sum, print) => sum + print.size, 0));
+    }
+    for (const [position, p] of MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices.entries()) {
+      const d = MECHANISM.labels.direction[position];
+      const window = MECHANISM.bars.slice(p - 20, p).map((bar) => bar.close);
+      expect(MECHANISM.bars[p].close).toBe(d > 0 ? Math.max(...window) + 30 : Math.min(...window) - 30);
+      for (let back = 0; back <= 2; back += 1) for (const print of MECHANISM.printsByBar[p - back].filter((print) => print.size === 1)) expect(print.side).toBe(d > 0 ? "sell" : "buy");
+    }
+  });
+
+  it("the auto tier equals the nearest-rank p98 of the ring the fixture actually produces on every bar after the warm-up, and the contributions equal the reference classification with the boundary print large (amendment 6)", () => {
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "auto", tierSize: 0.5, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+    const ticks = fixtureTicks(MECHANISM);
+    const ring: number[] = [];
+    let firstTier = -1;
+    MECHANISM.bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar, ticks);
+      const expectedTier = ring.length < TIER_MIN_SIZES ? null : referenceNearestRank(ring.slice(-SIZE_RING_CAPACITY));
+      expect(reading.tier).toBe(expectedTier);
+      if (expectedTier !== null && firstTier < 0) firstTier = index;
+      const expected = referenceClassify(MECHANISM.printsByBar[index], bar.ts_open, bar.ts_open + bar.interval_ms, expectedTier);
+      expect(reading.largeBar).toBe(expected.largeBar);
+      expect(reading.smallBar).toBe(expected.smallBar);
+      expect(auto.outputs.largeBar[index]).toBe(expected.largeBar);
+      for (const print of MECHANISM.printsByBar[index]) ring.push(print.size);
+    });
+    expect(firstTier).toBe(2);
+    // With fewer than 4,096 sizes the p98 lands on the largest small print, which then classifies large; with a full ring it is 1.0.
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[2]).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(engine.committed[41].tierAtOpen).toBe(1);
+    expect(engine.committed[2].tierAtOpen).toBeLessThan(0.05);
+  });
+
+  it("pins the arrows: auto and fixed hit all 8 planted bars exactly with no off-label arrow; every arrow equals the reference rule's; the planted bars are the condition's onsets", () => {
+    expect(auto.reference.bearishHeld).toEqual(auto.reference.bearish);
+    expect(auto.reference.bullishHeld).toEqual(auto.reference.bullish);
+    expect(auto.bearish).toEqual([75, 135, 195, 255]);
+    expect(auto.bullish).toEqual([45, 105, 165, 225]);
+    expect(auto.reference.bearish).toEqual(auto.bearish);
+    expect(auto.reference.bullish).toEqual(auto.bullish);
+    expect(fixed.bearish).toEqual(auto.bearish);
+    expect(fixed.bullish).toEqual(auto.bullish);
+    expect(referenceScore(auto, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask)).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 0, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+    expect(referenceScore(fixed, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, fixed.mask)).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 0, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+  });
+
+  it("pins the conflict onsets to the count the reference implementation produces (17), on the same bars", () => {
+    expect(auto.reference.conflictOnsets).toEqual([22, 50, 54, 56, 59, 78, 109, 111, 114, 117, 139, 175, 200, 232, 236, 257, 285]);
+    expect(auto.circles).toEqual(auto.reference.conflictOnsets);
+    expect(auto.circles).toHaveLength(17);
+  });
+
+  it("measured on the CVD baseline (amendment 9): the candle delta also hits all 8 planted bars, with 5 off-label arrows the split does not draw; the CVD def equals the reference rule", () => {
+    expect(cvd.bearish).toEqual(cvd.reference.bearish);
+    expect(cvd.bullish).toEqual(cvd.reference.bullish);
+    expect(cvd.bearish).toEqual([75, 135, 190, 195, 255]);
+    expect(cvd.bullish).toEqual([42, 45, 56, 105, 165, 222, 225, 288]);
+    const score = referenceScore(cvd, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask);
+    expect(score).toEqual({ planted: 8, hits: 8, offLabel: 5, plantedEvaluable: 8 });
+    // Acceptance: recall >= 7/8 on bar p exactly; false divergences <= 2 and <= the CVD baseline's.
+    expect(referenceScore(auto, MECHANISM.labels.plantedIndices, MECHANISM.labels.direction, auto.mask).offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Math.min(2, score.offLabel));
+  });
+
+  it("marker budget at default settings (conflict circles off, day anchor) is within 40 per 1,000 on the mechanism fixture", () => {
+    const budget = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, MECHANISM.bars, SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(MECHANISM) } });
+    const verdict = judgeBudget(budget, 40, 2);
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta mechanism fixture, defaults", budget, verdict));
+    // Seven, not eight: the default day anchor resets at the UTC midnight between bars 213 and 214, and planted bar 225 has no full ring yet.
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(7);
+    expect(verdict.ok).toBe(true);
+    const withConflict = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, MECHANISM.bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showConflict: true }, { extras: { ticks: fixtureTicks(MECHANISM) } });
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta mechanism fixture, conflict circles on", withConflict, judgeBudget(withConflict, 40, 2)));
+    expect(withConflict.total).toBe(budget.total + (withConflict.byShape.circle ?? 0));
+    expect(withConflict.byShape.circle ?? 0).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(withConflict.maxPerBar).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
+  });
+});
+
+interface RegimeTotals {
+  hits: number;
+  offLabel: number;
+  eligible: number;
+  planted: number;
+}
+
+function regimeTable(priceFollows: SizeSplitPriceFollows): { auto: RegimeTotals; fixed: RegimeTotals; cvd: RegimeTotals; arrowsEqualReference: boolean } {
+  const totals = { auto: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 }, fixed: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 }, cvd: { hits: 0, offLabel: 0, eligible: 0, planted: 0 } };
+  let arrowsEqualReference = true;
+  for (const seed of SIZE_SPLIT_REGIME_SEEDS) {
+    const fixture = sizeSplitRegimeBars({ seed, priceFollows });
+    const auto = runIndicator(fixture, AUTO);
+    const fixed = runIndicator(fixture, FIXED);
+    const cvd = runCvd(fixture);
+    // The common evaluation mask: bars where both configurations have values and a full ring (amendment 8).
+    const mask = new Set([...auto.mask].filter((index) => fixed.mask.has(index)));
+    for (const [name, run] of [["auto", auto], ["fixed", fixed], ["cvd", cvd]] as const) {
+      const score = referenceScore(run, fixture.labels.plantedIndices, fixture.labels.direction, mask);
+      totals[name].hits += score.hits;
+      totals[name].offLabel += score.offLabel;
+      totals[name].eligible += mask.size;
+      totals[name].planted += score.plantedEvaluable;
+      if (JSON.stringify(run.bearish) !== JSON.stringify(run.reference.bearish) || JSON.stringify(run.bullish) !== JSON.stringify(run.reference.bullish)) arrowsEqualReference = false;
+    }
+  }
+  return { ...totals, arrowsEqualReference };
+}
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- regime fixtures, 10 seeds each (Acceptance, round 3 rulings 7 and 8)", () => {
+  const large = regimeTable("large");
+  const small = regimeTable("small");
+  const none = regimeTable("none");
+
+  it("prints the acceptance table", () => {
+    const row = (regime: string, name: string, totals: RegimeTotals) => `| ${regime} | ${name} | ${totals.eligible} | ${totals.planted} | ${totals.hits} | ${totals.offLabel} |`;
+    console.log(
+      [
+        "| regime | configuration | eligible bars | planted | hits | off-label arrows |",
+        "|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
+        row("large", "auto", large.auto),
+        row("large", "fixed 0.5", large.fixed),
+        row("large", "CVD", large.cvd),
+        row("small", "auto", small.auto),
+        row("small", "fixed 0.5", small.fixed),
+        row("small", "CVD", small.cvd),
+        row("none", "auto", none.auto),
+        row("none", "fixed 0.5", none.fixed),
+        row("none", "CVD", none.cvd),
+      ].join("\n"),
+    );
+    expect(large.arrowsEqualReference && small.arrowsEqualReference && none.arrowsEqualReference).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it("pins the published counts over 10 seeds", () => {
+    expect(large.auto).toEqual({ hits: 100, offLabel: 0, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    expect(large.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 100, offLabel: 0, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    expect(large.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 80, offLabel: 171, eligible: 5_780, planted: 100 });
+    // Onset-only arrows (PM amendment to rule 11): the held condition would count 703/709/37 on small and 290/293/186 on none.
+    expect(small.auto).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 488, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(small.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 490, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(small.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 37, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.auto).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 256, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.fixed).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 256, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+    expect(none.cvd).toEqual({ hits: 0, offLabel: 180, eligible: 5_780, planted: 0 });
+  });
+
+  it("acceptance: auto beats or matches fixed (no more false markers on small and none at equal or better recall on large); the split beats the candle delta on its own regime", () => {
+    expect(large.auto.hits).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(large.fixed.hits);
+    expect(small.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(small.fixed.offLabel);
+    expect(none.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThanOrEqual(none.fixed.offLabel);
+    expect(large.auto.hits).toBeGreaterThan(large.cvd.hits);
+    expect(large.auto.offLabel).toBeLessThan(large.cvd.offLabel);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- whale versus crowd (protocol check 7) in fixed mode, tier 1.0 (round 3 ruling 9)", () => {
+  it("responds differently to the two tapes that make the same candle while the candle delta cannot", () => {
+    const fixture = whaleVersusCrowd({ whaleSize: 50, crowdCount: 100 });
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...FIXED, tierSize: 1 };
+    const whale = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], settings, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.whaleTape) });
+    const crowd = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], settings, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.crowdTape) });
+    expect(whale.outputs.largeBar[0]).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(whale.outputs.smallBar[0]).toBe(0);
+    expect(crowd.outputs.largeBar[0]).toBe(0);
+    expect(crowd.outputs.smallBar[0]).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(fixture.candle.trade_count).toBeNull();
+    expect(barSides(fixture.whaleCandle).delta).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    expect(barSides(fixture.crowdCandle).delta).toBeCloseTo(50, 9);
+    // In auto mode an isolated tape cannot classify before 200 sizes: honest null, not a guess.
+    const auto = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [fixture.candle], AUTO, { ticks: ticksOf(fixture.whaleTape) });
+    expect(auto.outputs.large[0]).toBeNull();
+  });
+});

+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- 10,000 real BTC-USDT aggregate trades (recorded 2026-10-26)", () => {
+  const BINANCE_SPOT: FixtureIdentity = { provider: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT", symbol: "BTC-USDT" };
+
+  function loadAggregates(): TradeFrame[] {
+    const file = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "../../../../workers/tests/fixtures/binance-mirror/aggtrades-btcusdt-10k.json.gz");
+    const rows = JSON.parse(gunzipSync(readFileSync(file)).toString("utf8")) as [number, string, string, number, number][];
+    return rows.map(([id, price, quantity, time, maker]) => ({ type: "trade", ...BINANCE_SPOT, ts_exchange: time, price: Number(price), size: Number(quantity), side: maker ? "sell" : "buy", trade_id: `a${id}` }) as TradeFrame);
+  }
+
+  it("the auto tier after each commit equals the test's nearest-rank p98 of the same ring, the share is 1 within 1e-9 and the tier totals equal the signed sum", () => {
+    const prints = loadAggregates();
+    expect(prints).toHaveLength(10_000);
+    const firstMinute = Math.ceil(prints[0].ts_exchange / 60_000) * 60_000;
+    const lastMinute = Math.floor(prints[prints.length - 1].ts_exchange / 60_000) * 60_000;
+    const bars: BarFrame[] = [];
+    const byBar: TradeFrame[][] = [];
+    for (let tsOpen = firstMinute; tsOpen < lastMinute; tsOpen += 60_000) {
+      const inside = prints.filter((print) => print.ts_exchange >= tsOpen && print.ts_exchange < tsOpen + 60_000);
+      let volume = 0;
+      let buy = 0;
+      for (const print of inside) {
+        volume += print.size;
+        if (print.side === "buy") buy += print.size;
+      }
+      bars.push({ type: "bar", ...BINANCE_SPOT, interval_ms: 60_000, ts_open: tsOpen, open: inside[0].price, high: Math.max(...inside.map((print) => print.price)), low: Math.min(...inside.map((print) => print.price)), close: inside[inside.length - 1].price, volume, buy_volume: buy, sell_volume: volume - buy, trade_count: inside.length, closed: true } as BarFrame);
+      byBar.push(inside);
+    }
+    expect(bars).toHaveLength(5);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, firstMinute));
+    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "auto", tierSize: 0.5, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
+    const ring: number[] = [];
+    let signed = 0;
+    let tiered = 0;
+    bars.forEach((bar, index) => {
+      const reading = engine.onBar(bar, ticks);
+      const expectedTier = ring.length < TIER_MIN_SIZES ? null : referenceNearestRank(ring.slice(-SIZE_RING_CAPACITY));
+      expect(reading.tier).toBe(expectedTier);
+      expect(reading.share).toBeCloseTo(1, 9);
+      if (index > 0) {
+        expect(expectedTier).not.toBeNull();
+        expect(reading.valid).toBe(true);
+        tiered += (reading.largeBar as number) + (reading.smallBar as number);
+        for (const print of byBar[index]) signed += print.side === "buy" ? print.size : -print.size;
+      }
+      for (const print of byBar[index]) ring.push(print.size);
+    });
+    expect(tiered).toBeCloseTo(signed, 9);
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta aggregates: ${bars.length} full minutes, ${bars.map((bar) => bar.trade_count).join("/")} aggregates per minute, tiers ${engine.committed.map((bar) => (bar.tierAtOpen === null ? "null" : bar.tierAtOpen.toFixed(5))).join(", ")}`);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- marker budget on the protocol's busy instrument (check 5)", () => {
+  it("reports the budget on trendBars with busyTapeFor at default settings with onset-only arrows: 59 per 1,000, still above the 40 signal ceiling, because on a monotone trend the ring high is the previous bar and the condition rarely persists (PM decision)", () => {
+    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: 1000 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 60 });
+    const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []), bars[0].ts_open));
+    const budget = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, { extras: { ticks } });
+    const verdict = judgeBudget(budget, 40, 2);
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta trendBars(1000) + busyTapeFor(60), defaults", budget, verdict));
+    expect(budget.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.total).toBe(59);
+    expect(budget.maxPerBar).toBe(1);
+    // How often the condition persists from one bar to the next on this fixture: the onset rule can only remove those bars.
+    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, showBarLines: true }, { ticks });
+    const reference = referenceMarkers(bars.map((bar, index) => ({ close: bar.close, largeBar: capture.outputs.largeBar[index], smallBar: capture.outputs.smallBar[index] })), RULE);
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta trendBars: condition held on ${reference.bearishHeld.length + reference.bullishHeld.length} bars, onsets ${reference.bearish.length + reference.bullish.length}`);
+    expect(reference.bearish.length + reference.bullish.length).toBe(59);
+    const deeper = countMarkers(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, m: 3 }, { extras: { ticks } });
+    console.log(summarizeBudget("sizeSplitDelta same instrument, m = 3", deeper, judgeBudget(deeper, 40, 2)));
+    const newHighs = bars.filter((bar, index) => index >= 20 && bar.close > Math.max(...bars.slice(index - 20, index).map((earlier) => earlier.close))).length;
+    console.log(`sizeSplitDelta trendBars: ${newHighs} of ${bars.length - 20} bars close above their previous 20 closes`);
+    expect(newHighs).toBeGreaterThan(500);

+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS } from "../../../chart/candleLifecycle";
+import { resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/creationScope";
+import { resetDrawRegistryForTests } from "../../../chart/draw/registry";
+import {
+  busyTapeFor,
+  formatFormingBarProbe,
+  formatPooledReplayProbe,
+  formatReplayProbe,
+  formatRolloverProbe,
+  probeFormingBar,
+  probeReplay,
+  probeReplayPooled,
+  probeRollover,
+  tapeOf,
+  ticksOf,
+  trendBars,
+} from "../../../chart/validation";
+import { SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, type SizeSplitDeltaSettings } from "../sizeSplitDelta";
+import { withTapeVolume } from "./helpers";

+afterEach(() => {
+  resetDrawRegistryForTests();
+  resetDrawOwnerScopeForTests();
+});
+
+/**
+ * Protocol check 6 with the brief's frozen method: `probeReplayPooled` (three
+ * untimed warm-ups, five timed runs, `onBar` plus `readOutput`) on 10,080
+ * trend bars whose 20 prints each sit in one store of 201,600 prints (under
+ * the tape's cap, so every window is `"all"`); `probeReplay` for the real
+ * activation; `probeFormingBar` in fixed mode so the whole classification path
+ * runs on 1,000 prints revised 50 times a second; `probeRollover` for the
+ * bounded state. Set SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT=<file> to keep the JSON.
+ */
+
+const POOLED_P99_CEILING_MS = 0.5;
+const FORMING_P99_CEILING_MS = 0.1;
+
+describe("sizeSplitDelta -- performance (protocol check 6, brief Performance)", () => {
+  it("pooled p99 under 0.5 ms, forming p99 under 0.1 ms, replay wall under 5 s, every output key 10,080 long after the rollover with flat heap growth", () => {
+    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, driftPerBar: 0.5, noise: 25 });
+    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 20 });
+    const bars = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
+    const prints = bars.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []);
+    expect(prints).toHaveLength(201_600);
+    const ticks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, bars[0].ts_open));
+    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...SIZE_SPLIT_DEFAULT_SETTINGS, anchor: "tape" };
+
+    const pooled = probeReplayPooled(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { warmupRuns: 3, timedRuns: 5, extras: { ticks } });
+    const replay = probeReplay(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { ticks });
+    const rollover = probeRollover(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, settings, { extraBars: 1_000, extras: { ticks } });
+    const fixed: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...settings, tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 0.5 };
+    const forming = probeFormingBar(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars.slice(-300), fixed, { seconds: 60, revisionsPerSecond: 50, printsPerMinute: 1_000 });
+
+    const lines = [
+      formatPooledReplayProbe(pooled),
+      formatReplayProbe(replay),
+      formatFormingBarProbe(forming),
+      `sizeSplitDelta p99 side by side: replay (pooled) ${pooled.pooled.p99.toFixed(4)} ms, forming bar ${forming.onBar.p99.toFixed(4)} ms`,
+      formatRolloverProbe(rollover),
+    ];
+    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
+    if (process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT) {
+      mkdirSync(dirname(process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT), { recursive: true });
+      writeFileSync(process.env.SIZE_SPLIT_PERF_OUT, `${JSON.stringify({ node: process.version, pooled, replay, forming, rollover }, null, 2)}\n`);
+    }
+
+    expect(pooled.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.count).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS * 5);
+    expect(pooled.pooled.p99).toBeLessThan(POOLED_P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(replay.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(replay.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
+    expect(replay.wallMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5_000);
+    expect(forming.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(forming.revisions).toBe(3_000);
+    expect(forming.prints).toBe(1_000);
+    expect(forming.onBar.p99).toBeLessThanOrEqual(FORMING_P99_CEILING_MS);
+    expect(rollover.faulted).toBe(false);
+    expect(rollover.retainedBars).toBe(MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS);
+    expect(rollover.outputLengths).toEqual({ large: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, small: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, largeBar: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS, smallBar: MAX_RETAINED_SOURCE_BARS });
+    expect(rollover.markersBeforeRetention).toBe(0);
+    // The heap slope is meaningful only under NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc; without it the probe says so and the number is reported, not judged.
+    if (rollover.heap.gcExposed) expect(rollover.bytesPerExtraBar).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3 * 370);
+    else console.log("sizeSplitDelta rollover heap slope not judged: gc not exposed");
+  }, 600_000);
+});

# Validation protocol (cycle 1)

Owner: validation and performance specialist. Applies to every indicator that
reaches step 6 of the pipeline in `PM/CHARTER.md`. The harness that runs these
checks lives on the repository at `apps/web/src/lib/chart/validation/`
(import from `./validation/index.ts`); each utility has its own proving test in
`apps/web/src/lib/chart/validation/tests/`. A check is not passed because the
specialist says so; it is passed because a vitest file in the indicator's
`tests/` folder (or a validation spec next to it) runs the check and the
evidence file named in the report records the result.

Vocabulary: **replay** is the runtime's `onSnapshot`/activation pass over the
retained bars (`replaying: true`, no `trades`, `HISTORICAL_UNAVAILABLE`);
**live** is bars arriving one at a time through `onBar` as `APPENDED`, then
`REPLACED_EDGE` for every forming revision, then `REPLACED_EDGE` once more when
the bar closes. The harness classifies arrivals with the chart's own
`mergeChartBar`, so a duplicated closed bar is a `REPLACED_EDGE` and a late bar
is a `REBUILT`, exactly as `ChartPanel` would dispatch them.

## How to run the harness

```bash
export PATH=/root/.nvm/versions/node/v24.21.0/bin:$PATH
cd apps/web
npx vitest run src/lib/chart/validation          # the harness proves itself (84 tests)
npx vitest run src/lib/indicators/<folder>       # the indicator's own checks
VALIDATION_BASELINES=1 NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc npx vitest run src/lib/chart/validation/tests/baselines.test.ts
```

Heap numbers are meaningful only under `NODE_OPTIONS=--expose-gc` (vitest's
workers inherit it); every probe result carries `heap.gcExposed` so the evidence
says which kind it is.

| Utility | File | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `trendBars`, `rangeBars`, `compressionBreakoutBars`, `volumeSpikeBars`, `timeGapBars`, `equityUnsidedBars` | `fixtures.ts` | labelled synthetic regimes, deterministic by seed |
| `relativeVolumeEquityFixture`, `relativeVolumeCryptoFixture`, `mulberry32`, `standardNormals` | `fixtures.ts` | the frozen Relative Volume fixtures of `PM/cycle1/brief-rvol-clock.md` (15-session equity U-curve with 12 labelled anomalies and predictable slots; 12,000-bar crypto cosine day), shared by the builder and the validation loop |
| `formingRevisions`, `liveArrivals`, `duplicateClosedArrivals`, `rebuiltArrivals`, `sampledIndices` | `fixtures.ts` | arrival scripts for the live driver |
| `openLiveSession`, `driveLive`, `replayCapture`, `diffOutputs`, `diffMarkers` | `liveDrive.ts` | the real `indicatorRuntime` driven the way the chart drives it, with the rollover |
| `checkTruncationInvariance` | `truncation.ts` | check 1 |
| `checkLiveReplayEquivalence`, `checkDuplicateClosedIdempotent`, `checkRebuiltEquivalence`, `checkArrivalsAgainstReplay` | `liveEquivalence.ts` | checks 2 and 3 |
| `countMarkers`, `countMarkersOver`, `markerBudgetOf`, `judgeBudget`, `captureMarkers` | `markerBudget.ts` | check 5 |
| `whaleVersusCrowd`, `busyTapeFor`, `tapeOf`, `historyTapeOf`, `ticksOf`, `candleOf` | `syntheticTape.ts` | real `AggressiveTape`s for L1 checks and the whale-versus-crowd baseline |
| `fakeL2`, `fakeL2Unsynced`, `segmentOf`, `ladderOf`, `ladderWithWall`, `wallOf`, `wallLifecycle`, `absorbingAt`, `tradeEvent` | `fakeL2.ts` | a scripted `IndicatorL2` whose trust, segment and event-slice semantics mirror `indicatorL2Of`, `L2Store.trusted` and `EventRing.since`; `atTime` cuts the ladder, walls and events at a bar close. A wall under a cut is served from a timestamped snapshot or rebuilt from its `wall_born`/`wall_trade` events, with `absorbing` by the runtime's rule (traded into within 5 s, holding half its peak); a wall with neither is omitted, never served at its final size |
| `probeReplay`, `probeFormingBar`, `probeRollover`, `probeReplayPooled` | `perfProbe.ts` | check 6; `probeReplayPooled` is the brief's frozen method (warm-ups, pooled timed runs, `onBar` plus `readOutput` only) |

## The checks

Each check states the method, the pass criterion and how the evidence is
recorded. "Evidence file" means a vitest file committed on the indicator's
branch whose test names match the check, plus any JSON or log the test writes
(kept under `PM/log/validation/<indicator>/`).

### 1. No future data

Method. `checkTruncationInvariance(def, bars, settings)` replays the fixture
once in full, then once truncated at every index (`sampledIndices` for series
longer than 1,000 bars: every 25th index plus the last 50), and compares the
committed outputs of every line and every marker at or before the cut. Run it
on at least `compressionBreakoutBars()` and `trendBars()`; tick and book
indicators also run it with the fixture's `extras` (`ticks` from `tapeOf`, `l2`
from `fakeL2` with `atTime` cutting walls and events at the bar close).

Pass. `report.ok === true`: zero output disagreements at tolerance 0 (a
documented floating-point tolerance is acceptable only for a value computed in
a different order live versus replay, never for a different value), zero marker
disagreements, no fault. An indicator that reads `barIndex`/`barCount` to place
anything is suspect by default and must show this check passing.

Evidence. Test named `no future data: truncation invariance` in the
indicator's tests; `summarizeTruncation(report)` written to the log on failure.

### 2. Replay versus live equivalence

Method. `checkLiveReplayEquivalence(def, bars, settings, { revisionsPerBar: 5 })`
feeds every bar as five forming revisions (`closed: false`, path open through
the wicks to the close, volume growing with buy, sell and unknown volume scaled
independently through `barSides`, so an equity bar's unsided share stays
unsided) followed by the closed bar,
through the real runtime, and compares the committed outputs and markers with a
clean replay of the same bars. For a tick or book indicator supply `tradesFor`
and `extras` so the live side sees what the terminal would give it.

Pass. Either `report.equal === true`, or the indicator's doc page states the
exact difference and the test pins it: for a tick-only indicator the expected
and accepted difference is `outputsEqual === true`, replay markers `[]`, live
markers present (`largeTradeBubbles` is the reference: see
`validation/tests/liveEquivalence.test.ts`). Any output difference on a
candle-only indicator fails; it means forming revisions were committed (the
harness catches this with a running sum that ignores `replace`).

Evidence. Test named `replay versus live equivalence`; the documented
difference quoted in the indicator's doc page under "Replay and Historical
Mode".

### 3. Gap, duplicate and late-event behavior

Method, five parts, all through the live driver so the runtime's own
classification is what the indicator sees:

  }, 60_000);
});

describe("sizeSplitDelta -- warm-up, empty input and NaN (protocol check 4)", () => {
  it("auto mode is null until 200 trade frames from complete windows have been committed, then values appear at the next bar; fixed mode values from the first bar; never NaN or a fabricated zero", () => {
    const auto = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 10), AUTO, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
    expect(auto.outputs.large[0]).toBeNull();
    expect(auto.outputs.large[1]).toBeNull();
    expect(auto.outputs.large[2]).not.toBeNull();
    expect(FIXTURE.printsByBar[0].length + FIXTURE.printsByBar[1].length).toBe(TIER_MIN_SIZES);
    const fixed = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 10), FIXED, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
    expect(fixed.outputs.large[0]).not.toBeNull();
    noNaN(auto.outputs);
    noNaN(fixed.outputs);
    const shorter = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 2), AUTO, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
    expect(shorter.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("handles an empty bar array and a store without prints", () => {
    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, [], AUTO, { ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf([], FIXTURE_T0)) });
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(capture.outputs.large).toEqual([]);
    const empty = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars.slice(0, 5), FIXED, { ticks: ticksOf(tapeOf([], FIXTURE_T0)) });
    expect(empty.faulted).toBe(false);
    expect(empty.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("sizeSplitDelta -- equity tape share (protocol check 9, rule 5)", () => {
  it("draws when the sided tape covers 60 percent of the candle volume and is null with the share row at 40 percent; candle sides are never read", () => {
    const { bars: raw } = trendBars({ count: 120, identity: DXFEED_EQUITY, startPrice: 190, driftPerBar: 0.02, noise: 0.3 });
    const printsByBar = busyTapeFor(raw, { printsPerBar: 50, identity: DXFEED_EQUITY, smallSize: 10 });
    const taped = withTapeVolume(raw, printsByBar);
    const prints = taped.flatMap((bar) => printsByBar.get(bar.ts_open) ?? []);
    const equityTicks = ticksOf(tapeOf(prints, taped[0].ts_open));
    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...FIXED, tierSize: 50 };
    const sixty = taped.map((bar) => ({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume / 0.6, buy_volume: undefined, sell_volume: undefined }) as BarFrame);
    const forty = taped.map((bar) => ({ ...bar, volume: bar.volume / 0.4, buy_volume: undefined, sell_volume: undefined }) as BarFrame);
    const ok = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, sixty, settings, { ticks: equityTicks });
    expect(ok.outputs.large.every((value) => value !== null)).toBe(true);
    const low = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, forty, settings, { ticks: equityTicks });
    expect(low.outputs.large.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(low.markers).toEqual([]);
    const engine = createSizeSplitEngine({ tierMode: "fixed", tierSize: 50, anchor: "tape", K: 20, m: 2, q: 2, minTapeShare: 0.5 });
    const readingLow = engine.onBar(forty[0], equityTicks);
    expect(readingLow.status).toBe("shareLow");
    expect(readingLow.share).toBeCloseTo(0.4, 9);
  });

  it("equityUnsidedBars without a tape is null throughout, whatever the candle sides say", () => {
    const { bars } = equityUnsidedBars();
    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, bars, FIXED);
    expect(capture.faulted).toBe(false);
    for (const values of Object.values(capture.outputs)) expect(values.every((value) => value === null)).toBe(true);
    expect(capture.markers).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("sizeSplitDelta -- anchor rollover on the fixture (rule 8, round 3 ruling 3)", () => {
  it("the first bar of the new UTC day shows only its own contribution, the ring is empty and no marker crosses the reset", () => {
    const settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings = { ...AUTO, anchor: "day" };
    const capture = replayCapture(sizeSplitDeltaIndicator, FIXTURE.bars, settings, { ticks: fixtureTicks(FIXTURE) });
    const first = FIXTURE.bars.findIndex((bar, index) => index > 0 && anchorStartFor(bar.ts_open, "day") !== anchorStartFor(FIXTURE.bars[index - 1].ts_open, "day"));
    expect(first).toBe(214);
    expect(capture.outputs.large[first]).toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.largeBar[first] as number, 9);
    expect(capture.outputs.small[first]).toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.smallBar[first] as number, 9);
    expect(capture.outputs.large[first - 1]).not.toBeCloseTo(capture.outputs.largeBar[first - 1] as number, 3);
    const arrows = [...markerIndices(capture.markers, FIXTURE.bars, "arrowDown"), ...markerIndices(capture.markers, FIXTURE.bars, "arrowUp")];
    expect(arrows.filter((index) => index >= first && index < first + 20)).toEqual([]);
    expect(arrows.filter((index) => index >= first + 20).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

+function syntheticBars(count, lastTsOpen, startPrice) {
+  const bars = [];
+  let price = startPrice;
+  for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
+    const regime = Math.sin(i / 55) * 18;
+    const open = price;
+    const close = Math.round((open + regime + (random() - 0.5) * 70) * 10) / 10;
+    const high = Math.max(open, close) + random() * 25;
+    const low = Math.min(open, close) - random() * 25;
+    const volume = Math.round((4 + random() * 20 + Math.abs(close - open) / 10) * 1000) / 1000;
+    const buy = Math.round(volume * (close >= open ? 0.52 + random() * 0.2 : 0.28 + random() * 0.2) * 1000) / 1000;
+    bars.push({
+      type: "bar", ...identity, interval_ms: INTERVAL, ts_open: lastTsOpen - (count - 1 - i) * INTERVAL,
+      open, high: Math.round(high * 10) / 10, low: Math.round(low * 10) / 10, close, volume,
+      buy_volume: buy, sell_volume: Math.round((volume - buy) * 1000) / 1000,
+      trade_count: 40 + Math.floor(random() * 300), closed: true,
+    });
+    price = close;
+  }
+  return bars;

+const shiftPrint = (print, tsShift, priceShift, suffix = "") => ({
+  type: "trade", ...identity, ts_exchange: print.ts_exchange + tsShift, price: Math.round((print.price + priceShift) * 100) / 100,
+  size: print.size, side: print.side, trade_id: `${print.trade_id}${suffix}`,

+async function main() {
+  let closedBars;
+  let pool = null;
+  /** The minute being formed: its frame plus the prints still to be released, by offset inside the minute. */
+  let forming;
+  let pending = [];
+  let sent = [];
+  let cycle = 0;
+  let fixture = null;
+
+  if (withTape) {
+    fixture = await loadSizeSplitFixture();
+    const fixtureBars = fixture.bars.length;
+    // Fixture bar i sits at openTime - (fixtureBars - 1 - i) minutes: its last bar is the forming minute.
+    const tsShift = openTime - (fixtureBars - 1) * INTERVAL - fixture.bars[0].ts_open;
+    const prefixCount = Math.max(0, barCount - fixtureBars);
+    const prefix = syntheticBars(prefixCount, openTime - fixtureBars * INTERVAL, fixture.bars[0].open);
+    const priceFix = prefix.length > 0 ? fixture.bars[0].open - prefix[prefix.length - 1].close : 0;
+    for (const bar of prefix) { bar.open += priceFix; bar.high += priceFix; bar.low += priceFix; bar.close += priceFix; }
+    const history = fixture.bars.slice(0, -1).map((bar) => shiftBar(bar, tsShift, 0));
+    closedBars = [...prefix, ...history];
+    const pooledBars = poolBars === null ? POOL_PRINTS / fixture.printsByBar[0].length : poolBars;
+    const firstPoolBar = Math.max(0, fixtureBars - 1 - pooledBars);
+    const poolPrints = [];
+    for (let i = firstPoolBar; i < fixtureBars - 1; i += 1) for (const print of fixture.printsByBar[i]) poolPrints.push(shiftPrint(print, tsShift, 0));
+    if (pooledBars > 0) pool = { aggressive_prints: poolPrints.slice(-POOL_PRINTS), aggressive_prints_floor: 0, aggressive_prints_from: history[firstPoolBar].ts_open };
+    const last = fixture.bars[fixtureBars - 1];
+    forming = { type: "bar", ...identity, interval_ms: INTERVAL, ts_open: openTime, open: last.open, high: last.open, low: last.open, close: last.open, volume: 0, buy_volume: 0, sell_volume: 0, trade_count: 0, closed: false };
+    pending = fixture.printsByBar[fixtureBars - 1].map((print) => ({ offset: print.ts_exchange - last.ts_open, print: shiftPrint(print, tsShift, 0) }));
+  } else {
+    const bars = syntheticBars(barCount, openTime, 65_000);
+    // Every snapshot bar must be closed (the parser refuses a forming bar there), so the
+    // forming bar is a separate live `bar` frame for the current minute, revised every second.
+    closedBars = bars.slice(0, -1);
+    forming = { ...bars[bars.length - 1], ts_open: openTime, closed: false };
+  }
+
+  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/json");
+    res.end(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, fixture: true, tape: withTape, symbol: identity.symbol, bars: closedBars.length + 1 }));
+  });
+  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: "/ws" });
+  const send = (socket, frame) => { if (socket.readyState === 1) socket.send(JSON.stringify(frame)); };
+  const broadcast = (frame) => { for (const socket of wss.clients) send(socket, frame); };
+  const initialize = (socket) => {
+    send(socket, { type: "selection", ...identity });
+    send(socket, { type: "snapshot", ...identity, bars: closedBars, trades: sent, markers: [], ...(pool ?? {}) });
+    send(socket, { type: "health", ...identity, recoveryState: "READY", stale: false });
+    send(socket, { type: "book", ...identity, status: "READY", bids: [{ price: forming.close - 0.5, size: 3.2 }], asks: [{ price: forming.close + 0.5, size: 2.1 }], lastUpdateId: 1, stale: false });
+    send(socket, { ...forming });
+  };
+  wss.on("connection", (socket) => {
+    socket.on("message", (wire) => {
+      try { if (JSON.parse(wire.toString()).type === "select") initialize(socket); } catch { /* not JSON: ignore */ }
+    });
+    initialize(socket);
+  });
+
+  /** The minute whose candle volume is inflated so the share gate refuses it (1 = the first live minute). */
+  let minuteIndex = 1;
+  /** Fold one print into the forming bar's candle. */
+  const fold = (print) => {
+    if (forming.volume === 0) { forming.open = print.price; forming.high = print.price; forming.low = print.price; }
+    forming.close = print.price;
+    forming.high = Math.max(forming.high, print.price);
+    forming.low = Math.min(forming.low, print.price);
+    forming.volume += lowShareMinute === minuteIndex ? print.size * 2.6 : print.size;
+    if (print.side === "buy") forming.buy_volume += print.size; else forming.sell_volume += print.size;
+    forming.trade_count += 1;
+  };
+
+  /** After the fixture's last minute: the next minute replays fixture bars 200..299 with prices continuing from the last close. */
+  const nextMinute = () => {
+    const sourceIndex = fixture.bars.length - 100 + (cycle % 100);
+    cycle += 1;
+    const source = fixture.bars[sourceIndex];
+    const priceShift = Math.round((forming.close - source.open) * 100) / 100;
+    const tsShift = forming.ts_open + INTERVAL - source.ts_open;
+    forming = { type: "bar", ...identity, interval_ms: INTERVAL, ts_open: forming.ts_open + INTERVAL, open: forming.close, high: forming.close, low: forming.close, close: forming.close, volume: 0, buy_volume: 0, sell_volume: 0, trade_count: 0, closed: false };
+    pending = fixture.printsByBar[sourceIndex].map((print) => ({ offset: print.ts_exchange - source.ts_open, print: shiftPrint(print, tsShift, priceShift, `-c${cycle}`) }));
+    sent = [];
+    minuteIndex += 1;
+  };
+
+  setInterval(() => {
+    if (!withTape) {
+      forming.close = Math.round((forming.close + (random() - 0.5) * 12) * 10) / 10;
+      forming.high = Math.max(forming.high, forming.close);
+      forming.low = Math.min(forming.low, forming.close);
+      broadcast({ ...forming });
+      return;
+    }
+    const elapsed = Date.now() - forming.ts_open;
+    while (pending.length > 0 && pending[0].offset <= Math.min(elapsed, INTERVAL - 1)) {
+      const { print } = pending.shift();
+      fold(print);
+      sent.push(print);
+      broadcast(print);
+    }
+    if (elapsed >= INTERVAL) {
+      const closed = { ...forming, closed: true };
+      broadcast(closed);
+      closedBars.push(closed);
+      // A browser connecting later gets this minute's prints with the pool, as the runtime's pool would hold them.
+      if (pool !== null) pool.aggressive_prints = [...pool.aggressive_prints, ...sent].slice(-POOL_PRINTS);
+      else if (sent.length > 0) pool = { aggressive_prints: [...sent], aggressive_prints_floor: 0, aggressive_prints_from: forming.ts_open };
+      nextMinute();
+    }
+    broadcast({ ...forming });
+  }, 1000);
+
+  server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1", () => {
+    console.log(`fixture runtime on http://127.0.0.1:${port} (ws at /ws), ${closedBars.length + 1} synthetic 1m bars for ${identity.symbol}${withTape ? `, tape: ${pool ? `${pool.aggressive_prints.length} pooled prints from ${new Date(pool.aggressive_prints_from).toISOString()}` : "no pooled history"} plus live trade frames${lowShareMinute === null ? "" : `, low share on live minute ${lowShareMinute}`}` : ""}`);
+  });
+}
+
+main().catch((error) => { console.error(error); process.exit(1); });

`time gap`.

### 4. Warm-up

Method. Replay a fixture shorter than the warm-up length and one exactly at
it; read every line of `replayCapture(...).outputs`.

Pass. Every value before the documented warm-up length is `null`; the first
non-null value appears exactly at the documented index; no value anywhere is
`NaN`, `Infinity`, or a fabricated `0` standing in for "not yet" (a genuine
zero from complete data is fine, and the test must say which it is). The
warm-up length (bars, and for tick indicators the coverage condition) is stated
in the doc page and in a `staticHint` of the settings schema.

Evidence. Test named `warm-up is null, never NaN or zero`; the doc sentence.

### 5. Marker and alert budget

Method. `countMarkers(def, bars, settings, { live })` on a busy synthetic
instrument: `trendBars({ count: 1000 })` or the breakout fixture with
`busyTapeFor(bars, { printsPerBar: 60 })` supplying trades live (whales at 0.2
percent of prints, 100 times the small size). In cycle 1 an alert is a marker
(`PM/CHARTER.md`), so this is also the alert budget. Record `per1000Bars`,
`maxPerBar`, `barsWithMarkers` and the breakdown by shape and color.

The budget carries `faulted`; a run in which the runtime latched the indicator
describe("sizeSplitDeltaMath -- marker rule (rule 11, amendment 2, PM onset amendment)", () => {
  const settings = { K: 2, m: 2, q: 2 };
  const NONE = { conflict: false, divergence: null };
  const ring = [
    { close: 100, largeBar: 2, smallBar: -1, large: 2 },
    { close: 101, largeBar: 1, smallBar: -1, large: 3 },
  ];

  it("a strict new high confirms when large_t exceeds large_h, lags on equality, and arrows at depth m times the mean absolute contribution", () => {
    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: 0.5, smallBar: 0, large: 3.5 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("confirms");
    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: 0, smallBar: 0, large: 3 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("lags");
    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 0, smallBar: 0, large: 3 }, NONE, settings).state).toBe("noExtreme");
    // mabsL = 1.5, depth needed 3: large_t <= 0
    const shallow = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -2.5, smallBar: 0, large: 0.5 }, NONE, settings);
    expect(shallow.state).toBe("lags");
    expect(shallow.divergence).toBeNull();
    const bearish = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, NONE, settings);
    expect(bearish.divergence).toBe("bearish");
    expect(bearish.depth).toBe(-3);
    const bullish = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 99, largeBar: 3, smallBar: 0, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
    // l is the bar with close 100 (large 2); 6 >= 2 + 3
    expect(bullish.divergence).toBe("bullish");
    expect(bullish.state).toBe("lags");
    expect(bullish.depth).toBe(4);
    expect(evaluateMarkers(ring.slice(0, 1), { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, NONE, settings).state).toBeNull();
    // Onset only: the same condition held at the previous valid bar draws nothing but still reads as held.
    const continuing = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, { conflict: false, divergence: "bearish" }, settings);
    expect(continuing.divergenceHeld).toBe("bearish");
    expect(continuing.divergence).toBeNull();
    expect(continuing.depth).toBeNull();
    const flipped = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 102, largeBar: -3, smallBar: 0, large: 0 }, { conflict: false, divergence: "bullish" }, settings);
    expect(flipped.divergence).toBe("bearish");
  });

  it("the conflict holds when the two tier sums oppose at strength q·sqrt(K+1) and fires only at its onset", () => {
    // ring sums: large 3, small -2; mabsL 1.5, mabsS 1; strength 2*sqrt(3) = 3.464 -> need |sumL| >= 5.2, |sumS| >= 3.46
    const held = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: -2, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
    expect(held.conflictHeld).toBe(true);
    expect(held.conflictOnset).toBe(true);
    expect(held.conflictSums).toEqual([6, -4]);
    const continuing = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: -2, large: 6 }, { conflict: true, divergence: null }, settings);
    expect(continuing.conflictHeld).toBe(true);
    expect(continuing.conflictOnset).toBe(false);
    const sameSign = evaluateMarkers(ring, { close: 101, largeBar: 3, smallBar: 5, large: 6 }, NONE, settings);
    expect(sameSign.conflictHeld).toBe(false);
  });
});

Pass. Ceilings, unless the build brief sets a different one with a reason:
at most **40 markers per 1,000 bars** for a signal-type tool (a trader can act
on roughly one mark an hour on 1-minute bars), at most **200 per 1,000** for a
descriptive annotation tool (levels, labels), and at most **3 markers on one
bar**. A duplicated closed bar must not add markers (`countMarkersOver` with
`duplicateClosedArrivals`). The judged verdict is `judgeBudget(budget, ceiling,
maxPerBar).ok`.

   193	export function sizeSplitTableRows(
   194	  reading: SizeSplitReading | null,
   195	  settings: SizeSplitDeltaSettings,
   196	  state: Pick<SizeSplitDeltaState, "provider" | "symbol" | "assetClass">,
   197	): readonly SizeSplitTableRow[] {

   203	  ];

I also found that scans collect every size before trimming to 4,096, and retention shifts all 10,080 records on each new bar. Test execution was blocked before any tests ran because Vitest requires filesystem writes that this environment denies.
codex
1. **WARNING — Marker-budget failures pass the tests.**  
   [sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/size-split-delta/apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/tests/sizeSplitDeltaBaseline.test.ts:329), lines 329–339; also 167–178. The busy fixture reports 59 markers per 1,000 against the required ceiling of 40, but asserts that failing count instead of `verdict.ok`. The mechanism fixture checks the ceiling with conflict disabled, then omits it for the combined markers.  
   **Fix:** Assert `judgeBudget(..., 40, 2).ok` for both required configurations. Resolve failures through an approved specification change or implementation correction before acceptance.

2. **WARNING — The scan’s size snapshot exceeds its specified bound.**  
   [sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/size-split-delta/apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts:187), lines 187–201. `sizes.push()` collects every valid print before `splice()` retains 4,096. A busy window can therefore allocate an array approaching the entire 250,000-print store on every forming revision, exceeding the promised 32 KB snapshot and creating repeated allocation pressure.  
   **Fix:** Collect sizes in a fixed-capacity circular buffer during scanning while continuing to accumulate flow across every print. Test a window containing substantially more than 4,096 prints.

3. **WARNING — Retention shifts the entire record history per commit.**  
   [sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/size-split-delta/apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/sizeSplitDeltaMath.ts:500), lines 500–501. Once retention fills, removing index zero with `splice()` shifts approximately 10,080 references on every committed bar. This is an array with bounded length, but it violates the specified circular-record storage and prohibition on moving large arrays per bar.  
   **Fix:** Preallocate the record buffer and overwrite entries using a head index. Produce chronological views only when tests or diagnostics request them.

4. **WARNING — Unavailable and learning states always render three rows.**  
   [sizeSplitDelta.ts](/home/user/Terminal-L1-L2/worktrees/size-split-delta/apps/web/src/lib/indicators/size-split-delta/sizeSplitDelta.ts:193), lines 193–203. `sizeSplitTableRows()` unconditionally returns state, condition, and status rows, producing two unavailable placeholders during warm-up and REST operation. The brief explicitly requires one status row for these states.  
   **Fix:** Return only the status row during learning or unavailable operation, clear previously populated cells when transitioning, and pin those transitions in tests.

The PM’s UX rulings authorize onset-only divergence arrows. I found no additional defect in the threshold timing, close/supersession freeze, window cutoff, or coverage/share gates.

Test execution was attempted, but filesystem restrictions blocked Vitest’s temporary-file creation before any tests ran.

VERDICT: REVISE
